Result: 在该基准上成功率达74.58%，较最强提示优化基线高13.56个百分点，仅两次迭代即达52.54%；20位真实专业人士的双盲人类研究显示，本框架适配的提示在71%的成对比较中胜过静态基线。

Conclusion: 面向用户的提示适配是企业IE的有效建模范式，分层双循环的提示演化机制显著优于全局单一提示优化，并在模拟基准与真实人工评估中均得到验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [176] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出L0-MoE轻量级MoE方法，通过L0正则化加速稠密LLM，最高2.5倍加速且性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理慢、成本高；现有加速方法性能损失明显，而MoE模型训练需大量算力资源。因此需要一种既轻量又能保持性能的加速方案。

Method: 采用基于L0正则化的轻量级MoE结构加速稠密LLM；引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知的数据集构建，并使用动态批处理提升训练效率。

Result: 相比稠密模型最高实现2.5倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，优于现有LLM加速基线方法。

Conclusion: L0-MoE以低成本实现近乎无损的LLM推理加速，为高效部署稠密大模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [177] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出GUARD方法，将大推理模型的不安全披露转化为"自然遗忘轨迹"，即连贯的非披露思维链加稳定拒答，并引入NFRS指标衡量替代质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）的思维链（CoT）会在最终答案前暴露受保护事实或不安全推理，而现有遗忘目标只压制目标内容或重定向内部表征，未规定遗忘后的轨迹应如何延续，导致幻觉替代、边界错乱或重复输出。

Method: 提出Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation（GUARD）：将模型生成的不安全披露转化为安全退出轨迹，通过引导token对齐冻结的LRM，并将引导行为蒸馏进模型参数。

Result: 在R-TOFU及基于STAR-1构建的有害意图设定上，GUARD在两种主流蒸馏LRM上大幅减少不安全与隐私披露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRM遗忘应学习自然遗忘轨迹而非仅压制内容；GUARD结合NFRS指标可有效提升遗忘后输出的结构稳定性、流畅性并减少无依据替代。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [178] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReACT-TTS两阶段框架，利用听者一秒预反应面部序列规划对话下一句的情感和韵律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音依赖对话上下文与听者紧邻前序行为，现有系统多只利用文本，缺乏对听者即时反应动态的建模。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用一秒预反应听者面部序列进行时间条件建模，预测下一句的情感与韵律（VAD），并做对比实验与消融（时间建模 vs 视觉变体、早期-晚期差分、循环错配）；第二阶段将预测的响应风格接入Grad-TTS骨干实现端到端语音生成。

Result: 严格二元MELD协议下，十个随机种子中Temporal条件化在宏平均F1与VAD一致性上优于Text-only，准确率基本不变；消融显示时间建模在视觉变体中最佳，显式早期-晚期差分无必要，正确听者反应平均优于循环错配；20名语音研究者中76%偏好Temporal，9%偏好Text-only，15%无偏好。

Conclusion: 预反应听者动态可作为对话响应规划的补充线索，支持将听者即时行为纳入对话语音生成。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [179] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制分析与因果干预证明：TaxiGPT的行为失败并非缺乏世界模型，而是叠加的交叉口特征相互干扰导致内部地图定位受损；同合法动作的"可供性打包"可缓解错误，并提出机制指标以比较模型与刻画世界建模能力的分阶段涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"行为失败即缺乏世界模型"的常见推断：模型可能已学到忠实的内部环境表征，但行为错误被误读为内部地图不连贯。论文旨在区分"表征是否存在"与"表征能否被有效用于行为"，并推动从"是否有世界模型"的二元提问转向对世界建模机制的解析。

Method: 以在曼哈顿随机游走数据上训练的 transformer（TaxiGPT）为对象，开展机制分析与因果干预：识别交叉口、街道、位置跟踪与目标指北（goal compass）等内部表征与回路；定位失败来源为叠加（superposition）的交叉口特征之间的干扰；并引入"可供性打包"（affordance packing），将具有相同合法移动的交叉口表征分组。同时提出一组机制指标用于跨模型比较与训练阶段分析。

Result: 模型确实表征交叉口与街道、跟踪自身位置，并利用目标指北进行导航；其失败源于叠加交叉口特征间的干扰，破坏内部地图中的定位，而非缺少世界模型。可供性打包能限制这类错误的后果。机制指标显示不同模型的差异，且世界建模能力在训练的不同阶段依次涌现。

Conclusion: 应放弃"模型是否具有世界模型"的二元判断，转而机制性地研究"世界建模"：即模型表征环境并以此指导行为的多项相互作用能力。该视角可解释行为失败与内部表征之间的不对称，并为模型比较与训练动力学提供可操作的机制指标。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [180] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出" ECG Mirage（心电图海市蜃楼）"这一失效模式，指视觉-语言模型（VLM）在多模态急诊预测中看似具备能力，实则未有效利用患者特异性 ECG 信息；作者通过匹配/错配/无图像三条件对比验证其存在，并用受限视觉提示微调加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊决策依赖病史、生命体征、检验与心电图等多源异构信息，VLM 可联合处理这些模态，但预测性能强并不意味着模型真正使用了正确患者的 ECG。作者希望揭示并量化这种"表面多模态能力"的潜在失效，即 ECG Mirage。

Method: 研究将 ECG Mirage 分为两种形式：ECG neglect（ECG 几乎不带来预测增益）与 ECG confusion（匹配 ECG 优于无图像输入，但不优于错配 ECG）。实验在固定临床文本与预测目标的前提下，对比匹配 ECG、与结局不一致的错配 ECG、无图像三种输入下四个 VLM 在 MDS-ED 上的预测。随后在冻结 VLM 主干的情况下，先用监督学习训练四个受限视觉提示，再进行条件直接偏好优化（conditional DPO）。

Result: 在 MDS-ED 上，匹配 ECG 对 ICU 入住与临床恶化预测均无一致优势，表明存在 ECG Mirage。经视觉提示微调后，模型在 ICU 入住预测上达到 70.6% 平衡准确率，在恶化预测上达到 67.5%，匹配与错配之间的性能差距分别扩大至约 16.5 和 5.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究识别了多模态临床预测中的 ECG Mirage 现象，并证明视觉提示微调是一种高效、低成本的缓解策略，可在不更新 VLM 主干的情况下增强模型对患者特异性 ECG 信息的依赖。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [181] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单的统计式单变量时间序列异常检测流水线，通过滑动窗口统计特征、MAD稳健评分与按域特征选择，在TSB-AD-U上取得0.529 VUS-PR，并发现性能上限主要来自候选统计池；进而用多模态LLM生成候选池并与手工池取并集，提升至0.588。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在严格选择协议下，简单统计流水线能在单变量时间序列异常检测上达到何种水平，并探究性能瓶颈究竟来自特征池还是选择/聚合策略。

Method: 在滑动窗口上提取小型统计特征池，用直推式稳健（MAD）模型对窗口打分，并在留出调优集上按域选择特征子集；随后通过消融（三种选择策略、事后oracle、聚合网格、候选池变化）定位性能上限来源，并用多模态LLM结合该域的上下文示例窗口生成每域候选池，与手工池在匹配选择下比较并取并集。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到每序列VUS-PR 0.529，高于公开榜单最佳神经（0.45）与统计（0.44）条目，距最强预训练基础模型差0.06；消融显示选择策略仅带来0.031变化、聚合网格0.096、候选池0.226；LLM生成池与手工池在匹配选择下相当且覆盖不同域，十二个生成器-种子对中并集均优于生成池，流水线提升至0.588，与榜单最佳条目持平。

Conclusion: 候选统计池决定性能上限，而对池的搜索仅为二阶因素；多模态LLM生成的域特定候选池可与手工池互补，二者并集在匹配选择下稳定提升性能，使简单统计流水线达到与榜单最佳条目相当的水平。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [182] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIST模型，用基因组特征作为查询token去检索紧凑的病理基础模型上下文token，实现基因组引导的病理注意力融合，在四个外部队列上提升生存预测C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型可融合全切片图像（WSI）与基因组谱的互补预后信息，但面临外部队列分布漂移（cohort shift）与计算复杂度高的双重挑战，且传统的晚期融合难以充分交互模态信息。

Method: MIST将基因组特征表示为token，在生存预测前以其为query去检索由基础模型导出的紧凑病理上下文token，从而在早期用病理上下文丰富分子信息，而非仅在末端合并各自独立编码的模态。训练目标结合离散时间生存预测损失、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout，以及配对的WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾脏、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个队列的外部评估中，MIST在主要对比上相较标准融合基线提升了外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的病理注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤结局预测策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [183] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EnterpriseVal——面向企业用例的评估系统，弥补公开基准与部署决策之间的测量鸿沟，并在某全球银行三项工作流试点中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型已能产出专家认可的专业交付物，但多数企业生成式AI项目未显现可测量业务效果，大量智能体项目预期被取消。作者认为这本质是测量问题：公开基准回答"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需要回答"该工作流在我们数据与控制下是否适用、可靠、安全且值得扩展"。

Method: EnterpriseVal 由五部分构成：(i) 用例与社会技术配置（模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏、人工监督）的形式化规范，并以自主性等级与后果层级共同确定评估强度；(ii) 涵盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保证与监督的指标目录；(iii) 结合盲评专家判断与经校准的 LLM-as-judge 评分、通过预测驱动推断（PPI）实现的评级协议；(iv) 以可执行算法表述的两级阈值闸门，将带置信界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 决策；(v) 将评审者捕获率作为可测参数的价值—风险模型。

Result: 在某全球银行三项工作流开展试点：信贷备忘录起草中，最优模型的人工评阅引用精确率达88%、幻觉率1.6%，对应闸门为70%与5%；流程改造中，分析师精修工作量由估计的每文档27.4小时降至2.9小时。

Conclusion: 论文明确区分已确立结果、试点证据、所提系统与开放假设，并给出全面验证所需的实验设计。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [184] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRecLab：基于自然语言提示自动完成推荐系统实验的自主实验室，通过RAG、静态类型校验与执行引导的树搜索实现从需求到可执行代码的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统实证评估至关重要，但将实验设计转化为可执行代码仍是人工且易错的过程，亟需自动化手段降低实现成本与出错率。

Method: 提出基于Python的自主推荐系统实验室AutoRecLab：从自然语言提示中推导显式实验需求，构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为完整实验；工作流融合RAG文档检索、静态类型验证与执行引导的树搜索。

Result: 自主实现了显式到隐式反馈转换研究；在六种算法、三个数据集的基线对比中，9次运行有8次成功，单次平均成本约1美元（使用GPT-5.4-mini）。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab证明了大语言模型驱动的自主代理能够以较低成本端到端自动化推荐系统实验流程，具备可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [185] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAVEL：面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，用"问题-回答-检索"全回路的名次反馈来优化提问策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式检索在部分证据下是一个序贯信息获取问题：智能体需判断哪个问题能为下一轮检索更新带来最有用证据。现有系统通过模仿离线候选QA对的排序来训练该决策，但问题的价值实际由其所诱发的回答及其对检索的下游影响决定；论文先证实在线目标上"候选区分度"与"主观有用性"仅构成弱监督，因而需要直接以检索效果为奖励信号。

Method: RAVEL：先以监督式问题生成进行初始化；随后直接观测当前Top-4候选，并用完整"问题-回答-检索"回路产生的名次反馈，通过在线强化学习优化提问策略。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES上，RAVEL在五个交互轮次中持续带来更强的检索性能；分析显示其将提问预算重新分配到局部化、开放式属性上，这类问题提供更有效的检索证据，并在初始困难的查询上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 问题的价值应由其对检索的下游影响衡量，而非离线排序或启发式指标；在线RL结合全回路名次反馈优于监督式模仿，且提问资源配置（偏好局部化开放式属性）是性能增益的关键来源。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [186] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem 将长期对话记忆在写入时按自配置、低重叠的语义视图进行结构化抽取，使标准 top-K 检索即可获得聚焦证据，在 LoCoMo 与 PersonaMem 上提升长程问答与个性化并保持简单推理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需长期记忆以在长交互中保持一致性与个性化，但现有系统依赖固定粒度或静态 schema，难以处理偏好、事件、约束、时间更新等异质信息混合于同一表示的问题；语义干扰使 top-K 检索对噪声敏感，相关证据排序偏低。

Method: AutoViewMem 是数据驱动框架：从交互轨迹中发现候选语义视图，选取紧凑且互补的视图集合，并据此在写入阶段进行带来源依据（provenance-grounded）的结构化记忆抽取；把语义解耦从检索时前移到写入时，从而无需显式路由或迭代检索即可用标准 top-K 相似度搜索召回聚焦证据；另加离线整合以提升记忆紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 PersonaMem 基准、Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-14B 两种骨干下，相较强记忆基线，AutoViewMem 提升了长程问答与个性化表现，同时保持简单的推理管线。

Conclusion: 以“表示优先”为核心的写入时语义解耦与视图引导结构化抽取，是提升 LLM 智能体长期记忆检索质量的有效途径，且不增加推理复杂度。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [187] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对运动应激与跨会话变异下的心电生物识别鲁棒性问题，提出并评估了一种基于孪生ResNet与晚期多导联融合的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电（ECG）蕴含个体特异性模式，可用于身份识别，并在保护敏感心脏数据及自监督学习预训练任务方面具有潜力。然而，现有研究多局限于单会话静息数据，对时间漂移与生理变化下的鲁棒性缺乏充分验证。作者旨在填补这一空白，在包含运动诱发应激与跨会话变异的真实条件下评估ECG生物识别。

Method: 构建基于心肺运动试验的大型ECG数据集，采用孪生ResNet（Siamese ResNet）并结合晚期多导联融合策略进行训练；评估时采用运动感知与时间感知（exercise-and time-aware）协议，并在公开基准数据集上进行对照评测。

Result: 在生理与时序双重变异条件下，同会话静息到峰值（rest-to-peak）EER达1.7%；在CYBHi数据集上取得3.9%的当前最优（state-of-the-art）结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，存在一种对生理与时序漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征（intrinsic cardiac signature），为ECG生物识别在真实动态条件下的可行性提供了支持。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [188] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR)，借鉴法医学隐藏信息测试，通过向模型呈现问题及候选答案并探测其内部状态，判断模型"识别"出的正确选项，从而区分模型是"隐藏答案"还是"确实不知"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能掌握某些知识却不报告。模型可能在能力评估中故意放水，或给出与内部认知相反的答案，仅凭输出无法区分它是隐藏答案还是真的不知道。现有的诚实参考模型或标注真值语料依赖限制了检测手段。

Method: 借鉴法医学隐藏信息测试（CIT）：向嫌犯呈现真实细节与干扰项并测量其对识别项的更强反应。PIR在模型内部做同样的事：给定问题及候选答案，从模型内部状态读取其识别为正确的候选。PIR无需参考模型，也无需标注真值语料（reference-free）。

Result: 在来自5个家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）的8个模型上，PIR以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复识别答案，远高于0.28–0.40的未知项基线和0.25的随机水平。在各种隐藏形式下（提示诱导欺骗、训练放水、外部密码锁定及电路破坏检查点）识别率保持在0.85–0.93。模型隐藏已知答案时识别率高；遗忘使知识移除时识别率降至从未知晓问题的水平。

Conclusion: PIR能分离"不愿回答"与"无法回答"的模型，支持放水审计和遗忘验证。该信号具有因果性，提供超越黑箱行为线索的信息，并从多选题扩展到自由形式生成。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [189] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeMidas，一条仅以源代码为任务特定输入、将已有代码库中的功能转化为可执行 RL 环境的智能体流水线，构建了覆盖 23 种语言、15 个技术领域的 5,545 个训练任务，并用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5，在五个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练具备编程能力的智能体需要多样化且有可靠验证器的任务。开源代码库是这类任务的丰富来源，但现有方法通常依赖 issue、commit 等开发副产物，限制了可提取任务的范围，难以规模化构建 RL 环境。

Method: CodeMidas 是一条智能体流水线，仅以源代码作为任务特定输入，将代码库中已实现的功能转化为可执行 RL 环境。它为环境构建的每个阶段分配智能体算力：智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规格，基于原始代码的实际执行构建测试，并通过执行检查与重复解答采样的 rollout 来验证和筛选候选任务。最终用 GRPO 在生成任务上训练 MiMo-V2.5。

Result: 数据集包含来自 3,185 个开源代码库的 5,545 个训练任务，覆盖 23 种编程语言和 15 个技术领域。使用 GRPO 训练后在五个基准上均取得提升：issue 修复（DeepSWE +11.7%）、整程序构建（ProgramBench +17%）、终端任务（Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%）。消融实验显示高质量训练任务数量增加能提升性能；轨迹分析显示 RL 训练后的智能体表现出更多代码库探索和更丰富的自验证行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，源代码可作为构建 RL 环境的可扩展基础，能在多样化软件任务上提升编程智能体的能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [190] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种持续自适应框架：冻结的前沿模型通过230+工具操作专业设计软件，外部"程序性记忆"以自然语言技能形式从经验中积累并精炼可复用设计流程；记忆通过"拓宽"（获取新子任务流程）与"加深"（依据成功/失败执行修订已有流程）扩展，并以配对回放门控只接受能修复失败且不回退成功的变更。在1460份真实用户简报、5轮、无权重更新与人工标注下，技能库由76增至139，GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%升至99.3%，并在四个设计基准上取得对无技能智能体的胜率，两机制结合效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务：结构化可编辑产物的形成依赖大量相互依赖的动作，且结果缺乏可靠的程序化评判标准（无oracle），因此难以用传统监督或强化学习方式进行持续改进。作者希望探索在反馈嘈杂、不可验证的条件下，让智能体实现持续自适应的一条实用路径。

Method: 框架保持前沿模型权重冻结，使其通过逾230个工具操作专业设计软件；同时维护一个外部程序性记忆，以自然语言技能（可复用设计流程）的形式存储。记忆扩展含两种机制：拓宽——为反复出现但尚未覆盖的子任务获取新流程；加深——根据流程自身的成功与失败执行记录对其修订。引入配对回放门控（matched replay gate），仅接纳能修复失败且不使已观察成功出现回退的变更。整个过程不更新模型权重、不使用人工标签，在1460份真实用户简报上运行5轮，共1869条自动评分轨迹。

Result: 技能库由76个源自文档的技能增至139个。在Claude-Sonnet-4上，GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%提升至99.3%（生成质量+11.99分）；在四个专业设计基准上，对无技能智能体的胜率分别为61.8%（Claude-Sonnet-4）与67.6%（Claude-Opus-4.6）。在用户流量基准的200份留出简报上，单独拓宽或单独加深的胜率分别为49.4%与48.6%，二者结合达58.5%（p = 0.025），表明两机制组合有效。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在嘈杂且不可验证的反馈环境下实现持续自适应提供了切实可行的途径；拓宽与加深两种机制互补，需结合使用方能取得最佳效果。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [The Right Tool for the Job: On the Selection of Mitigations for GenAI Privacy Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20884)
*Jonah Bellemans,Qianying Liao,Laurens Sion,Lieven Desmet,Wouter Joosen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张生成式人工智能（GenAI）隐私工程的核心挑战不在于缺乏威胁或缓解知识，而在于二者之间缺失的"桥梁"，并提出三项子问题、四条建议及一套研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI已从实验性技术演变为现代软件系统的基础组件，但其广泛应用使敏感个人数据保护愈发困难。GenAI不仅放大传统隐私威胁，还引入基于推断的新风险（如从看似无害的输入中构建详细用户画像）。虽然隐私威胁建模框架开始更细粒度地刻画GenAI特有威胁，缓解技术也不断涌现，但问题空间与解空间基本各自独立发展，形成知识割裂。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，进行问题分析与概念性论证。作者将"威胁—缓解"脱节拆解为三个子问题，并据此推导建议与方案。

Result: 将核心鸿沟分解为三项子问题：(i) 缺乏针对GenAI系统的细粒度"威胁到缓解"映射；(ii) 解决方案空间假设在GenAI情境下不适用；(iii) 在GenAI约束条件下的优先级排序困难。基于此提出四条面向未来缓解选择方法的建议，并勾勒出一种扩展既有威胁到缓解映射方法、以适配GenAI特有威胁特征的思路。

Conclusion: 论文呼吁转向更系统化的GenAI隐私缓解选择，提出相应研究议程，以弥合威胁与缓解之间的知识断层。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has rapidly evolved from an experimental technology into a foundational component of modern software systems. However, as its adoption grows, protecting sensitive personal data becomes increasingly challenging. Specifically, GenAI systems not only amplify traditional privacy threats but also introduce new inference-based risks, such as constructing detailed user profiles from seemingly harmless inputs. In response, privacy threat modeling frameworks are beginning to capture GenAI-specific privacy threats with finer granularity. At the same time, a growing number of mitigation techniques have been proposed to address these threats. However, although knowledge of both threats and mitigations continues to mature, the problem- and solution-space have developed largely independently.
  This position paper argues that the primary challenge in GenAI privacy engineering is not the lack of knowledge about privacy threats or mitigation techniques, but the missing bridge between them. We decompose this gap into three sub-problems: (i) lack of fine-grained threat-to-mitigation mapping for GenAI systems, (ii) inapplicable solution-space assumptions in the GenAI context, and (iii) prioritization difficulty under GenAI constraints. We derive four recommendations for future mitigation-selection approaches, and outline a suggested approach that extends established threat-to-mitigation mapping methods to GenAI-specific threat characteristics. We propose a research agenda toward more systematic privacy mitigation selection for GenAI-based systems.

</details>


### [192] [TPM-Attest: Hardware-Rooted Integrity Attestation as a Kernel-Level Anti-Cheat Alternative for Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20909)
*Anudeep Gedela,A. Yaswanth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 TPM-Attest，一个基于 TPM 2.0 与 Linux IMA 的硬件根信任远程证明框架，完全取代专有 Ring 0 反作弊内核模块，实现无内核驱动、无专有代码的多人游戏客户端完整性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux 平台多人 PC 游戏面临结构性矛盾：发行商要求的反作弊系统以专有 Ring 0 内核模块形式运行，与 Linux 的安全模型、GPL 许可及稳定 ABI 保证在架构上不兼容。作者认为正确路径不是将这些侵入式模块移植到 Linux，而是彻底替代它们。

Method: 通过用户态 LD_PRELOAD 钩子拦截 Epic Online Services（EOS）SDK 调用；以服务器下发的 nonce 绑定的实时 TPM quote 作为会话访问的门控条件；在 IMA 日志上构建带索引前缀的 Merkle 树，以抵御重复叶子碰撞攻击；并使用增量叶子缓存优化重复证明的延迟。

Result: 在 500 个构造的篡改会话中实现 100% 检测率；在真实 TPM 2.0 硬件上，增量叶子缓存将重复证明延迟降至 3 秒以内；针对真实演示游戏的受控红队评估确认所有四类基于文件的攻击向量均被阻断，并精确刻画了两个已确认的绕过条件。

Conclusion: TPM-Attest 证明可用硬件根信任的远程证明替代专有反作弊内核模块，在不扫描玩家内存、不引入专有代码的前提下提供可验证的客户端完整性保证，且完整实现已以开源软件形式发布。

Abstract: Multiplayer PC gaming on Linux faces a structural problem: the anti-cheat systems that publishers require operate as proprietary Ring 0 kernel modules that are architecturally incompatible with Linux's security model, GPL licensing, and stable ABI guarantees. We argue the right response is not to port these invasive modules to Linux, but to replace them entirely. This paper presents TPM-Attest, a hardware-rooted remote attestation framework that uses the Trusted Platform Module (TPM) 2.0 and the Linux Integrity Measurement Architecture (IMA) to prove, cryptographically, that a client booted cleanly and ran only authorised software -- without any kernel driver, without proprietary code, and without scanning player memory. The system intercepts Epic Online Services (EOS) SDK calls via a userspace LD_PRELOAD hook, gates session access on a live TPM quote bound to a server-issued nonce, and constructs an index-prefixed Merkle tree over the IMA log that is immune to duplicate-leaf collision attacks. Across 500 constructed tamper sessions we achieve a 100% detection rate; incremental leaf caching reduces repeat-attestation latency to under 3 seconds on real TPM 2.0 hardware. A controlled red-team evaluation against a live demo game confirms all four file-backed attack vectors are blocked while precisely characterising the two confirmed bypass conditions. The full implementation is released as open-source software.

</details>


### [193] [(Don't) Trust, but (Don't) Verify: Developers' Attention to Security in AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21020)
*Hamza Khalid,Ronald E. Thompson,Alejandra Sabater,Perucy Mussiba,Kelsey R. Fulton,Daniel Votipka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过远程观察实验（100人）与访谈，聚焦AI辅助开发中的"代码评估"环节，考察开发者能否识别AI生成代码的漏洞、依赖何种线索，以及信任如何影响其选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手正快速改变软件开发，但已知会生成不安全代码。既有工作多测量AI辅助开发者产出的代码是否安全，却较少关注开发者如何评估AI生成的代码：能否识别漏洞、使用哪些线索、信任如何影响决策。而这一评估环节是使用AI（自动补全、对话工具或智能体）进行安全开发的基础。

Method: 作为第一步，开展远程观察研究，100名参与者，隔离"评估"这一阶段。任务：为四个C语言链表任务编写安全且功能正确的代码。每个任务中，参与者可循环浏览五个在安全性与功能性上各不相同的AI生成建议，选择一个并编辑为最终提交。参与者还完成了一份关于决策过程及对AI生成代码安全性认知的后测问卷，其中23人完成了更深入的访谈。

Result: 摘要未报告量化结果，仅说明研究设计与数据收集（100名参与者、四个C链表任务、每个任务5个建议、问卷及23份深度访谈）。

Conclusion: 该工作将AI辅助安全开发的研究焦点从"产出"转向"评估"，为理解开发者识别与处置AI代码漏洞的机制提供了实验与访谈证据基础。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are rapidly transforming software development, but are known to produce insecure code. Prior work has measured whether AI-assisted developers produce secure code, but less is known about how they evaluate AI-generated code: whether they can identify vulnerabilities, what cues they use, and how trust shapes their decisions. This evaluation step is foundational to secure development with AI, whether using auto-complete, chat tools, or AI agents. As a first step, we conducted a remote observational study with 100 participants isolating this evaluation stage. Participants were tasked with producing secure and functional code for four C linked-list tasks. For each, participants were able to cycle through five AI-generated suggestions varying in security and functionality, select one, and edit their choice into a final submission. Participants also completed a post-study survey about their decision-making and perception of AI-generated code's security and 23 completed a more in-depth interview.

</details>


### [194] [Loopjacking: Hijacking Human-in-the-Loop Approval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21081)
*Adithyan Arun Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了一类新型安全漏洞"Loopjacking"：人类批准的操作A与系统实际执行的操作B之间发生绑定失效，分为"表征型攻击"和"批准后状态替换攻击"两种变体，并在多款智能体产品中成功复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类批准通常被视为智能体执行重大操作前的最后安全边界，但该边界只有在"被审查的操作"与"被授权/执行的操作"完全一致时才有意义。作者关注批准决策与最终执行之间绑定失效这一被忽视的安全风险，并将其定义为Loopjacking。

Method: 作者提出Loopjacking概念并区分两种变体：（1）表征型攻击——操作B已被编码，但在批准环节被省略或虚假呈现；（2）批准后状态替换攻击——人类看到正确的操作A，但可变的workflow状态随后将其替换为B。随后对一组已发布的智能体产品进行目的性抽样评估。

Result: 在Agno AgentOS（至3.0.9）的7个测试版本和条件式内存LangGraph Agent Server组合（至0.14.0）的12个测试版本中复现了批准后状态替换攻击；在OpenClaw 2026.2.23中复现了表征不匹配，并在2026.2.24中复现了其被拒绝；OpenAI Agents SDK 0.22.0与0.22.2作为阴性对照，其序列化续延保持了精确的逐调用绑定并能拒绝被篡改的B。作者强调这些结果不用于估计生态系统的普遍流行率。

Conclusion: 完整的规范化批准渲染与精确的使用时比较，或阻止未经授权的待定状态变更，能够阻断所测试的攻击，同时保留合法执行。作者将本贡献与既有的误导性对话、会话走私、动作绑定和授权连续性研究相区分。

Abstract: Human approval is often treated as the last security boundary before an agent executes a consequential operation. That boundary is only meaningful if the operation presented for review is the operation later authorized or released. We call failures of this binding Loopjacking: a human approves what they understand as operation A, while the implementation uses that decision for a materially different operation B. We distinguish two variants. In a representation-based attack, B is already encoded but omitted or misrepresented at approval time; in a post-approval state-substitution attack, the human sees the correct A and mutable workflow state later replaces it with B.
  We evaluate a purposive set of released agent products. We reproduce post-approval substitution in seven tested Agno AgentOS releases ending at 3.0.9 and in 12 tested versions of a conditional in-memory LangGraph Agent Server composition ending at 0.14.0. We reproduce representation mismatch in OpenClaw 2026.2.23 and its rejection in 2026.2.24. OpenAI Agents SDK 0.22.0 and 0.22.2 provide a negative control: serialized continuation preserves exact per-call binding and rejects mutated B. These results do not estimate ecosystem prevalence. They show that complete canonical approval rendering and exact use-time comparison, or preventing unauthorized pending-state mutation, block the tested attacks while preserving legitimate execution. We separate this contribution from established work on misleading dialogs, session smuggling, action binding, and authorization continuity.

</details>


### [195] [Origin Is All You Need: Provenance-Aware Transformers for Structural Trust-Boundary Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21088)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Jeff Huang,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出溯源感知Transformer（Provenance-Aware Transformers），将来源标签作为模型内部一等的架构信号，从结构上隔离权威与非权威信息源，从而防御间接提示注入（IPI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer缺乏来源权威性的架构概念，检索文档、用户输入与系统指令经由同一无差别的注意力机制处理，模型只能靠措辞推断应服从谁、应把谁当作数据，导致间接提示注入（IPI）成为LLM系统的核心安全挑战。

Method: 为每个输入token分配编码其来源的ring ID；在模型中引入来源嵌入（origin embeddings）、可学习的来源注意力偏置（origin attention bias）以及在归一化下保持溯源信息的可学习来源缩放（origin scale），在生成过程中强制建立权威与非权威来源之间的结构性边界；并设计两阶段微调流程，在已发布的预训练模型上教会模型来源语义与ring约束下的任务行为。

Result: 在分布内与分布外场景下均保持对IPI的稳健抵御能力，同时效用与基座预训练模型相当。

Conclusion: 将溯源信息作为一等的架构信号暴露给模型，可推动LLM安全对齐从脆弱的模式匹配转向显式的信任分离。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection (IPI) remains a central safety and security challenge for large language model (LLM) systems because standard transformers lack architectural notion of source authority. Retrieved documents, user inputs, and system instructions are all processed through the same undifferentiated attention mechanism, forcing the model to infer from wording alone what should be obeyed and what should be treated as data. We propose Provenance-Aware Transformers, a provenance-aware defense that makes application-supplied source labels actionable inside the model. Each input token is assigned a ring ID encoding its origin, and the model is augmented with origin embeddings, a learnable origin attention bias, and a learnable origin scale that preserves provenance under normalization. The resulting architecture enforces a structural boundary between authoritative and non-authoritative sources during generation. To instantiate this architecture on released pretrained models, we propose a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline to teach the model origin semantics and task behavior under ring constraints. Evaluation shows that Provenance-Aware Transformers maintain robust resistance to IPI both in-distribution and out-of-distribution while preserving utility comparable to the base pretrained model. More broadly, our work shows that exposing provenance as a first-class architectural signal can shift LLM safety alignment from brittle pattern matching toward explicit trust separation.

</details>


### [196] [NetInspector: Measuring and Improving LLM Capabilities for Reliable Intent-Based Networking Policy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21103)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Hongxin Hu,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在意图驱动网络(IBN)策略生成中的可靠性与安全问题：微调LLM擅长意图翻译，但在安全策略违规检测上存在高假阴性率，根源在于缺乏对网络拓扑与群组层级的持续性"接地"。为此提出NetInspector三层代理框架，通过verify-then-act协议将信息检索与推理解耦，使策略决策基于来自实时环境层的可验证网络事实。在NetInspector-Bench（2,224个样本，覆盖园区、企业与WAN拓扑）上，相比无接地基线FNR降低超30%，并在语言分布偏移下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络规模大、配置异构，人工策略管理难以为继；IBN通过自动化高层意图到低层配置的转换来应对，但现有系统依赖静态启发式与固定特征分类器，对分布偏移（如新服务定义、请求措辞演化）泛化能力差。LLM具备强推理与翻译能力，是IBN策略生成的自然候选，但其可否可靠应用、以及是否会缓解或加剧安全风险尚不明确。

Method: 首先实证分析微调LLM在意图翻译与安全策略违规检测上的表现，发现其假阴性问题源于缺乏对网络拓扑和群组层级的持续性接地（而非逻辑推理能力不足）。据此提出NetInspector三层代理式框架，执行verify-then-act协议：将信息检索与推理解耦，LLM专注于符号推理，每个策略决策在批准前均以从实时Environment Layer检索到的可验证网络事实为依据。同时构建NetInspector-Bench合成基准，含2,224个样本，覆盖园区、企业与WAN拓扑。

Result: 微调LLM在意图翻译任务上表现优异，但在检查提案意图是否违反既有安全策略时存在假阴性率（FNR）偏高问题。在NetInspector-Bench上，NetInspector相较无接地（ungrounded）基线将FNR降低超过30%，并在语言分布偏移下仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: LLM用于IBN策略生成时，可靠性与安全性瓶颈在于网络事实接地而非推理能力；通过verify-then-act与检索-推理解耦的代理框架引入可验证网络事实，可显著降低安全策略违规漏检率并提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性，为LLM在IBN中的可靠部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern networks are large in scale and heterogeneous in configuration, making manual policy management increasingly impractical. Intent-Based Networking (IBN) addresses this by automating the translation of high-level operator goals into low-level network configurations. Yet existing IBN systems rely on static heuristics and fixed-feature classifiers that generalize poorly to distribution shifts such as new service definitions or evolving phrasing in operator requests. Large Language Models (LLMs), with strong reasoning and translation capabilities demonstrated across many domains, are a natural candidate for IBN policy generation. However, it is unclear whether LLMs can be reliably applied to this task, nor whether their use mitigates or worsens the underlying security risk.
  In this work, we show that while fine-tuned LLMs excel at intent translation, they exhibit false negative rates when checking whether a proposed intent violates an existing security policy. The root cause is not a lack of logical reasoning capability, but LLMs lack of persistent grounding in network topology and group hierarchy. Motivated by this finding, we introduce NetInspector, a three-layer agentic framework that enforces a verify-then-act protocol, decoupling information retrieval from reasoning so that the LLM focuses on symbolic reasoning while every policy decision is grounded in verifiable network facts retrieved from a live Environment Layer before approval. On NetInspector-Bench, a 2,224-sample synthetic benchmark spanning campus, enterprise, and WAN topologies, NetInspector reduces FNR by over 30\% relative to ungrounded baselines and remains robust under linguistic distribution shifts.

</details>


### [197] [Toss If Perishable: An Ethnographic Study on Building Scenario-Based Training for Non-Perishable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21147)
*Francis Hahn,Spencer Cherry,Kumar Shashwat,Laura Araujo Buldrini,Daniel Lende,Xinming Ou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"非易逝知识"概念并设计场景驱动训练法，通过基于真实事件的工具无关调查场景，结合20小时、25名学员的人种志研究，识别促进与抑制调查思维技能学习的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC通常依赖针对特定工具与流程的在岗培训，工具复杂性会掩盖底层推理过程，阻碍分析员学习调查技能。作者希望将独立于工具的调查思维能力（即"非易逝知识"）分离出来并加以训练。

Method: 提出"非易逝知识"概念，开发场景驱动训练法；基于两起真实事件设计两个工具无关的调查场景；招募大学生作为被试开展人种志研究；收集五个场次、25名学员共20小时训练数据，并采用扎根理论进行分析。

Result: 通过扎根理论分析揭示出抑制或促进调查思维技能学习的若干因素，并展示了被试对该训练方法的接受程度及其在任务中的表现。

Conclusion: 研究结合场景训练、人种志研究与技术分析，为培养业界视为SOC核心的推理技能提供了可行路径与实证依据。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) often rely upon on-the-job training focusing on the specific tools and procedures a SOC utilizes. The complexity in tooling can overshadow the underlying reasoning process, hindering an analyst's ability to learn investigative skills. We formulate the concept of "non-perishable knowledge" which corresponds to investigative thinking skills independent of tools. We developed a scenario-driven training method to understand whether such non-perishable knowledge can be imparted through specially designed incident scenarios, where trainees are presented with and solve investigative challenges in a tool-agnostic manner. We designed two such scenarios based on real-world incidents. Human subjects were recruited from a university's student body for ethnographic study to understand how this training method is received by the subjects and how they perform on such tasks. We collected data from 20 hours of documented training with 25 trainees spread across five sessions. Using grounded-theory, we analyzed the data and uncovered factors that inhibit or promote learning of the investigative thinking skills. Our research combines scenario-based training, ethnographic research, and technical analysis to examine how to best train students in the reasoning skills that industry deems vital to SOCs.

</details>


### [198] [X-SPUR: Explainable Surprisal-Based Protocol-Aware Unsupervised Reasoning for Automotive Ethernet Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21217)
*Jisoo Kim,Seonghoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出可解释、基于惊异度(surprisal)且协议感知的无监督车载以太网入侵检测框架X-SPUR，以原始报文token序列替代人工特征，在TOW-IDS上AUC达0.9987。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载以太网承载异构多协议流量，标注攻击数据稀缺；现有最强无监督检测器仍依赖人工设计的流量特征，泛化性与可解释性受限。

Method: 将原始报文字段表示为token序列，通过因果语言建模学习良性流量模式，以逐token交叉熵惊异度作为异常分数；引入双模态融合架构，将载荷token嵌入与包间时序通过加性融合和Hadamard交互结合；针对不同协议族得分分布异构，提出双重top-k%的分协议Z-score校准，同时捕捉中等分布与稀疏异常特征。

Result: 在TOW-IDS数据集上AUC为0.9987，略高于AERO的0.9969，并免除人工特征工程；用相同架构与训练超参训练的CarDS模型在第二个车载以太网数据集上仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: X-SPUR以无监督、协议感知方式实现高精度检测，并通过逐token惊异度将异常分数归因到具体协议字段，为异构车载网络的细粒度可解释安全分析提供支持。

Abstract: Automotive Ethernet carries heterogeneous multi-protocol traffic in modern in-vehicle networks, where labeled attack data are rarely available and the strongest prior unsupervised detector still relies on handcrafted traffic features. This article presents X-SPUR, an explainable, surprisal-based, protocol-aware unsupervised reasoning framework that instead represents raw packet fields as token sequences, learns benign traffic patterns through causal language modeling, and detects anomalies from per-token cross-entropy surprisal. To incorporate temporal context, we introduce a bimodal fusion architecture that combines payload-token embeddings with inter-packet timing through additive fusion and a Hadamard interaction. To handle the heterogeneous score distributions of different protocol families, we further propose a dual top-$k$% per-protocol $Z$-score calibration that jointly captures moderately distributed and sparse anomaly signatures. On the TOW-IDS dataset, X-SPUR achieves an AUC of 0.9987. This is marginally higher than the 0.9969 reported for AERO. X-SPUR also eliminates handcrafted feature engineering. We train a separate CarDS model using the same architecture and training hyperparameters. This model retains strong performance on the second automotive Ethernet dataset. Beyond detection, per-token surprisal provides fine-grained explainability by attributing anomaly scores to specific protocol fields, supporting interpretable security analysis in heterogeneous in-vehicle networks.

</details>


### [199] [Combining Exploratory Analysis and Automated Analysis for Anomaly Detection in Real-Time Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21222)
*Ahmed Shah,Ibrahim Abualhaol,Mahmoud Gad,Michael Weiss*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个实时BGP流量异常监测原型系统，并探讨了探索式分析与自动化分析的互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师面临海量实时安全信息，易被淹没，且往往只关注有限告警，可能遗漏重要事件及其关联。核心问题在于分析师用于检测、探索和响应网络攻击的系统本身：开发者需要在多抽象层级呈现多源信息的同时保证系统直观易用。BGP是互联网基础设施的关键组件，但高度脆弱，可被劫持用于传播垃圾信息或发起拒绝服务攻击，且部分攻击场景复杂，难以完全自动化检测。

Method: 通过测试一个监测实时BGP流量异常（可能指示安全威胁）的系统开发，考察探索式分析与自动化分析的互补性；构建了一个实时计算指标与威胁告警并可视化告警上下文的原型平台，并分析探索式分析（可视化）与自动化分析之间的交互。

Result: 提出了一个实时计算指标与威胁告警、并可视化告警上下文的原型平台；围绕探索式分析与自动化分析的交互进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 自动化分析与探索式分析具有互补性；通过开发实时流式系统可获得相关洞见。本文对学生、安全研究人员及关注实时安全监控系统开发或使用的开发者具有参考价值。

Abstract: Security analysts can become overwhelmed with monitoring real-time security information that is important to help them defend their network. They also tend to focus on a limited portion of the alerts, and therefore risk missing important events and links between them. At the heart of the problem is the system that analysts use to detect, explore, and respond to cyber-attacks. Developers of security analysis systems face the challenge of developing a system that can present different sources of information at multiple levels of abstraction, while also creating a system that is intuitive to use. In this article, we examine the complementary nature of exploratory analysis and automated analysis by testing the development of a system that monitors real-time Border Gateway Protocol (BGP) traffic for anomalies that might indicate security threats. BGP is an essential component for supporting the infrastructure of the Internet; however, it is also highly vulnerable and can be hijacked by attackers to propagate spam or launch denial-of-service attacks. Some of the attack scenarios on the BGP infrastructure can be quite elaborate, and it is difficult, if not impossible, to fully automate the detection of such attacks. This article makes two contributions: i) it describes a prototype platform for computing indicators and threat alerts in real time and for visualizing the context of an alert, and ii) it discusses the interaction of exploratory analysis (visualization) and automated analysis. This article is relevant to students, security researchers, and developers who are interested in the development or use of real-time security monitoring systems. They will gain insights into the complementary aspects of automated analysis and exploratory analysis through the development of a real-time streaming system.

</details>


### [200] [Transcript-Bound Combiners for Downgrade-Resilient Hybrid Post-Quantum Key Establishment: Definition, Proof, and Embedded-Device Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21273)
*Bhanwar Gupta,Sanjeev Rana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将降级弹性定义为组合器（combiner）层面的局部性质，给出基于博弈的定义，并证明了一个双向分离结果：忽略握手转录（transcript）的混合KEM组合器可被确定性降级攻击；而将会话密钥与确认标签绑定到转录哈希的组合器可阻止所有此类攻击（除可忽略项外），且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合密钥建立（后量子KEM + 经典Diffie-Hellman）已被TLS、SSH、IKE标准化，但在作为独立可替换原语或置于无转录认证的极简握手中时，其派生密钥虽安全，协商过程的完整性却无保障，主动攻击者可剥离后量子选项实现降级攻击。

Method: 作者在组合器层面提出基于博弈的降级弹性定义，分析密钥调度（key schedule）需要包含哪些内容才能使降级弹性成为组合器的局部性质；并给出显式的最强链接（strongest-link）安全界。此外构建了基于已发表Cortex-M4测量值的校准成本模型，并实现了通过30项校验门（30-check validation gate）的验证工具链（harness）。

Result: 证明双向分离：忽视转录的组合器被确定性降级；将会话密钥和确认标签绑定到转录哈希的组合器可阻断所有降级尝试，其失败概率对256位转录哈希而言为可忽略项。成本方面，转录绑定每方增加一次哈希计算，约占握手计算量的11.8%，但仅占含无线电能耗的1.5%，且不增加任何消息或线上字节。

Conclusion: 转录绑定是实现混合KEM降级弹性的关键且低成本的手段：它以极小的计算与能耗代价（无线通信场景下仅1.5%能耗）在不增加通信开销的前提下，将降级弹性转化为组合器的局部性质，为独立部署的混合KEM提供安全保证。

Abstract: Hybrid key establishment runs a post-quantum key-encapsulation mechanism (KEM) alongside a classical Diffie-Hellman primitive, so that the session key stays secure while either component resists attack. This design is now standardized in the Transport Layer Security protocol, Secure Shell, and the Internet Key Exchange, with the standardized module-lattice KEM (ML-KEM) as the post-quantum component. A hybrid KEM secures the derived key, but not the integrity of the negotiation that selects which primitives are used. Full protocols authenticate that negotiation through a handshake transcript; a hybrid KEM deployed as a standalone drop-in primitive, or inside a minimal handshake without transcript authentication, inherits no such guarantee, and an active attacker can strip the post-quantum option. We ask what the key schedule alone must contain to make downgrade resilience a local property of the combiner. We give a game-based definition at the combiner layer and prove a two-sided separation: a combiner that ignores the transcript is downgraded with certainty, whereas one that binds the session key and the confirmation tag to a hash of the transcript blocks every such attempt, up to a term negligible for a 256-bit transcript hash. We also give an explicit strongest-link security bound. Using a calibrated cost model composed from published Cortex-M4 measurements, transcript binding adds one hash per party - about 11.8% of handshake computation but only 1.5% of radio-inclusive energy - and adds no messages or bytes on the wire. Every reported number is produced by a released harness that passes a 30-check validation gate.

</details>


### [201] [Identifying Security Platform Product Abuse with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21303)
*Shaefer Drew,Michael Brautbar,Paul Knight,Edward Raff,Lana Peric-McDermott,Simran Sarin,Nickolas Machado,Hanna Albright,Vitaly Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究SaaS产品滥用防御的端到端落地方案，应对LOTL攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SaaS行业中产品滥用虽个体罕见但持续增长，高级威胁者可在客户环境内滥用安全平台，或对产品本身进行绕过实验；其常采用LOTL攻击规避易被检测的恶意软件。防御需跨多类数据库采集多模态数据、解决稀有高级事件的冷启动问题，并满足成本、用户行为、性能等实际部署约束。

Method: 构建并部署可运营的全系统防御能力：采集跨不同类型数据库的多模态数据；针对事件内在稀有性设计冷启动应对方案；在真实部署约束（成本、用户行为、性能等）内进行系统设计；并引入可解释特征与反事实性能评估。

Result: 产品滥用覆盖率提升35%，月度告警减少30%，并能适应恶意行为者行为的变化；同时对已识别攻击进行了可解释特征价值与反事实性能的回顾性评估。

Conclusion: 该研究证明在真实运营环境下构建全系统产品滥用防御是可行且有效的，兼顾检测覆盖、告警降噪与行为适应性，并为后续系统设计提供了约束与评估经验。

Abstract: Product abuse is an individually rare, but growing, problem across the SaaS industry. Highly sophisticated threat actors can misuse security platforms within customer environments or conduct bypass experiments on the product itself. Threat actors can leverage living-off-the-land (LOTL) attacks to avoid using cumbersome, frequently detected malware. Remediating this threat requires collecting multiple data modalities across different types of databases, addressing a cold-start problem in the intrinsic rarity of such sophisticated but dangerous events, and designing within the constraints of real-world deployment (e.g., cost, user behavior, performance, etc). To wit, we provide the first study of such a whole-system defense, especially with respect to a deployed and operational capability. Our results show an increase in product abuse coverage by 35\%, a 30\% reduction in monthly alerts, and adaptability to changes in malicious actors' behavior. We review both the constraints we considered in designing the system to meet operational requirements and a retrospective evaluation of the value of explainable features and counterfactual performance on previously identified attacks.

</details>


### [202] [Conformal Privacy Auditing: Calibrated Re-identification Attacks with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21340)
*Shuo Huang,Gholamreza Haffari,Xingliang Yuan,Ting Yu,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Privacy Auditing（CPA），一个免分布假设的校准框架，为每篇发布的文本提供针对LLM赋能攻击者的重识别风险统计证书，输出保证以用户指定置信度包含真实身份的同构模糊集，并支持logit访问与仅采样两类攻击者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计通常只报告特定攻击流程的成功率，缺乏有限样本统计保证；而差分隐私等训练期保护难以转化为针对单篇自然语言文档的发布期决策。因此需要一种发布时、逐文档、具备统计保证的重识别风险评估方法。

Method: CPA采用免分布的同构（conformal）校准框架，针对LLM结合辅助知识的身份链接攻击，输出候选身份的同构模糊集，在可交换性假设下保证以用户选定置信度包含真实身份；并由集合大小导出可解释的泄漏代理指标。框架统一支持logit访问与仅采样攻击者，从而可同时审计开源模型与专有API模型。

Result: 在多个发布基准与多种攻击者配置下，CPA实现了校准的覆盖率，并揭示出当辅助知识、LLM增强与发布机制变化时，经认证的可识别性出现显著变化。

Conclusion: CPA为报告和比较跨攻击者配置、数据集与发布机制的发布期链接风险提供了统计基础的依据。

Abstract: Empirical identity leakage from released text is increasingly driven by attackers that combine large language models (LLMs) with auxiliary knowledge to link documents to individuals. Existing audits typically report success rates for specific attack pipelines but lack finite-sample statistical guarantees, while training-time protections such as differential privacy are difficult to translate into release-time decisions for individual natural-language documents. We introduce Conformal Privacy Auditing(CPA), a distribution-free calibration framework that provides a statistical certificate of re-identification risk for each released document against LLM-empowered adversaries. CPA outputs a conformal ambiguity set of candidate identities that is guaranteed to contain the true identity with user-chosen confidence under exchangeability, together with an interpretable leakage proxy derived from set size. CPA supports both logit-access and sampling-only attackers, enabling audits of open-source models and proprietary API models in a unified framework. Across multiple release benchmarks and attacker configurations, CPA achieves calibrated coverage and reveals sharp shifts in certified identifiability as auxiliary knowledge, LLM augmentation, and release mechanisms vary, providing a statistically grounded basis for reporting and comparing release-time linkage risk across attacker configurations, datasets, and release mechanisms alike.

</details>


### [203] [TrustBOM: A Scalable Architecture for Confidentiality-Preserving SBOMs Across Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21419)
*Van Thang Nguyen,Frederic Rupprecht,Tom Lawrence,Lucca Di Benedetto,Sören Schubert,Amor Rezgui,Sebastian Werner,Maria C. Borges,Stefan Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 TrustBOM 架构，用零知识非成员证明实现可保密的软件物料清单（SBOM），在不泄露依赖图的前提下证明漏洞或许可证约束缺席，证明生成随约束数线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBOM 已成为企业架构中软件供应链治理的关键机制，但组织采用受限，主要顾虑在于会暴露敏感的依赖信息。因此需要一种既能完成合规声明、又不泄露依赖图的方案。

Method: TrustBOM 是可集成到企业 CI/CD 工作流中的可扩展保密 SBOM 架构。软件提供方基于消费者定义的政策约束，选择性地对特定漏洞或受限许可证生成零知识非成员证明，从而证其"不在"软件中，而不公开底层依赖图。架构设计使证明生成规模与所断言约束的数量线性相关，而非与 SBOM 规模相关。

Result: 实证评估显示线性性能表现，在通用商用硬件上平均每条约束的证明生成时间约为 0.9 秒，表明在企业平台生态中部署具有可行性。

Conclusion: 基于零知识非成员证明的保密 SBOM 架构在性能上可行，能在保护依赖信息的前提下支撑企业软件供应链治理。

Abstract: Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) have emerged as a key mechanism for software supply chain governance in enterprise architectures. However, their adoption across organizations remains limited due to concerns about exposing sensitive dependency information. To address this limitation, we propose TrustBOM, a scalable architecture for confidentiality-preserving SBOMs integrated into enterprise CI/CD workflows. TrustBOM enables software providers to attest that specific vulnerabilities or restricted licenses are absent from their software without revealing the underlying dependency graph. This is achieved using zero-knowledge non-membership proofs, which are applied selectively based on consumer-defined policy constraints. The architecture ensures that proof generation scales linearly with the number of asserted constraints rather than with the size of the SBOM, enabling efficient operation in large-scale enterprise environments. Empirical evaluation demonstrates linear performance, with an average proof generation time of 0.9 seconds per constraint on commodity hardware, indicating the feasibility of deployment in enterprise platform ecosystems.

</details>


### [204] [CESBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Cryptographic Engineering Security for IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21344)
*Wenquan Zhou,An Wang,Jing Liang,Peien Feng,Jingqi Zhang,Yaoling Ding,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出CESBench，一个面向IoT设备密码工程安全的LLM基准，包含380道专家编写的题目，覆盖六个子领域和四类任务。11个LLM的评测显示综合得分54.4%–83.6%，选择题和代码任务表现接近饱和，而安全判定的论证能力最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备仅靠安全算法不足以保证安全：具有物理访问权限的攻击者可直接攻击实现，且部署后缺陷难以修复。LLM已被用于构建和分析此类实现，但现有基准仅覆盖密码学与通用网络安全，缺少针对密码工程安全的评测基准。

Method: 构建CESBench，含380道专家编写题目，覆盖IoT密码工程安全六个子领域（侧信道、故障注入、实现、对策、评估、集成）；设计四类任务：209道选择题测记忆、67道判断题要求给出安全判定及理由、63道场景题要求工程诊断、41道代码题由572个测试用例评分。选取11个开源与闭源LLM作答；选择题与代码题自动评分，判断题与场景题由LLM评审打分，并与另一模型家族的评审及人工重评交叉校验。

Result: 综合得分区间为54.4%–83.6%。各任务最高分为选择题98.6%、代码95.1%、场景诊断88.4%，但判断题仅58.8%。跨模型统计中，88.5%的安全判定结论正确，但其理由仅获得53.4%的评分点。选择题对最强模型已接近上限，多数代码任务被解决。

Conclusion: 为安全判定提供合理辩护是当前LLM最薄弱的密码工程能力，而记忆性与代码实现类任务已趋于饱和。基准、提示词及逐题结果已公开。

Abstract: For Internet of Things (IoT) devices, a secure algorithm alone is not enough: an attacker with physical access can attack the implementation directly, and its flaws are hard to fix once deployed. Large language models (LLMs) are now used to build and analyze such implementations. LLM benchmarks exist for cryptography and general cybersecurity, but none covers cryptographic engineering. In this paper, we present CESBench, 380 expert-written items across six sub-domains of cryptographic engineering security for IoT devices: side-channel, fault injection, implementation, countermeasures, evaluation, and integration. Four task types target different competences: 209 multiple-choice items test recall, 67 judgment items require a security verdict and its justification, 63 scenario items require an engineering diagnosis, and 41 code tasks are graded by 572 test cases. To validate the benchmark, 11 open-weight and proprietary LLMs answer every item. Multiple-choice and code responses are scored automatically, and judgment and scenario responses by an LLM judge, whose scores are checked against a second judge from another model family and human re-scoring. Composite scores range from 54.4% to 83.6%. The top score on each task type is 98.6% for multiple choice, 95.1% for code, and 88.4% for scenario diagnosis, but only 58.8% for judgment. Across models, 88.5% of verdicts are correct, yet their justifications earn only 53.4% of the rubric marks. Multiple choice is near its ceiling for the strongest models and most code tasks are solved, whereas justifying a security verdict remains the weakest competence. The benchmark, prompts, and per-item results are public.

</details>


### [205] [Verifiable Computation with Trusted Execution Environments and On-Chain Digital Rights Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21728)
*Bingle Stegmann Kruger,Co-Pierre Georg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于TEE与数字权利代币(DRT)的架构，使数据所有者可组合私有数据为数据池，并以限定范围的"计算权利"授权第三方分析者在TEE内对封存数据执行指定开源代码，结果可取回但原始数据不可见，并在分布式账本上发行与兑付，实现参考原型于Solana上运行WASM/Python作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据共享与隐私保护之间存在矛盾：数据所有者希望保留对数据使用方式的事前控制权，而现有方案难以在允许第三方分析的同时避免原始数据泄露。作者主张以"控制"为中心的隐私观，即数据创造者应保留对数据处理方式的前置控制。

Method: 1) 以可信执行环境(TEE)为隔离计算基础，将多方私有数据组合为封存数据池；2) 将计算权利编码为数字权利代币(DRT)，每个DRT将特定开源代码与某一数据池绑定；3) DRT在分布式账本上发行与赎回，实现权利的可审计流转；4) 分析者凭DRT在TEE内对封存数据执行代码，仅获得计算结果，无法访问底层数据；5) 构建参考实现，在Solana上记录赎回，运行WASM/Python作业。

Result: 参考实现成功在封存数据集上执行WASM/Python作业，并将赎回记录写入Solana，验证了该平台在机制上的可行性与工程可实现性，同时暴露了其局限（如依赖TEE信任假设、账本性能与集成开销等）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了以TEE结合DRT与分布式账本实现"计算权利而非数据"共享的可行性，为数据所有者保留事前控制权提供了可落地路径，并为后续在信任模型、性能与通用性方面的改进奠定基础。

Abstract: We present an architecture that enables data owners to combine private data into data pools using Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and manage these pools by issuing narrowly scoped computational rights, encoded as Digital Rights Tokens (DRTs), to third-party data analysts. Each DRT binds specific open-source code to a pool and is issued and redeemed on a distributed ledger. Data analysts can obtain the right to execute open-source code on the combined sealed data inside a TEE and receive the result from this code execution, but not the underlying data. We argue for a control-centric view of privacy in which creators retain ex ante control over how their data is processed. A reference implementation runs WebAssembly (WASM)/Python jobs over sealed datasets and records redemptions on Solana, illustrating the feasibility and limitations of the platform.

</details>


### [206] [Batched Paillier-Based Hamming-Distance Computation over Binary Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21364)
*Yavor Litchev,Liwen Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于Paillier的同态加密客户端，融合进位分离二进制编码、查表加密、降指数解密、CUDA/CGBN运算与持久化设备状态，在512比特嵌入批次上显著提升加密与汉明距离解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加法同态加密支持对加密二进制嵌入的外包计算，但大整数运算与数据搬运成为吞吐瓶颈，需要从编码、算术与运行时状态管理等层面进行系统优化。

Method: 将进位分离的二进制编码、基于查表的加密、降指数解密、CUDA/CGBN大整数算术、持久化设备状态以及批量检索集成组合为Paillier客户端；证明编码正确性，并对比四种CPU与GPU配置，其中查表配置采用280比特指数参数。

Result: 在3次热态试验、每批10,000个随机512比特嵌入下，查表GPU配置达到中位批吞吐43,091次加密/秒与28,983次汉明距离解码/秒；摊薄开销为每向量0.0232 ms与0.0345 ms，相对CPU基线分别提升453.8倍与200.9倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，密码学预计算、批量加速器执行与持久化运行时状态相结合可带来显著吞吐收益；同时区分热批处理性能与单请求延迟，并指出初始化、传输与检索集成仍存在剩余开销。

Abstract: Additively homomorphic encryption supports outsourced computation on encrypted binary embeddings, but large-integer arithmetic and data movement can limit throughput. We describe a Paillier-based client that combines a carry-separated binary encoding, table-based encryption, reduced-exponent decryption, CUDA/CGBN arithmetic, persistent device state, and batched retrieval integration. We establish the encoding's correctness and characterize four CPU and GPU client configurations. The lookup configuration uses a 280-bit exponent-size parameter. Across 3 warm-state trials on batches of 10,000 random 512-bit embeddings, the lookup GPU configuration achieved median-batch throughputs of 43,091 encryptions/s and 28,983 Hamming- distance decodes/s. Its amortized costs were 0.0232 ms and 0.0345 ms per vector, corresponding to factors of 453.8 and 200.9 relative to the measured CPU baseline. These implementation- specific results demonstrate the throughput benefits of combining cryptographic precomputation, batched accelerator execution, and persistent runtime state. The study distinguishes warm-batch performance from isolated-request latency and identifies the remaining costs of initialization, transport, and retrieval integration.

</details>


### [207] [DEFEAT: Stitching Fragmented File I/O Contexts for Early Ransomware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21426)
*Muhammad Ejaz Ahmed,Hyoungshick Kim,Mohsen Ali Alawami,Alsharif Abuadbba,Seyit Camtepe,Surya Nepal,Junaid Qadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DEFEAT通过将勒索软件碎片化的文件操作重组为File Event Gadgets（FEG）并用属性控制流图（ACFG）+GNN无监督聚类，实现单文件粒度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件将文件操作分散到临时/中间文件中，割裂了I/O事件与加密活动的语义联系，使基于孤立文件流的模式匹配或统计学习方法失效。

Method: 将因果相关的文件事件聚类为File Event Gadgets（FEG），每个FEG建模为属性控制流图（ACFG），用图神经网络嵌入后做无监督聚类。

Result: 在覆盖67个勒索家族、97,816,471个文件I/O事件的数据集上，检测准确率99.2%，优于UNVEIL、RWGuard和Peeler 6.57–7.56%；标注工作量减少94%。

Conclusion: FEG不依赖全系统provenance instrumentation，成本低、可扩展，且能在第一个文件加密完成时即完成检测。

Abstract: Ransomware increasingly fragments its file operations across temporary and intermediate files, scattering the semantic context that links individual I/O events to an overarching encryption campaign. This fragmentation defeats existing detectors that reason over isolated file streams -- whether pattern-based methods that match rigid event sequences or learning-based methods that require accumulating statistical evidence across many files. We present DEFEAT, a framework that reconstructs this fragmented, scattered context by grouping causally related file events into File Event Gadgets (FEGs), semantically coherent units that capture the full intent behind sequences of file operations spanning multiple dynamically created files. Unlike provenance graphs (system-wide causal graphs that record relationships among all OS entities, such as processes, files, sockets, and registry keys, across the entire system), FEGs are scoped to the file-operation context of a single user asset, enabling lightweight, targeted analysis without whole-system instrumentation. Each FEG is modelled as an attributed control flow graph (ACFG) and embedded via a graph neural network for unsupervised clustering, enabling analysts to label entire behavioural clusters rather than individual samples, reducing annotation effort by 94%. Evaluated on a corpus of 97,816,471 file I/O events spanning 67 ransomware families, DEFEAT achieves 99.2% detection accuracy and outperforms state-of-the-art methods including UNVEIL, RWGuard, and Peeler by 6.57 to 7.56%. The framework operates at the granularity of a single file encryption: because each ACFG represents exactly one FEG (one user asset context), a cluster label can be assigned as soon as the first file operation completes, enabling detection at the first encrypted file.

</details>


### [208] [HE-Guardrail: A Homomorphic Guardrail Against Jailbreak Attacks for Encrypted Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21484)
*Byeongseo Min,Yongwoo Lee,Young-Sik Kim,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文指出同态加密（HE）驱动的隐私保护大模型推理存在被恶意客户端以越狱提示攻击的安全漏洞，并提出 HE-Guardrail 框架，在密文域内完整评估三种代表性安全护栏并同态控制响应返回，从而在不破坏隐私的前提下防御此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同态加密隐私保护机器学习（HE-based PPML）场景中，客户端提交密文输入，服务器在不解密的情况下推理大语言模型（LLM）。这种端到端的机密性在保护良性客户端的同时，也导致服务器无法检查输入提示或生成结果，因而恶意客户端可提交对抗性提示（如越狱攻击）而不被察觉，现有 HE-LLM 推理存在关键安全漏洞，需要在不破坏隐私的前提下加入防护。

Method: 提出 HE-Guardrail 框架，在密文域内端到端评估安全护栏，并以同态方式控制目标模型的响应是否返回给客户端。实例化采用三种代表性护栏：Llama Guard、JBShield 与 GradSafe，在加密数据上复现其判定逻辑。

Result: HE-Guardrail 在密文域内能够高度复现对应明文护栏的判定结果，并在安全性、效率与效用之间呈现不同的权衡（distinct security-efficiency-utility trade-offs）。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 HE-LLM 推理中被忽视的恶意客户端越狱攻击面，并证明通过密文域护栏评估可在不牺牲客户端隐私的条件下实现有效防御，为隐私保护 LLM 服务提供了可落地的安全方案，同时给出了不同护栏的安全-效率-效用权衡参考。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) has emerged as a promising approach to privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML), enabling computation directly over encrypted data. In HE-based PPML, a client submits an encrypted input to the server, which evaluates models such as large language models (LLMs) without access to the underlying plaintext. However, we identify a critical security vulnerability in this setting: HE-LLM inference is vulnerable to malicious clients that submit adversarial prompts, such as jailbreak attacks. The same confidentiality that protects benign clients also prevents the server from inspecting incoming prompts or generated responses, making adversarial attempts difficult to detect or block and potentially allowing successful attacks to remain entirely invisible to the server. To address this vulnerability, we propose HE-Guardrail, a framework that evaluates guardrail mechanisms entirely over encrypted data and homomorphically controls whether the target-model response is returned to the client. We instantiate HE-Guardrail with three representative guardrails - Llama Guard, JBShield, and GradSafe. Our results show that HE-Guardrail closely reproduces the decisions of the corresponding plaintext guardrails in the encrypted domain, with distinct security-efficiency-utility trade-offs.

</details>


### [209] [ServeGuard: Verifiable, Bounded-Residual Confinement of Operator-Invisible Channels Without Revealing the Certified Read Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21515)
*Dominik Dahlem,Rui Vieira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ServeGuard：一种"携带证明的适配器"供应链原语，通过零知识证明使发布者在不泄露被认证读取因子的前提下，证明第三方适配器相对声明监控器不存在该定向隐蔽信道，并叠加准入时类型守卫绑定字节级保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型的第三方适配器以不透明权重矩阵形式分发，接收方无法在不信任发布者或不检查权重（发布者核心资产）的前提下，确认适配器是否隐藏后门。对于"载荷置于安全监控器结构性盲区"这一类重要情形，检测作为防御是不可靠的：任何经过声明监控器分解的检测器在其盲子空间上恒定不变，且诚实与后门适配器在所有被评估的盲子空间统计量上重叠，因为良性适配同样使用该子空间。故而问题应从"检测该信道"转向"使该信道结构性地不存在并加以证明"。

Method: 发布者构造适配器，使其仅通过监控器覆盖的方向读取输入，并以零知识方式证明这一点而不泄露其认证的读取因子。证书开销极低：最昂贵的部分（识别监控器盲点）是公共基础模型的确定性函数，因此只需证明一个线性恒等式；所服务的残差为基础模型自身的公共下限，而非证明者自选容差。系统实现为 ServeGuard：发布者交付携带证明的适配器，消费者或监管者在无认证读取因子、不信任发布者的条件下验证该类隐藏信道不存在；准入时类型守卫将保证绑定到服务时被接纳的适配器字节。

Result: 在来自四个模型家族、规模至 7B 的八个检查点上：监控预算表现为架构属性——在分组查询（GQA）检查点上测得前沿在值路径秩处饱和，而在多头（MHA）检查点上不饱和。在 0.5B 模型上，对良性适配的"限域"几乎无代价，使监控器质量成为安全杠杆。

Conclusion: 对于监控器结构性盲区类隐蔽信道，检测不可靠而结构性消除可行且可证：ServeGuard 以廉价的零知识单线性恒等式证书，在无需信任发布者、无需暴露读取因子的情况下，为适配器供应链提供可验证的"无该类隐藏信道"保证，并由准入守卫绑定到实际服务字节。安全强度由监控器质量主导，且监控预算受模型架构（GQA/MHA）约束。

Abstract: Third-party adapters for open-weight language models ship as opaque weight matrices; a recipient cannot check whether an adapter hides a backdoor without trusting the publisher or inspecting the weights, the publisher's core asset. For one important class (payloads placed where a safety monitor is structurally blind), detection is unsound as a defense: every detector that factors through the declared monitor is invariant on its blind subspace, and honest and backdoored adapters overlap on every blind-subspace statistic we evaluate, because benign adaptation uses that subspace too. Rather than detect this channel, we make it structurally \emph{absent} and prove that we did. The publisher builds the adapter to read the input only through directions the monitor covers and proves this in zero knowledge, revealing nothing about the read factor it certifies. The certificate is cheap because the expensive part, identifying the monitor's blind spot, is a deterministic function of the \emph{public} base model, so only one linear identity is proved; the served residual is the base model's own public floor, not a prover-chosen tolerance. The result is \emph{ServeGuard}, a supply-chain primitive: the publisher ships a \emph{proof-carrying adapter} whose proof lets a consumer or regulator verify, without the certified read factor and without trusting the publisher, that the adapter carries no hidden channel of this class relative to the declared monitor; an admission-time typing guard binds the guarantee to the adapter bytes admitted at serving time. Across eight checkpoints up to 7B from four families, the monitoring budget is architectural: the measured frontier saturates at the value-path rank on grouped-query checkpoints but not on multi-head ones. On a 0.5B model confinement is nearly free for benign adaptation, making monitor quality the security lever.

</details>


### [210] [Critical sets of Latin squares based on autoparatopisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21532)
*Manuel González-Regadera,Raúl M. Falcón,María Dolores Frau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用拉丁方的自仿顶变换群的轨道结构，通过引入"给定仿顶变换位于自仿顶变换群中"的临界集计算问题，解决秘密共享方案中信息持有者缺失导致密钥无法恢复的难题，并给出了阶数不超过6的拉丁方的最小与最大临界集规模，实现了新的秘密共享方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于拉丁方临界集的密码学秘密共享方案中，存在一个主要问题：若某些同时隶属于多个临界集的信息片段持有者缺席，则会成为恢复秘密的必需环节，从而导致协议失效。本文旨在消除这一对特定持有者的依赖。

Method: 利用所考察拉丁方的自仿顶变换群（autoparatopism group）所描述的条目轨道，将问题推广为计算"自仿顶变换群中包含给定仿顶变换"的临界集。论证此类临界集仅依赖于该自仿顶变换的共轭类与拉丁方的主类。

Result: 以阶数不超过6的拉丁方为例，确定了与自仿顶变换相关联的临界集的最小与最大规模，并将该方法应用于新秘密共享方案的设计中。

Conclusion: 借助自仿顶变换群的轨道方法，可有效规避因共同信息持有者缺席而造成的秘密恢复问题，所得临界集具有良好的分类不依赖性，为构建更稳健的基于拉丁方的秘密共享方案提供了理论依据与实现途径。
