Abstract: In cryptography, critical sets of Latin squares have particularly been implemented to design secret sharing schemes. A main problem in these cryptographic protocols arises from absent holders of pieces of information that are common to different critical sets, because they become indispensable to recover the secret. This paper solves this problem by making use of the orbits of entries described by the autoparatopism group of the Latin square under consideration. To this end, we introduce the more general problem of computing critical sets of Latin squares having a given paratopism in their autoparatopism group. These critical sets depend only on the conjugacy class of the autoparatopism and the main class of the Latin square under consideration. Based on this fact, as an illustrative example, we determine the smallest and largest sizes of critical sets associated with autoparatopisms of Latin squares of order up to six. We implement this approach in the design of a new secret sharing scheme.

</details>


### [211] [Et Tu, MacBook? Unprivileged Keystroke Inference and Context Profiling via the Built-in IMU Side Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21569)
*Jiaji He,Yi Shi,Junfeng Cai,Chang Liu,Yongqiang Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示 Apple MacBook 内置 IMU 侧信道漏洞,并提出首个非特权侧信道攻击 BRUTUS,在按键恢复、用户识别和环境分析上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MacBook 单机身内置 IMU 虽用于设备姿态与运动感知,却无意中捕获用户交互及环境振动信号,构成新颖且此前未被探索的侧信道,且相关访问缺乏严格管控。

Method: 通过 IOKit 驱动实现非根权限的 IMU 数据访问,并结合 HIDIdleTime 与 CGEventSource 两个无内容系统元数据接口增强泄露;对 IMU 数据进行刻画,提取按键身份、桌面材质、用户行为三维度信息,构建非特权侧信道攻击 BRUTUS。

Result: BRUTUS 按键恢复字符级准确率达 89.1%–97.5%;借助语言模型可 100% 准确重建部分句子;在用户识别与环境画像上,无标签地发现并正确分配用户与环境配置文件。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显对内置 IMU 传感器访问进行严格管控的迫切必要性。

Abstract: Recent generations of Apple MacBooks embed an inertial measurement unit (IMU) within their unibody chassis for device orientation and motion sensing. However, this IMU inadvertently captures not only intended device-level information but also subtle physical vibrations from user interactions and the surrounding environment. These signals establish a novel, previously unexplored side channel. We uncover a vulnerability allowing non-root access to IMU data via an IOKit driver, alongside two content-free system metadata interfaces (HIDIdleTime and CGEventSource) that further enrich the side-channel leakage. Through rigorous characterization of the IMU data, we reveal that the leakage spans three core dimensions: (1) keystroke identity (which key is typed), (2) desk surface (where the laptop is placed), and (3) user behavior (who is typing). Leveraging these findings, we introduce BRUTUS, the first comprehensive unprivileged side-channel attack targeting built-in IMU sensors on Apple MacBooks. BRUTUS achieves a character-level accuracy of 89.1% to 97.5% in key recovery. Furthermore, aided by language models, it can successfully reconstruct certain sentences with 100% accuracy. For user identification and environment profiling, BRUTUS correctly discovers user and environment profiles without labels and correctly assigns subsequent segments to their corresponding profiles. Ultimately, this work highlights the urgent necessity of strictly regulating access to built-in IMU sensors.

</details>


### [212] [Micro-Collaborative Poisoning: A Distributed Attack on RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21573)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"微观协同投毒"（Micro-Collaborative Poisoning），一种将虚假目标声明拆分到多个局部合理的文档中的分布式RAG攻击，并在108种配置下验证其攻击效果与影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部知识源增强大模型输出，但这种依赖也带来了投毒攻击面。传统投毒多集中于单一恶意段落，而分布式、弱信号的协同投毒威胁可能被现有防御忽视，因此需要系统评估其机制与影响因素。

Method: 将虚假目标声明分散到多个局部合理的文档中，形成跨文档的弱对抗信号累积；通过变化数据集、检索器架构、检索深度、数据库构成、投毒数据库数量、生成器模型等，构建108种RAG配置进行评测，并进行文档级投毒可见性分析。

Result: 攻击效果并非由单个主导性投毒段落驱动，而是多个检索源中弱对抗信号的累积。增大top-k、投毒多个数据库会提高这些信号同时出现在检索上下文中的概率；干净数据库多样性和更强的检索器可降低其影响。文档级分析显示其显式投毒特征弱于直接投毒，难以通过孤立文档检查暴露。

Conclusion: 微观协同投毒是一种隐蔽且有效的分布式RAG投毒威胁，其下游影响依赖跨文档弱信号累积，防御需超越单文档审查，从检索上下文整体和信号协同层面进行检测与缓解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves large language models by grounding outputs in external knowledge sources, but this dependency also creates a surface for poisoning attacks. This paper introduces Micro-Collaborative Poisoning, a distributed attack in which a false target claim is divided across multiple locally plausible documents instead of being concentrated in a single malicious passage. We evaluate the attack across 108 RAG configurations by varying dataset, retriever architecture, retrieval depth, database composition, number of poisoned databases, and generator model. The results indicate that Micro-Collaborative Poisoning is not driven by a single dominant poisoned passage, but by the accumulation of weak adversarial signals across retrieved sources. Increasing top-$k$ and poisoning multiple databases make it more likely that these signals will appear together in the retrieved context, while clean database diversity and stronger retrievers can reduce their influence. The document-level poisoning visibility analysis further shows that this threat is difficult to expose through isolated document inspection, since Micro-Collaborative Poisoning achieves downstream influence while leaving a weaker explicit poisoning signature than direct poisoning.

</details>


### [213] [CIPL: A Channel-Aware Framework for Recoverable Privacy Leakage in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21686)
*Tao Huang,Guosen Wu,Guolong Zheng,Jiayang Meng,Chen Hou,Xu Yang,Xuechao Yang,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CIPL框架，以"通道"视角端到端评估LLM智能体的黑盒隐私泄露，揭示敏感单元的存储标签并不能决定其被攻击者恢复的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体隐私泄露评估多局限于单一组件（记忆、检索、工具调用），无法区分"内部暴露"与"外部观察者实际可恢复的信息"，缺乏跨异构流水线的可比标准。

Method: CIPL将目标建模为敏感来源、选择、组装、执行、观察、提取六个阶段，在统一协议下评估从"被选中的敏感单元"到"攻击者可恢复输出"的转换；在基于记忆、检索中介、工具中介三类目标上开展实验，并以BrowserUse实时智能体作案例研究，辅以分层语义审计。

Result: 存储标签本身不决定可恢复性：记忆类目标接近饱和，检索中介泄露常为部分泄露，工具中介与实时智能体泄露强烈依赖观察面、提示—通道对齐度、检索深度与提供方行为；分层语义审计还能发现精确匹配遗漏的、对攻击者有用的披露。

Conclusion: CIPL为比较异构智能体流水线中"内部敏感依赖"如何转化为"外部可恢复泄露"提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Privacy leakage in LLM agents is commonly evaluated within individual components such as memory, retrieval, or tool-use pipelines, which makes it difficult to distinguish internal exposure from information that an external observer can actually recover. We present CIPL (Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage), a channel-aware evaluation framework for black-box privacy leakage in LLM agents. CIPL represents a target through sensitive source, selection, assembly, execution, observation, and extraction stages and evaluates the transition from selected sensitive units to attacker-recoverable output under a shared protocol. Experiments across memory-based, retrieval-mediated, and tool-mediated targets, together with a BrowserUse live-agent case study, show that storage labels alone do not determine recoverability. Memory targets form a near-saturated reference case, retrieval-mediated leakage is frequently partial, and tool-mediated and live-agent leakage varies strongly with observation surface, prompt-to-channel alignment, retrieval depth, and provider behavior. A stratified semantic audit further identifies attacker-useful disclosures that canonical exact matching misses. CIPL therefore provides a common framework for comparing how internal sensitive dependence is realized as externally recoverable leakage across heterogeneous agent pipelines.

</details>


### [214] [TERMon: Detecting Persistent Behavioral Threats in Edge AI via Hardware-Native Ternary Runtime Monitor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21713)
*Arish Sateesan,Edlira Dushku*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TERMon：一种轻量级硬件运行时监控器，通过观测推理行为检测边缘AI加速器异常，采用三值模式与温度计编码指纹并行匹配，无需片上BRAM/DSP，决策延迟仅两个时钟周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI加速器日益部署于安全攸关场景，其输出可能控制物理执行器、访问控制决策或触发告警。此类场景中，模型损坏、分布偏移和对抗输入仍能产生格式正确且置信度高的预测，导致运行时故障常被漏检。现有方法依赖重新执行模型或形式化验证，开销过大，因此需要一种轻量的行为级监控手段。

Method: TERMon以类条件可信行为的三值（ternary）模式表示，硬件高效地与温度计编码（thermometer-encoded）的指纹并行匹配。三值编码能够精确复现对应的未量化区间决策。监控对象为推理行为特征而非重新执行或形式化验证模型。

Result: TERMon能按行为影响程度成比例地检测有害权重损坏；但在严格假阳性工作点下，分布外（OOD）与对抗输入基本无法通过所监控的特征加以区分。在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现，流水线设计无需片上块RAM与DSP，决策延迟为两个时钟周期。

Conclusion: TERMon验证了行为级硬件运行时监控在边缘AI安全场景中的可行性与低开销优势，尤其对权重损坏检测有效；但当前监控特征对OOD与对抗输入的区分能力有限，是后续工作需改进的方向。

Abstract: Edge AI accelerators are increasingly deployed in safety-critical environments, where model outputs may control physical actuators, make access-control decisions, or trigger alarms. In these settings, runtime failures often remain undetected because model corruption, distribution shift, and adversarial inputs can still produce well-formed, confident predictions. This paper presents TERMon, a lightweight hardware runtime monitor that detects such anomalies by observing inference behavior rather than re-executing or formally verifying the model. TERMon represents class-conditional trusted behavior as hardware-efficient ternary patterns that are matched in parallel against a thermometer-encoded fingerprint. The ternary encoding reproduces the corresponding unquantized range decision exactly. TERMon detects harmful weight corruptions in proportion to their behavioral impact, while out-of-distribution and adversarial inputs are largely not separable using the monitored features at a strict false-positive operating point. We implemented TERMon on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA, and the pipelined design requires no on-chip block RAM or DSPs and has a two-cycle decision latency.

</details>


### [215] [A Framework to Quantify the Probability of Future Cyber Loss Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21717)
*Siem Peters,Martin Eian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LEFSA概率框架，将损失事件频率（LEF）估计重构为机器级网络损失事件（CLE）预测与分层基础设施聚合，基于23家组织的MDR遥测数据验证，XGBoost平均AUC达0.90，证明运营遥测数据可用于可扩展、可解释的定量网络风险估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络风险量化面临运营数据有限、损失事件频率（LEF）难以量化两大挑战。现有方法缺乏可扩展且可解释的估计手段，亟需一种能利用运营安全遥测数据、并在多层级（机器、服务、业务流程、组织）上一致应用的建模框架。

Method: 提出LEFSA概率框架：将LEF估计重构为机器级CLE预测与分层基础设施级聚合两步。先从运营网络安全遥测中估计经校准的机器级CLE概率，再在考虑机器间依赖性的前提下跨基础设施层聚合，从而支持机器、服务、业务流程和组织层级的风险估计。评估使用来自23家组织、基于Microsoft Defender for Endpoint的专有MDR遥测数据，比较多种模型的预测性能与校准误差。

Result: XGBoost取得最强预测性能，平均ROC-AUC为0.90，且在各评估周期内校准误差持续较低。结果表明运营网络安全遥测包含关于未来CLE发生的大量预测信息。

Conclusion: 机器级概率建模与分层聚合可作为定量、数据驱动网络风险管理的可行基础，为在机器、服务、业务流程及组织层面实现可扩展、可解释、可落地的网络风险估计提供支撑。

Abstract: Cybersecurity risk quantification remains challenging due to limited operational data and difficulties in quantifying Loss Event Frequency (LEF). This paper introduces the Loss Event Frequency Security Analyser (LEFSA), a probabilistic framework that reformulates LEF estimation as machine-level Cyber Loss Event (CLE) prediction combined with hierarchical infrastructure-level aggregation. LEFSA estimates calibrated machine-level CLE probabilities from operational cybersecurity telemetry and aggregates them across infrastructure layers while accounting for machine-level dependencies. This provides a foundation for scalable, explainable, and operationally applicable cyber risk estimation at the level of machines, services, business processes, and the entire organization. The framework was evaluated using proprietary Managed Detection & Response telemetry from 23 organizations using Microsoft Defender for Endpoint. XGBoost achieved the strongest predictive performance, with a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.90 and consistently low calibration error across evaluation periods. The results demonstrate that operational cybersecurity telemetry contains substantial predictive information for future CLE occurrence, supporting probabilistic machine-level modeling and hierarchical aggregation as a promising foundation for quantitative, data-driven cyber risk management.

</details>


### [216] [CASCADE Against Jailbreaks: Combination Across Stages with Controlled Attack-Defense Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21793)
*Jiale Luo,Eric Han*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大语言模型越狱防御在流水线各阶段内部及跨阶段的组合效果，提出统一威胁模型与标准化攻击成功率评估框架，覆盖19种攻击与15种防御，发现无单一最优防御，但合理组合可在极小效用损失下显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM越狱攻击的防御分布在输入修改、输出审查等不同流水线阶段，但各阶段应部署何种防御、如何组合尚不明确；以往实证研究受攻击成功率定义与实验设置不一致的影响，多为孤立评估，难以横向比较与组合指导。

Method: 在统一的直接、黑箱、单轮攻击威胁模型下，构建决策框架：提出原则化的攻击成功率公式并结合受控查询预算进行标准化评估，同时设定明确的公平性规则；据此对19种攻击和15种防御进行跨阶段与阶段内的组合系统评测。

Result: 实验表明不存在普遍最优的单一防御；但经过良好选择的防御组合能够以极小的效用退化实现显著的安全提升，并据此给出分层防御流水线的实用建议。

Conclusion: 防御效果依赖组合而非单点方案，标准化评估与公平比较是获得可靠结论的前提；分层防御流水线结合合理组合是兼顾安全与效用的可行路径。

Abstract: Defenses against jailbreak attacks on Large Language Models (LLMs) operate at different pipeline stages, such as input modification or output guard, but it remains unclear which defenses to deploy at each stage and how to combine them. Prior empirical studies, fragmented by inconsistent attack-success-rate definitions and experimental settings, have evaluated defenses largely in isolation. Here we present the first systematic study, to our knowledge, of defense combinations both within and across pipeline stages, under a consistent threat model of direct, black-box, single-turn attacks. Our decision framework standardizes evaluation through a principled attack-success-rate formulation with controlled query budgets, together with explicit fairness rules. Across 19 attacks and 15 defenses, we find that no single defense is universally best, but well-chosen combinations achieve substantial safety with minimal utility degradation, yielding practical recommendations for layered defense pipelines.

</details>


### [217] [Watermarkable Multi-Draft Speculative Sampling via Poisson Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21858)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Zhan Gao,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于泊松过程的多草稿投机采样算法，在大语言模型推理中同时优化采样效率与输出水印，首次实现多草稿、草稿器不变的投机采样方案，兼顾水印强度与采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署需兼顾推理效率与输出溯源，可分别通过投机采样和水印实现；但已有研究表明二者结合极为困难，甚至可能无法兼得，因此需要新方法改进这一根本性权衡。

Method: 提出基于泊松过程的多草稿投机采样算法，可在不降低投机接受率的前提下嵌入无偏水印；算法基于精确的无通信列表耦合（list-coupling-without-communication）方案，从而获得草稿器不变性。

Result: 该算法自身具备强采样效率，并可自然嵌入水印；实验验证其在水印强度与采样效率两方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: 这是首个同时保持水印强度与采样效率的多草稿、草稿器不变投机采样方案，改进效率与溯源之间的权衡前沿。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of tasks, motivating two important aspects of deployment: inference efficiency and output provenance, which can be tackled by speculative sampling and watermarking, respectively. However, recent works have shown that combining these two goals is highly nontrivial and can be potentially impossible. In this work, we develop a novel multi-draft speculative sampling algorithm based on Poisson processes that improves the frontier of this fundamental trade-off. The proposed algorithm has strong sampling efficiency on its own and, more interestingly, is naturally watermarkable: we can embed an unbiased watermark without degrading speculative acceptance. Moreover, our algorithm is based on an exact list-coupling-without-communication scheme, which yields a drafter invariance property that benefits both sampling and watermarking. It is the first multi-draft, drafter-invariant speculative sampling scheme that maintains both watermark strength and sampling efficiency, and we experimentally verify its strong performance in both aspects.

</details>


### [218] [SFPF: Spatio-Frequency Polarization Fingerprint for Anomalous Wireless Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21873)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,Daoyuan Shen,Meng Zhang,Dong Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-频率极化指纹（SFPF），通过在多个频率与观测方向上联合表征复极化响应，用于检测部署无线设备中被替换的异常硬件。仿真表明在相同观测预算下，SFPF 相比传统极化指纹（PF）在归一化距离、Fisher 分数和类间/类内比上分别提升 17.7%、45.8% 和 11.3%；实验表明在 0–20 dB 范围内 SFPF 稳定优于射频指纹（RFF）与 PF，在 15–20 dB 时异常设备检测的 F1 达 87.3–90.4%、AUROC 达 85.4–95.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署无线设备需定期检测，因为未授权硬件替换可能保留通信功能、凭证与逻辑身份，导致异常设备难以识别。传统射频指纹（RFF）在替换硬件与合法硬件高度相似时区分度不足；而单一观测方向构建的极化指纹（PF）可能遗漏空间非均匀的极化变化。

Method: 提出空间-频率极化指纹（SFPF），在多个频率和观测方向上联合表征复极化响应，传统 PF 可视为其固定方向切片。从硬件相关的模态激励、方向性远场辐射和极化投影推导 SFPF 的形成机理，并进行一阶灵敏度分析，说明同一硬件变化引起的响应随频率和方向变化，从而论证联合空间-频率采集的必要性。

Result: 电磁仿真验证了空间-频率灵敏度的非均匀性；在相同观测预算下，SFPF 相比 PF 在归一化距离、Fisher 分数和类间/类内比上分别提升 17.7%、45.8% 和 11.3%。实验显示 SFPF 在 0–20 dB 范围内稳定优于 RFF 与 PF；在 15–20 dB 时异常设备检测 F1 为 87.3–90.4%，AUROC 为 85.4–95.5%。

Conclusion: SFPF 通过联合空间与频率维度刻画极化响应，有效克服了 RFF 区分度不足和单方向 PF 空间信息缺失的问题，在低信噪比条件下仍保持较高的异常设备检测性能，为部署无线设备的硬件一致性检测提供了更可靠的指纹方案。

Abstract: Periodic inspection of deployed wireless devices is necessary because unauthorized hardware replacement may preserve communication functions, credentials, and logical identity, making anomalous devices difficult to detect. Such inspections are conducted under controlled measurement conditions to verify that each device remains consistent with its enrolled hardware state. Conventional radio-frequency fingerprint (RFF) may provide insufficient separation when replacement hardware closely resembles legitimate hardware, while a polarization fingerprint (PF) constructed at one observation direction may miss spatially nonuniform polarization changes. This paper proposes the spatio-frequency polarization fingerprint (SFPF), which jointly represents complex polarization responses over multiple frequencies and observation directions; conventional PF is its fixed-direction slice. We derive SFPF formation from hardware-dependent modal excitation, directional far-field radiation, and polarization projection. A first-order sensitivity analysis shows that the response to the same hardware change varies with both frequency and direction, motivating joint spatio-frequency acquisition. Electromagnetic simulations confirm the nonuniform spatio-frequency sensitivity and show that, under the same observation budget, SFPF improves normalized distance, Fisher score, and the inter-/intra-class ratio over PF by 17.7%, 45.8%, and 11.3%, respectively. Experiments show that SFPF consistently outperforms RFF and PF over 0--20~dB. At 15--20~dB, SFPF achieves anomalous-device F1 scores of 87.3--90.4% and AUROC values of 85.4--95.5%.

</details>


### [219] [End-to-End Hard-Label Cryptanalytic Model Extraction Using Efficient Sign Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21941)
*Akira Ito,Takayuki Miura,Yosuke Todo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对硬标签模型提取中符号恢复步骤查询量大的问题，提出一种无需专用查询的新符号恢复算法，实现了黑盒端到端提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN参数是宝贵资产，硬标签（仅可观察最终标签）是模型提取中最难的场景。Eurocrypt 2025提出多项式时间硬标签ReLU MLP提取，但其中符号恢复步骤查询量大、计算成本高，难以黑盒实现，导致对已训练深层ReLU MLP的完整黑盒端到端演示仍属空白。

Method: 提出基于与现有方法完全不同原理的新符号恢复算法，无需为符号恢复设计专用查询，并将该算法与硬标签提取其余步骤结合，全部在黑盒设置下实现。

Result: 新方法符号恢复准确率高于现有方法，可高效恢复已训练模型的符号；在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上训练、宽度16、4或6隐层的模型实现端到端提取，标签一致率超过98%。

Conclusion: 新符号恢复算法消除了硬标签模型提取的瓶颈，使全流程黑盒端到端提取成为可能，并在已训练模型上验证了有效性。

Abstract: The importance of deep neural networks (DNNs) is widely recognized, and the parameters obtained through training are regarded as valuable assets. Recently, attacks that extract these parameters using only oracle queries to a DNN have been actively studied at IACR conferences. The hard-label setting is the most challenging setting for model extraction, where an adversary can observe only the final output label, such as "dog" or "cat." At Eurocrypt 2025, Carlini et al. proposed polynomial-time hard-label extraction of ReLU-based MLPs. However, one step of this attack process, i.e., sign recovery, requires a large number of queries and substantial computation. Implementing this step in a black-box setting remains difficult. Consequently, a fully black-box end-to-end demonstration on trained deep ReLU MLPs has remained a challenge. In this paper, we propose a new sign-recovery algorithm based on a completely different principle from the existing method. Our method requires no dedicated queries for sign recovery. In our experiments, it achieves higher sign-recovery accuracy than the existing method. Consequently, it enables efficient sign recovery even for trained models. With our sign-recovery algorithm, all steps of hard-label model extraction can be implemented in a black-box setting. By combining these implementations, we demonstrate end-to-end model extraction from models trained on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, with width 16 and 4 or 6 hidden layers, achieving over 98% label agreement.

</details>


### [220] [Provisional Reachability: Containing Agents by Making Every Crossing Revocable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21957)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在"防御者每期须阻断的信息量（bits/period）"这一度量基础上，提出以托管—审计—吊销构成的速率型防御方案，给出上界 L(r)~c/(er) 并证明其可作为公开方案；进一步引入秘密衰减机制得到误差阈值 mu > g/L，并通过仿真与欺骗/密钥化实验验证泄露从 100,000 降至 59 bits（1,704 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作指出防御者随时间必须阻断的量具有"每期比特数"的单位，但未给出具体方案来达到这一速率。本文旨在构造并刻画一种能达到该速率的防御机制，同时厘清"速率"与"总量"的区别——仅靠托管无法阻止秘密在每次运行中沉淀，因此需要额外的衰减机制来设定可达性阈值。

Method: 方案将每个穿越项托管一期，以概率 r 独立审计，若任一审出即吊销窗口。对携带 c bits、穿越 k 次的对手，期望泄露为 kc(1-r)^k，在 k*=1/ln(1/(1-r)) 处取极大，得到每窗口上界 L(r)~c/(er)；该上界是对对手选择取上确界，故方案可公开。引入秘密每期以 mu 比例衰减，使持有量收敛于 g/mu。并做仿真对比、欺骗实验（表面命名说谎、拒绝提供参考）、密钥化实验（入口点/指称），以及方差位置分析（审计率处为凸、激活预算处为乘性）。

Result: 仿真与解析上界吻合至 7.7 个标准误。衰减机制给出误差阈值：在 20,000 个窗口中，0.9mu* 时 100% 运行可组装，2mu* 时 0%。欺骗实验显示命名说谎对准确率无影响（18/18，三种读者强度下 100%）；拒绝提供参考有效且方式不同——没有读者愿意commit。密钥化入口点仅隐藏 0.10 bits，密钥化指称以随机准确率隐藏 3.00 bits 中的 2.64 bits，而 100% 读者仍认定其为普通 Python。方差：从审计率中移除（此处战利品对 r 凸），加到激活预算中（此处存活率为乘性），固定均值下灭绝率由 70% 升至 100%。端到端将泄露从 100,000 降至 59 bits（1,704 倍），保留 12% 合法工作；将窗口按调用者密钥化可无损恢复至 100%，代价是上界变为按主体计。65 个只读工具中，托管每调用仍留 2,400 bits，即 10 倍而非无穷。

Conclusion: 该方案给出了可公开的速率型泄露上界 L(r)~c/(er)，并借由秘密衰减得到 Eigen 意义上的尖锐误差阈值 mu > g/L。托管本身是速率而非总量控制，需配合衰减才使 L-bit 秘密不可达。欺骗性防护依赖对手推理失误则不可靠，而拒绝参考与密钥化指称有效但各有代价（前者无人commit，后者泄露 2.64/3.00 bits）。方差应置于乘性预算而非凸的审计率处。整体将泄露压缩 1,704 倍，但残余泄露仍为 10 倍量级而非无穷。

Abstract: A companion paper found that what a defender must block over time has units: bits per period [Takashita, 2026a]. This paper sets it. Hold every crossing in escrow for one period, audit each held item independently with probability r, and revoke the window if any audit catches something. An adversary crossing k times, each carrying c bits, expects kc(1-r)^k, maximised at k* = 1/ln(1/(1-r)), a bound of L(r) ~ c/(er) per window. The bound is a supremum over the adversary's choice, so the scheme may be public; simulation matches it to 7.7 standard errors. It is a rate, not a total: escrow alone still lets the secret assemble in every run. But if the secret decays at a fraction mu of held bits per period, holdings converge to g/mu at any horizon, so an L-bit secret is unreachable once mu > g/L -- an error threshold in Eigen's sense, sharp where the closed form puts it (100% of runs assemble at 0.9mu*, 0% at 2mu*, over 20,000 windows). Deception that needs the adversary to reason badly fails: a surface whose names lie left accuracy at 18 of 18, and 100% at three reader strengths. Withholding reference works, and differently: no reader would commit at all. Keying the entry points hides 0.10 bits of what a module does; keying the denotation hides 2.64 of 3.00 at chance accuracy, while 100% of readers still call it ordinary Python. Variance must be removed from the audit rate, where loot is convex in r, and added to the activation budget, where survival is multiplicative: extinction 70% to 100% at a fixed mean. End to end the stack takes the leak from 100,000 to 59 bits, a factor of 1,704, leaving 12% of legitimate work standing; keying the window to the caller restores that to 100% at no cost in leakage, at the price of a bound that is per principal. Of 65 read-only tools, escrow leaves 2,400 bits per call: a factor of 10, not infinity.

</details>


### [221] [The Supersingular Isogeny Problem in Time and Memory $p^{1/3+o(1)}$, Unconditionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22018)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文给出求解超奇异椭圆曲线 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的 Las Vegas 算法，在无光滑性启发式假设下，期望时间与空间复杂度为 $p^{1/3+o(1)}$，改进了此前的无条件指数 $2/5$，并与 Wesolowski 在额外假设下达到的 $1/3$ 指数相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题（求超奇异椭圆曲线 $E/\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ 的非标量自同态）经已知归约可一并解决超奇异自同态环问题与同源问题。此前无条件最好指数为 $2/5$，Wesolowski 在关于小次数分解的假设下达到 $1/3$。本文动机在于去除该启发式假设，给出无条件达到 $1/3$ 指数的算法。

Method: 算法为 Las Vegas 类型。事先固定一族由小素数乘积构成的次数；利用已知计数结果获得从曲线到其 Frobenius 共轭曲线的众多该次数同源，并通过碰撞估计证明这些同源分布在足够多不同曲线上，使得随机游走能抵达其中一条曲线。在该曲线上将次数拆分为两部分，枚举两组较短同源的列表，通过匹配目标得到通往共轭曲线的同源，再与 Frobenius 复合即得所需自同态。

Result: 在不对光滑性作启发式假设的分析下，算法期望时间与空间为 $p^{1/3}\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p})\bigr)=p^{1/3+o(1)}$，即无条件达到指数 $1/3$，优于此前无条件指数 $2/5$。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了 Wesolowski 结果中对小次数分解的假设，给出无条件达到 $p^{1/3+o(1)}$ 复杂度求解 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的 Las Vegas 算法，从而同时改善超奇异自同态环与同源问题的求解复杂度。

Abstract: Given a supersingular elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, the $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ problem asks for a non-scalar endomorphism of $E$. By known reductions, solving this problem also solves the supersingular endomorphism ring and isogeny problems. Wesolowski obtained exponent $1/3$ under an assumption on the factorization of a small degree, whereas the previous unconditional exponent was $2/5$. We give a Las Vegas algorithm, analyzed without a smoothness heuristic, with expected time and memory \[ p^{1/3}\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p})\bigr) = p^{1/3+o(1)}. \]
  The algorithm fixes in advance a family of degrees that are products of small primes. Known counting results provide many isogenies of these degrees from curves to their Frobenius conjugates, and a collision estimate shows that the isogenies occur on sufficiently many distinct curves for a random walk to reach one of them. From such a curve, the algorithm splits a degree into two parts, enumerates two lists of shorter isogenies, and matches their targets to obtain an isogeny to the conjugate, whose composition with Frobenius gives the required endomorphism.

</details>


### [222] [APort Vault: Benchmarking AI Agent Payment Authorization with the Open Agent Passport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22076)
*Uchi Uchibeke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APort Vault 是一个面向工具调用型 AI 智能体的支付授权基准，在公开 CTF 事件中回放了 4,371 条人工攻击，覆盖 8 家实验室的 14 个模型、5 种策略配置与 2 条回放轨道，并对比有无 Open Agent Passport (OAP) 确定性前置检查的效果，共完成 225,964 次评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准常将多类失败压缩为单一分数，难以区分"模型愿意发起支付请求"与"授权边界被突破"两种性质不同的事件，导致结论无法通过审阅。作者希望以支付授权为切口，建立一个能分离请求倾向与授权边界违规的可复核基准。

Method: 基准回放人类在公开夺旗活动中对真实支付智能体编写的 4,371 条攻击，跨 14 个模型、5 种策略配置（Level 1–4 等）与 2 条回放轨道，分别在纯模型和叠加实现 OAP 规范的确定性前置检查两种条件下评估；每次评估记录五类不同事件而非合并为单一指标。Level 4 的 1,293 条提示在全模型上逐一评估。

Result: 请求发起率因配置差异远大于模型差异（Level 1 为 10.9%、Level 2 为 3.0%、Level 3 为 0.1%、Level 4 为 79.4%），但攻击与策略同变，故不宜作因果解读。Level 4 上各模型请求率为 71.2%–84.3%，809 条提示（62.6%）令全部 14 个模型发起请求并成功支付给该级允许名单收款方。授权边界差异显著：Level 2–4 中向未授权收款方的转账，纯模型为 140/76,842，叠加检查层后为 0/69,297；在匹配的模型、提示与轨道三元组上为 105 对 0。该零结果覆盖 790 个源会话，每会话上界 0.38%，且并非靠拒绝支付实现——检查层后仍执行 25,370 笔支付，策略层评估了 25,640 次转账调用，仅拒绝 187 次，其中 148 次因收款方被禁止。

Conclusion: 将"请求发起"与"授权边界合规"分开报告，能揭示合并指标掩盖的结构性差异：请求率主要由策略配置与攻击队列驱动，而 OAP 前置检查层可在几乎不影响正常支付执行的前提下将越权转账降至零。作者公开 225,964 次评估、各级护照、评分代码与分析脚本，便于复现与审阅。

Abstract: APort Vault is a benchmark for payment authorization in tool-using AI agents. It replays 4,371 attacks written by humans against a live payment agent during a public capture-the-flag event, across 14 models from 8 labs, five policy configurations and two replay tracks, with and without a deterministic pre-action check implementing the Open Agent Passport (OAP) specification. 225,964 evaluations completed. We report five distinct events per evaluation, because collapsing them is how an agent benchmark produces a number that does not survive review.
  Requests are common and their rate differs far more across configurations than across models, though each attack exists at exactly one configuration so policy and attack cohort vary together: 10.9% of model-alone evaluations at Level 1, 3.0% at Level 2, 0.1% at Level 3, 79.4% at Level 4. On the 1,293 Level 4 prompts, each evaluated on every model, request rates run from 71.2% to 84.3%, and 809 prompts (62.6%) elicited a request from all fourteen models, each ending in a successful payment to the level's allowlisted recipient.
  The authorization boundary is where the conditions diverge. At Levels 2 to 4, transfers to recipients the passport did not permit number 140 of 76,842 with the model alone and 0 of 69,297 behind the layer, and 105 against 0 on 68,970 matched model, prompt and track triples. The zero spans 790 source sessions, giving a per-session upper bound of 0.38%. It was not obtained by refusing payments: 25,370 payments executed behind the layer, while the policy denied 187 of the 25,640 transfer calls it evaluated, 148 of them for a forbidden recipient.
  We release the 225,964 evaluations, the level passports, the scoring code and the analysis script at huggingface.co/datasets/aporthq/vault-benchmark-v1 .

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [A Multi-Engine Dataflow for MoE Decoding on Scratchpad-Based Tensor Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21137)
*Bin Ma,Wenjie Fan,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CARDAN，将专家权重矩阵分解为向量量化分量+共享基低秩分量，并与多引擎解码数据流协同设计，在Trainium3上实现MoE解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于scratchpad的Tensor加速器（STA）上进行MoE解码时，瓶颈在于专家权重的搬运，而计算引擎处于空闲状态。该流量难以隐藏，因为专家只有在路由后才能确定；也难以压缩，因为压缩会损失质量或引入关键路径工作。

Method: CARDAN将每个专家权重矩阵表示为向量量化（vector-quantized）分量与共享基（shared-basis）低秩分量之和，并将该表示与多引擎解码数据流进行协同设计。该表示将专家共有（expert-common）与专家私有（expert-private）工作分离，使数据流能够在多个引擎上实现DMA与计算的overlap。

Result: 在AWS Trainium3上，针对五个MoE模型家族：CARDAN在全部五个模型上达到或优于BF16-teacher的perplexity；batch size为1的解码相比AWS dense MoE megakernel加速1.15–1.31倍，batch size为16时加速达1.7倍。

Conclusion: 通过权重表示与解码数据流的协同设计，CARDAN在保持模型质量的同时有效缓解了STA上MoE解码的权重搬运瓶颈，实现显著加速。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoding on scratchpad-based tensor accelerators (STA) is dominated by moving expert weights while the compute engines sit idle. This traffic is hard to hide, because the experts are known only after routing, and hard to shrink without losing quality or adding critical-path work. We present CARDAN, which represents each expert-weight matrix as a vector-quantized component plus a shared-basis low-rank component and co-designs this representation with a multi-engine decoding dataflow. The representation separates expert-common from expert-private work, so the dataflow overlaps DMA with computation on several engines. Across five MoE families on AWS Trainium3, CARDAN matches or improves BF16-teacher perplexity across all five models and speeds up batch-one decoding by 1.15-1.31x over AWS dense MoE megakernels, rising to 1.7x at batch size 16.

</details>


### [224] [Programming AMD XDNA NPUs with Open-source Compiler Tools: A FlashAttention Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21264)
*Erwei Wang,Ephrem Wu,Victor J. B. Jung,Jiajie Li,Andre Rosti,Joseph Melber,Samuel Bayliss*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文将 FlashAttention 映射到 AMD XDNA 空间型 NPU，比较四种参考设计，发现将三阶段注意力的 QK^T 分数留在计算瓦片本地内存、经级联互连归约的全融合内核在 XDNA 2 上达到 3.62 TFLOP/s 端到端性能，是 IRON 设计的 2 倍，并在 2K 及以上 token 时实现同芯片集成 GPU 5.3–7.2 倍的能效。作者用每一级内存的 Roofline 分析解释该结果并给出"融合到越过 ridge point 为止"的映射准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间型 NPU（如 AMD XDNA）把计算瓦片紧邻小型本地内存放置，数据在两者间的移动交由软件负责。因此把多阶段工作负载映射到这类设备的核心问题是决定中间张量存放在哪里。作者以此为动机，针对 FlashAttention 研究这类存储布局选择的影响，并希望给出可预先判断的映射准则。

Method: 作者基于开源 IRON 与 MLIR-AIR 流程，在 XDNA 1 和 XDNA 2 上对比四种参考设计：逐算子独立执行的版本、两种片上算子间流式版本、以及把注意力的三个阶段融合为单一内核的版本。融合内核将 QK^T 分数保存在计算瓦片本地内存，并通过级联互连归约部分结果，使其不回写共享 MemTile 内存。作者还对每一级内存进行 Roofline 分析，把映射的运算强度与各级 ridge point 相比较。

Result: 在 XDNA 2 上，融合内核在完整端到端执行中达到 3.62 TFLOP/s，是 IRON 设计的 2 倍；在 2K tokens 及以上时能效为同芯片集成 GPU 的 5.3–7.2 倍。评测覆盖从 BERT 到 DeepSeek 的十二种 LLM 配置，序列长度最高 128K tokens。Roofline 分析显示 XDNA 1 的 ridge point 更低，片上流式已能进入计算受限区间，因此在 XDNA 2 上使吞吐翻倍的相同融合在 XDNA 1 上几乎无收益。

Conclusion: 融合的价值取决于目标平台的 ridge point：用映射的运算强度与各级内存 ridge point 对比，可在写代码前预判哪种情况成立。经验法则是"融合到映射越过该 ridge point 即停止"。作者将参考设计作为持续维护的开源项目发布。

Abstract: Spatial NPUs such as AMD XDNA place compute tiles beside small local memories and leave data movement between them to software. Mapping a multi-stage workload onto such a device is largely a question of where the intermediate tensors live. We report what we learned making those choices for FlashAttention with the open-source IRON and MLIR-AIR flows.
  We compare four reference designs on XDNA 1 and XDNA 2: one runs each operator separately, two stream between operators on chip, and one fuses all three attention stages into a single kernel. The fused kernel holds the $\boldsymbol{QK}^{\mathsf T}$ scores in compute-tile local memory and reduces partial results over the cascade interconnect, so the scores never return to shared MemTile memory. On XDNA 2, it reaches 3.62 TFLOP/s over complete end-to-end execution, twice the IRON design, with 5.3 to 7.2 times the energy efficiency of the integrated GPU on the same chip at 2K tokens and above. It covers twelve LLM configurations, from BERT to DeepSeek, up to 128K tokens.
  Roofline analysis at each memory level explains this result and shows when to stop. XDNA 1 has lower ridge points, so streaming on chip already reaches the compute-bound regime: the same fusion that doubles throughput on XDNA 2 is nearly wasted on XDNA 1. Comparing a mapping's operational intensity against each level's ridge point predicts which case applies before writing any code. Fuse until the mapping clears that ridge point, then stop. We release the reference designs as maintained open source.

</details>


### [225] [Integrating Approximate Logic Synthesis into Approximate High-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21697)
*Jian Shi,Ruicheng Dai,Chang Meng,Yue Yang,Weikang Qian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将近似逻辑综合（ALS）集成到近似高层次综合（HLS）流程中，构建统一框架，扩展设计空间以生成更高质量的近似电路，在相同误差约束下平均降低11%硬件开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似HLS与近似逻辑综合（ALS）工作在不同粒度：前者在控制数据流图的指令层修改，后者在门级网表修改门与互连。二者缺乏统一框架，无法联合优化，限制了近似电路的质量提升潜力。

Method: 将ALS集成进近似HLS的流程中，形成跨粒度的统一近似设计框架；借助ALS引入细粒度近似，从而扩展近似HLS的设计空间。

Result: 实验结果表明，在相同误差界（error bound）约束下，所提方法相较当前最优方法平均降低11%的硬件成本。

Conclusion: 跨粒度融合近似HLS与ALS可有效拓展设计空间并生成更优的近似电路，验证了统一框架在硬件开销优化上的有效性。

Abstract: Approximate high-level synthesis (HLS) and approximate logic synthesis (ALS) are two techniques for generating approximate circuits. They operate at different granularities. Approximate HLS typically modifies instructions in a control and data flow graph, whereas ALS modifies gates and interconnects in a gate-level netlist. The absence of a unified framework combining these techniques limits the potential for joint optimization. To bridge this gap, we propose to integrate ALS into the flow of approximate HLS. This integration expands the design space of approximate HLS by introducing fine-grained approximation induced by ALS, thereby generating approximate circuits with higher quality. Experimental results show that under the same error bound, our method reduces the hardware cost by 11% on average compared to the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [226] [Scalable Packet Tracking on FPGAs for Erasure-Coded RDMA over Lossy WANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21774)
*Yicheng Qian,Konstantin Taranov,Yevgeny Yankilevich,Assaf Shacham,Mahmoud Elhaddad,Abdul Kabbani,Miriam Leeser,Nadeen Gebara*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出COMET，首个面向有损WAN多路径RDMA的、基于FPGA NIC的全硬件卸载数据包到达追踪设计，可支撑400 Gbps以上线速运行，并显著提升并发连接数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI负载需要跨多数据中心互联形成"AI工厂"以突破单站点供电与散热限制，但将RDMA扩展到广域网会带来多路径乱序、高时延和丢包等根本性挑战，严重降低性能。纠删码（EC）虽是有前景的丢包恢复机制，但其效果关键依赖于硬件实现的、高效的数据包到达追踪。

Method: COMET采用可扩展的基于缓存的架构，在FPGA网卡（NIC）上实现全硬件卸载的多路径数据包到达追踪（Packet-arrival Tracking），支持高速链路速率运行；其缓存式架构将片上内存占用与链路BDP解耦。

Result: COMET在400 Gbps及以上速率维持线速运行；片上内存占用与链路BDP解耦；相比SOTA的SoC方案，其缓存式架构（COMET Cache）可支持6倍以上的并发连接数。

Conclusion: 可扩展、全硬件卸载的数据包到达追踪在FPGA网卡上于当前数据速率下是可行的，且其架构可扩展性可延伸至新兴的1.6 Tbps网卡及更高速率。

Abstract: Modern AI workloads increasingly rely on scale across architectures that interconnect multiple datacenters to form a single "AI factory", overcoming the power and cooling constraints of individual sites. However, extending Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) across wide area networks (WANs) introduces fundamental challenges: multi-path packet reordering, high latency, and packet loss that severely degrade performance. While erasure coding (EC) has emerged as a promising mechanism for loss recovery, its effectiveness critically depends on efficient packet arrival tracking implemented in hardware. We present COmpact Multi-path Erasure-coded Tracking (COMET), the first fully hardware-offloaded packet-arrival tracking design implemented on an FPGA-based network interface card (NIC) for multi-path RDMA over lossy WANs. COMET employs a scalable cache-based architecture that supports operation at high link rates. Our evaluation shows that COMET sustains line rate operation at 400 Gbps and beyond. Critically, COMET decouples on-chip memory footprint from link Bandwidth-Delay Product (BDP), and its cache-based architecture (COMET Cache) enables supporting 6 times more concurrent connections than state-of-the-art (SOTA) SoC-based designs. These results demonstrate that scalable, fully hardware-offloaded packet-arrival tracking is practical on FPGA-based NICs at current data rates, and its architectural scalability extends to emerging 1.6 Tbps NICs and beyond.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [BrainWideBench: Benchmarking large-scale pretraining and across-animal transfer in multi-region neural recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22064)
*Alexandre Andre,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Vinam Arora,Keshav Balaji,Divyansha Lachi,Nanda H. Krishna,Jingyun Xiao,Yizi Zhang,Ximeng Mao,Wenrui Ma,Han Yu,International Brain Laboratory,Daniel Birman,Niccolò Bonacchi,Gaelle A. Chapuis,Joana A. Catarino,Felicia Davatolhagh,Mayo Faulkner,Laura Freitas-Silva,Fei Hu,Julia M. Huntenburg,Anup Khanal,Inês Laranjeira,Petrina Lau,Guido T. Meijer,Nathaniel J. Miska,Jean-Paul Noel,Alejandro Pan-Vazquez,Georg Raiser,Cyrille Rossant,Karolina Z. Socha,Anne E. Urai,Miles J. Wells,Steven J. West,Olivier Winter,Blake Richards,Guillaume Lajoie,Cole Hurwitz,Mehdi Azabou,Matthew R. Whiteway,Liam Paninski,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BrainWideBench，一个基于 IBL Brainwide Map 数据（139 只小鼠、276 个脑区）的跨动物神经表征迁移评测基准，包含行为解码、神经活动预测与解剖组织恢复三个任务套件，系统评测预训练方法在微调和零样本迁移下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经记录技术已可跨多动物、多脑区采集数据，理论上可借此学习可跨任务迁移的通用神经表征；但该目标进展受限，原因在于评测协议碎片化，且研究多局限于单一任务域，缺乏统一、可复现的跨动物迁移评估框架。

Method: 构建 BrainWideBench 基准，基于 International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map 数据集（139 只小鼠执行感觉引导决策任务，覆盖 276 个脑区）。基准由三个互补任务套件组成：1）下游行为解码；2）掩码或未来神经活动预测；3）生物解剖组织结构的恢复。在此基础上系统评测多种预训练方法，涵盖下游目标微调与对未见动物的零样本泛化两类迁移设置，并与匹配的单会话基线对比。

Result: 预训练相比匹配的单会话基线确有性能提升；但现有方法在迁移能力上表现出异质性——增益高度依赖预训练目标与下游任务之间的对齐程度。没有任何单一方法在三个套件上均表现一致优异，且多数方法仅针对其中部分任务设计。

Conclusion: 学习能同时在行为、动力学与解剖结构上泛化的表征仍是未解难题。BrainWideBench 通过提供统一、可复现的评测套件，为衡量小鼠大脑通用模型进展建立了框架。

Abstract: Advances in large-scale neural recording have made it possible to collect data across many animals and distributed brain regions, raising the question of whether this scale can be exploited to learn general-purpose neural representations transferable across diverse downstream tasks. Yet, progress toward this goal has been limited by fragmented evaluation protocols and a narrow focus on individual task domains. Here, we present BrainWideBench, a benchmark for evaluating across-animal transfer on multi-region neural recordings, built on the International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map dataset of neural and behavioral recordings spanning 276 brain regions from 139 mice performing a sensory-guided decision-making task. The benchmark is organized around three complementary task suites that evaluate whether learned representations support downstream decoding of behavior, can predict masked or future neural activity, and can recover biologically meaningful anatomical organization. With this benchmark, we systematically evaluate pretraining methods across transfer settings, including finetuning on downstream objectives and zero-shot generalization to unseen animals. Our results confirm pretraining improves performance over matched single-session baselines, but we show current methods exhibit heterogeneity in transfer capabilities: gains depend strongly on the alignment between pretraining objectives and downstream tasks. No single approach performs uniformly well across all three suites, and most methods are designed to only address a subset of them. Together, these findings suggest that learning representations that jointly generalize across behavior, dynamics, and anatomy remains an open challenge. By providing a unified and reproducible evaluation suite, BrainWideBench establishes a framework for measuring progress toward general-purpose models of the mouse brain.

</details>


### [228] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战生成式AI中分布学习的悲观理论界，提出"稀疏先验"类与"稀疏维度k"概念，证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并在温和假设下对TV距离给出匹配上界，从而在n的依赖上克服维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI虽广泛成功，但从n个样本学习d维支撑分布的保证以O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})退化，受维数灾难影响严重。作者认为这些界过于悲观，因为仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布的真正结构，需要引入更能反映现实结构的先验假设。

Method: 1) 引入"稀疏先验"（sparse priors）类，并定义"稀疏维度"（Sparse Dimension）k，作为所有分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；2) 在该先验下推导分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界；3) 在温和附加假设下对TV距离给出匹配上界；4) 证明贝叶斯设定下分布学习与学习采样（learning to sample）的统计等价性。

Result: 在k-稀疏先验下，常见距离度量中分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))；在温和附加假设下，对TV距离给出了渐近匹配（至多相差对数项，关于n、k）的上界。k仍可依赖于维度d或某种内在维度概念。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在合适的先验下学习可以克服关于n依赖的维数灾难；分布学习与学习采样在贝叶斯框架下统计等价，因此结论同样适用于学习采样。这为生成式AI提供了更乐观且与实际结构相符的理论保证。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [229] [Reliability-Centered Evaluation of Sparse Longitudinal CT Lesion-Size Forecasting with Conformal Interval Calibration and Gompertz-Inspired Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21197)
*Lingfei Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了DeepLesion/DLT来源的5次随访同一病灶轨迹基准（129例患者、205条轨迹），在固定预测时域下比较稀疏历史观测对病灶尺寸预测的影响，并联合评估点预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏纵向CT随访中先前观测极少，限制了病灶尺寸预测；且现有评估多只关注点预测精度，忽视不确定性可靠性、校准与轨迹一致性之间的权衡。

Method: 从DeepLesion与Deep Lesion Tracker构建五访视同一病灶轨迹基准；以固定访视索引时域为主设计（由T3预测T4的对数变化），逐步加入更早观测（m=1~4）；比较Cohort-Level Feature GP、MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble、残差尺度区间等方法；开展事后保形区间校准、患者级分组开发交叉验证、亚组分析与Gompertz启发式轨迹正则化（选择lambda*）。

Result: 十个训练种子下平均留出RMSE为0.4726、0.4305、0.4499、0.4513（对应m=1~4），m=2最低，增加历史未改善RMSE；m=4时Cohort-Level Feature GP原始覆盖率接近95%名义水平，而MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble与残差尺度区间偏保守；患者级保形校准总体接近名义或偏保守，但区间更宽；患者分组交叉验证选出lambda*=0；全局群体参考常与病灶级变化方向相反；预测难度随解剖亚组变化。

Conclusion: 在控制预测时域后，额外历史观测的预测收益有限；预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性并不必然同步改善，稀疏纵向影像研究应联合评估三者。

Abstract: Sparse longitudinal CT follow-up limits lesion-size forecasting when only a few prior observations are available. We constructed a five-visit DLT-derived same-lesion trajectory benchmark from DeepLesion and Deep Lesion Tracker (DLT), yielding 205 trajectories from 129 patients. We compared an exploratory conventional sparse-to-final analysis with a primary fixed visit-index horizon design predicting the common log change from T3 to T4 while progressively adding earlier observations, evaluating predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, post-hoc conformal interval calibration, subgroup performance, and Gompertz-inspired trajectory regularization. The evaluated methods showed partially overlapping point-prediction accuracy but distinct uncertainty behavior. Mean held-out RMSE across ten training seeds was 0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, and 0.4513 for m = 1, 2, 3, 4, indicating the lowest mean RMSE at m = 2; additional history did not improve RMSE. At m = 4, raw Cohort-Level Feature GP coverage was near the 95% nominal level, whereas MC Dropout, Deep Ensemble, and residual-scale intervals were conservative. Patient-level conformal calibration generally produced near-nominal or conservative coverage at the cost of wider intervals. Patient-grouped development cross-validation selected lambda* = 0 for the Gompertz-inspired term. A global population reference frequently opposed lesion-level change directions, and prediction difficulty varied across anatomical subgroups. Overall, additional historical observations provided limited predictive benefit once the prediction horizon was controlled, while predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, and trajectory consistency did not necessarily improve together, and should be evaluated jointly in sparse longitudinal imaging.

</details>


### [230] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端商业智能(BI)评测基准 BI-Bench，发现前沿大模型准确率不足50%，进而设计工具增强的 BI-Agent 并配合领域后训练，实现最高40个百分点提升（后训练版本再增30点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流需用户手动完成识别相关表、数据变换、构建连接等繁琐准备工作，才能回答业务问题，过程复杂耗时；而LLM在数据处理上展现出较强能力，因此有必要系统评估其端到端完成BI任务的能力。

Method: 从公开来源采集真实BI项目，从用户仪表板中人工抽取(问题, 真值答案)对，构建BI-Bench基准；设计工具增强的BI-Agent，将BI流程分解为结构化数据上的搜索、连接、变换等子任务，并编排专门的数据管理方法；提出后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，结合SFT与RL对BI-Agent进行微调。

Result: 前沿LLM在BI-Bench上准确率不足50%；BI-Agent相较原始LLM提升最高达40个百分点，经后训练的BI-Agent再额外提升最高30个百分点。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中，将工具增强推理与领域特定后训练相结合至关重要，为该方向后续研究提供了有前景的思路。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [231] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可端到端训练的弹性阈值注意力（ETA），通过从查询表示中预测动态上下文阈值，实现高效稀疏注意力解码，在不损失密集模型质量的前提下获得硬件加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 KV 缓存会在长上下文解码中造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法依赖刚性启发式规则进行选择性加载，会丢弃必要上下文，导致质量下降。因此需要一种既能硬件加速解码、又不牺牲密集模型质量的方法。

Method: ETA 从查询表示直接预测动态上下文阈值，使模型能在困难检索或推理步骤分配类密集上下文、对常规 token 剪枝。训练时对低于阈值的 logits 进行乘法抑制而非直接删除，避免表示坍缩；通过平滑均匀注意力底座形成分布式概率储备，消除初始 token 上的局部注意力汇，并支持推理时硬剪枝无信息 KV 块、吸收粗粒度 GPU 块选择误纳入的 token。推理时用 Triton 实现定制解码内核，利用缓存的几何-概率界以 O(1) 时间筛选 KV 块。此外引入离线校准算法冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B 预训练 ETA 模型在约 85% 训练稀疏度和约 38% 活跃解码密度下，在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文针检索上媲美密集注意力。定制 Triton 解码内核在最长 512K token 序列上相比 FlashAttention-2 实现最高 2.5 倍墙钟解码加速。离线校准算法进一步减少 27% 注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA 通过可学习的动态阈值与乘法抑制训练机制，实现了兼顾密集模型质量与硬件加速解码的稀疏注意力方案，并通过定制内核和离线校准进一步提升部署效率。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [232] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bio-MF，一种无潜空间、单步 MeanFlow 框架，用于 EEG 条件生成 fNIRS 信号，实现快速且高质量的跨模态合成，以支撑混合 MI-BCI 解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-fNIRS 混合 MI-BCI 优于纯 EEG 系统，但在无法或不方便同步采集 fNIRS 时，需要 EEG-to-fNIRS 跨模态生成。现有方法生成速度慢、常需预训练，难以满足实时 MI-BCI；而单步生成模型虽降低延迟，却可能降低生成保真度并引入非生理伪影。

Method: Bio-MF 采用无潜空间的一步 MeanFlow 框架：直接在信号空间进行 x-prediction，并将其转化为 MeanFlow 速度监督，推理仅需一次网络评估。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，模型融合时空交互 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导以及噪声水平门控 FFT 正则化。

Result: 数据集 1 上，EEG+合成 fNIRS 相比纯 EEG，HbR 与 HbO 的 ACC 分别提升 3.37 和 4.15 个百分点；数据集 2 在未见过的 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇下，提升分别为 2.98 和 2.50 个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上，生成单个 fNIRS 试次仅需 7.0 ms，相比 1000 步 SCDM 延迟实现 857 倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 能够实现快速的 EEG-to-fNIRS 合成，并在下游混合 MI 解码中保持任务相关的生成质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [233] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了将随机微分方程（SDE）与神经网络参数化结合以建模类星体光变曲线的历史，并提出Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD（连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降）及一种连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，在二维景观仿真中展现出更宽的探索性与更精确的收敛性，其最优性条件可还原出Gibbs策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体光变曲线蕴含超大质量黑洞信息，但基于地面巡天数据的随机亮度波动建模是统计难题，需要借助SDE与神经网络参数化来加以刻画。

Method: 1）综述SDE与神经网络参数化结合的历史方法；2）提出Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD，使迭代依赖于离散迭代过程的过去状态；3）在二维景观上进行仿真，通过调节超参数与Vanilla SGD对比；4）提出无需求解HJB偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构用于探索性策略。

Result: Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD相比Vanilla SGD表现出更宽的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；所提连续时间策略梯度结构的最优性条件可还原出既有工作中的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: SDE与神经网络参数化结合是应对类星体光变曲线建模挑战的有效途径；引入延迟记忆的连续时间优化与策略梯度框架在探索与收敛上更具优势，且与经典Gibbs策略保持理论一致性。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [234] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RydbergGPT（基于相互作用里德堡原子阵列量子比特投影测量数据训练的自回归Transformer）的神经标度律，发现临界点附近损失随数据量呈带损失下限修正的幂律，远离临界时幂律描述变差；通过熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息两点函数比较，发现近临界统计最接近自然语言，支持多尺度依赖性促成稳定神经标度、标度行为应视为模型-数据对属性的假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经标度律（随数据量、模型规模变化的幂律）在语言模型中已被广泛观察，但其是否以及在何种条件下适用于量子多体测量数据尚不清楚。作者希望探究：训练数据（量子测量结果）的物理统计结构如何影响标度行为，尤其关注量子临界点这一多尺度关联显著的区域是否会产生类似自然语言的稳定标度行为。

Method: 1. 训练RydbergGPT：在相互作用里德堡原子阵列的量子比特投影测量数据上训练自回归Transformer，数据集涵盖不同激光失谐参数（跨越有限尺寸临界点）；2. 标度律拟合：以训练数据集大小为自变量，拟合损失函数，比较临界点附近与远离临界点的幂律+损失下限修正模型；3. 统计结构比较：构造熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息"两点函数"，量化Rydberg测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构，并比较临界与非临界配置下该两点函数的衰减行为。

Result: 临界点附近，Transformer损失随训练数据量的变化可由带损失下限修正的幂律很好地描述；远离临界点时，幂律描述的拟合质量显著下降。两点函数分析显示：近临界统计下两点函数最接近自然语言语料，而远离临界的其他量子比特配置中两点函数衰减更快（多尺度关联更弱）。

Conclusion: 多尺度依赖性（multi-scale dependence）是稳定神经标度律的重要促成因素；标度行为不应仅视为模型属性，而应视为模型-数据对的联合属性。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [235] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI审稿进入递归训练回路后的风险：用官方审稿微调的审稿模型再以官方+模型生成审稿混合数据训练后继模型，会压缩评分分布、降低语义多样性（称"科学判断坍缩"），并提出两阶段干预系统TrustReviewer加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益参与科学评审，其生成的审稿将进入公开数据与未来训练语料，使AI同行评审可能递归化——后续审稿者会学习先前模型的判断。作者希望在受控条件下刻画这一反馈回路中单步迭代所带来的退化风险。

Method: 以Llama 3.1 8B为起点，先在2018–2023年ICLR官方审稿上微调出审稿模型，再用ICLR 2024数据、以系统变化的官方审稿与模型生成审稿混合比例训练四个后继模型，以观察合成审稿的影响。缓解方案TrustReviewer包含两个互补阶段：训练时预防——在精选语料上单阶段训练核心审稿器，减少低质量与语义退化监督；测试时校正——采用配对激活引导（paired activation steering），在无需额外训练与专家标注的情况下削弱残余的坍缩判断倾向。

Result: 引入合成审稿会压缩评分分布，并降低同篇论文层面与语料库层面的语义多样性，作者将这一模式命名为"科学判断坍缩"（scientific-judgment collapse）。TrustReviewer的两阶段干预可缓解该残余倾向，有助于保持判断多样性并改善推荐对齐。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了递归审稿器训练的具体风险，说明合成审稿进入训练回路会导致判断趋同与多样性丧失，并提供训练时与测试时结合的实用干预手段，以在AI辅助科学评审中保留判断多样性并提升推荐一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [236] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端、基于模型的强化学习算法，在未知环境中针对给定LTL规约（如安全、可达性）高效合成策略。将LTL任务的LDBA与环境的BAMDP同步，通过贝叶斯RL实现探索-利用权衡，并提出新的BAMCP算法在同步BAMDP上进行近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。实验验证其在任务满足与样本效率上的优势，并可减少训练中的违规次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中满足LTL规约（安全、可达性等）的策略合成需要兼顾探索-利用权衡，传统非贝叶斯或无模型方法在样本效率和规约满足上存在不足。作者希望利用贝叶斯RL的探索-利用优势，实现高效且近似贝叶斯最优的策略合成。

Method: 将LTL任务的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）与环境的贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）表示进行同步，形成同步BAMDP结构；在此基础上提出新的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，用于在同步BAMDP中进行近似贝叶斯最优的策略合成。

Result: 在多种有限与无限时域任务实验中，该方法在规约满足和样本效率上优于传统无模型方法；消融研究显示新BAMCP算法在LTL任务满足上优于经典BAMCP；并成功应用于“谨慎”RL，减少策略训练过程中的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: LDBA与BAMDP的同步结合贝叶斯RL，可实现未知环境下LTL规约的高效策略合成，新BAMCP算法在满足性与样本效率上有效，并可支持减少训练违规的谨慎RL应用。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [237] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化的简单框架，通过构造无偏辅助随机序列，统一导出强凸、指数凹及一般凸损失下的最优动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾是衡量学习器相对时变比较器序列表现的重要指标。然而，针对强凸和指数凹损失获得最优界通常需要复杂精细的分析，缺乏统一且简洁的推导途径。

Method: 提出归约框架：对任意比较器序列构造逐轮无偏、方差受控且切换次数可控的辅助随机序列；结合合适的代理损失，将动态遗憾分解为对该随机序列的期望切换遗憾与其受控方差两部分，从而借助现成的具有切换遗憾保证的算法导出动态遗憾界。

Result: 对强凸和指数凹损失，建立了 Õ(T^{1/3} P_T^{2/3}) 的动态遗憾界；对一般凸损失，同一归约也恢复了 O(√(T(1+P_T))) 的动态遗憾界。其中 T 为时间跨度，P_T 为比较器序列的路径长度。

Conclusion: 所提出的归约框架简洁且通用，三类损失下的结果均与各自的极小极大最优界相匹配，凸显了框架的普适性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [238] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将竞争性地质解释转化为空间先验并用观测水头对其进行排序的工作流，结合文生图基础模型、VAE与反演网络，算例与WIPP案例验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高后果地下决策常在数据稀缺条件下进行，专家对同一地下系统可能给出相互竞争的解释，早期缺乏实用手段判断哪种解释最符合实际，往往需钻多口井才能澄清，成本高昂。现有评估方法依赖主观判断或早期难以获得的密集数据。

Method: 将不同地质解释转化为备选空间先验；对每种解释用文生图基础模型生成1600幅地质图像集合，并训练各自的变分自编码器（VAE）以获得解释专属的潜表征；用监督反演网络将水头观测映射到该潜空间，由冻结解码器生成图像并转为对数渗透率场；进行稳态流动模拟得到预测水头，将失配转换为高斯形式的兼容性得分并据此排序。

Result: 在基于Johansen组的合成基准上，对三种一致性递减的解释进行925个测试案例评估，平均水头RMSE由"精确且准确"解释的0.197增至"准确"的0.227和"不匹配"的0.280。应用于WIPP库莱布拉白云岩段两个已发表概念模型，修正模型的兼容性权重为0.991，原模型为0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流可在数据稀缺的早期阶段，以水力水头观测为客观依据对竞争性地质解释进行定量排序，合成基准与真实场地案例均表明其能正确区分解释优劣，为高后果地下决策提供可操作的解释筛选工具。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [239] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASGARD，一种两阶段师生（teacher-student）流水线，使基于强化学习的无人机控制策略对动作空间攻击具有弹性。教师阶段利用包含特权信息的编码器训练控制策略与监控器；学生阶段通过监督学习蒸馏出仅依赖物理状态历史的机载模型。实验表明ASGARD能在攻击下完成任务、泛化到未见攻击并抵御隐蔽攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已被用于无人机导航与控制，但现有防御多针对策略输入攻击；针对动作空间攻击（在策略输出动作后、执行器执行前被篡改）的防御通常只能在训练时重训练策略，缺乏运行时的弹性。

Method: ASGARD采用两阶段师生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息结合，生成攻击感知隐表示，用于训练RL控制策略和一个监控器，监控器向执行器输出修正后的动作指令。学生阶段：编码器与监控器通过监督学习从教师模型蒸馏，仅使用无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: 在针对无人机不同动作指令的攻击场景中评估，ASGARD对动作空间攻击具有弹性，能在攻击下完成任务；并可泛化到未见攻击，且对隐蔽攻击仍保持弹性。

Conclusion: ASGARD通过师生蒸馏将攻击感知能力迁移到仅依赖物理状态的机载模型，为RL无人机控制提供了运行时可用的动作空间攻击防御方案，兼具任务完成能力、泛化性和隐蔽攻击鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [240] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在参与概率未知时FedAvg实际优化的目标，并提出FedeRage——一种基于CVaR的分布式鲁棒风险规避扩展，仅增加每个客户端一个标量，保持对参与分布的不可知性，在收敛速率与实验上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID数据和随机客户端参与下性能下降。基于经典FedAvg的补救方法通常假设服务器已知客户端参与概率，而实际部署中这一信息极少可得，因此需要研究并解决参与完全未知情形下的聚合与优化问题。

Method: 首先刻画在参与未知、可能高度倾斜且各轮规模可变时"分布不可知"FedAvg所求解的优化问题，证明均匀聚合最小化一个由参与诱导边际加权的随机目标；随后在分布式鲁棒优化(DRO)框架下将条件风险价值(CVaR)嵌入本地目标，提出Federated Risk-Averse Averaging(FedeRage)，隐式地提升高损失和低参与频率客户端的权重，且仅需每个客户端增加一个标量。

Result: 均匀聚合在凸、可能非光滑损失下达到标准O(1/√T)速率；FedeRage获得O(κ/√T)速率，其中κ为风险厌恶"代价"的上界。在三个异构基准上，FedeRage在准确率、公平性和收敛速度上持续优于最先进方法，且无需以参与分布作为输入，优于基于最优传输的聚合对齐方案。

Conclusion: FedeRage在参与分布完全未知的情况下实现了风险规避的联邦聚合，兼顾理论收敛保证与对参与分布的不可知性，并在异构场景中同时提升准确率、公平性与收敛速度。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(κ/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $κ$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [241] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对WESAD与EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上的时序深度学习模型（Bi-LSTM、TCN、Transformer）进行系统性对比，发现最优架构依赖数据集特性，多模态传感优于单一配置，4 Hz为实用采样频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理情感识别研究通常只评估单一模型、传感配置或数据集，难以理解这些因素如何共同影响识别性能，缺少跨架构、跨配置、跨数据集的系统性对比证据。

Method: 在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上，对比双向LSTM、时序卷积网络（TCN）与Transformer三种时序深度模型；设置腕部单模态、胸部单模态与多模态三种传感配置；采用被试独立的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV）；并额外考察软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率与基于梯度的显著性分析。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态上取得最高准确率（99.02% ± 0.51%）；LSTM在EmoWear上取得最佳多模态准确率，唤醒为91.80% ± 1.06%，效价为89.96% ± 0.36%。多模态传感在两个数据集上均持续优于腕部或胸部单模态配置。采样频率分析表明4 Hz是实用工作点，性能与更高频率相当而训练成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 相对架构性能取决于数据集特性，不存在普遍最优的单一架构；多模态传感与适度采样（4 Hz）是更稳健的工程选择，为可穿戴生理情感识别的架构、模态与采样频率选择提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [242] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SR 用预训练 Transformer 生成多个初始草图，作为搜索起点，结合尺度感知常数优化与局部符号修复，在多数据集上取得最高符号解恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归需从观测中恢复闭式方程以获得可解释模型，但现有方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理：基于搜索的方法结构可精修，却依赖昂贵的组合优化与随机初始化；预训练神经模型可近乎即时生成公式，但预测常含符号错误。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 先由预训练 Transformer 提出多个初始草图，用其初始化若干有前景区域的搜索，避免在庞大表达式空间中随机起步；随后每个搜索通过尺度感知的常数优化与局部符号修复，联合恢复表达式结构与常数。

Result: 在含与不含 dummy 变量的 SRSD-Feynman 数据集及六个额外基准上评估，MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上取得最高符号解恢复率，同时预测精度排名进入前二；在存在无关 dummy 输入时该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习到的先验可将搜索聚焦于有前景的方程结构，而数值优化与符号修复对表达式恢复至关重要。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [243] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MCR²（最大编码率约简）在分布偏移下的OOD泛化局限：其目标本身可容许完全预测失败，且直接引入IRM/REx的不变性原则也无法消除该失败，可靠OOD保证需要新假设或新学习原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR²作为信息论框架可学习结构化、判别性的类子流形表示，并催生了可解释的白盒架构，旨在提升安全性与泛化性。但作者观察到MCR²在分布偏移下会完全失效，因此需要研究其OOD泛化极限。

Method: 通过构造反例（exact-optimum example）与理论分析：构造一个完全依赖不稳定环境特征的表示，它可达到全局编码最优，但在相关性反转后预测完全失败，即使存在完美稳定特征；进一步分析将IRM/REx背后的不变性原则直接纳入MCR²的效果。

Result: 证明MCR²的两点局限：(1) 仅靠MCR²目标可容许完全预测失败，该精确最优例中包含训练中不可能出现的测试输入；即便所有测试输入都可能在训练中出现，编码质量可任意接近最优而预测错误率任意接近100%。(2) 直接纳入IRM/REx的不变性原则无法消除该失败，失败表示在多个训练环境上共享同一最优编码算子，说明共享编码最优性不保证稳定预测。

Conclusion: MCR²要实现可靠的OOD保证，必须引入额外的新假设或学习原则，以建立跨环境的稳定预测关系。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction.
