Method: 在黎曼流形上组合固定动能项、学习标量势与显式测地蛙跳积分器构造哈密顿流；理论分析引入"隐式分布轮廓"与"匹配势"概念，并借助局部调和分析将结果推广到一般目标流形的各模态附近。

Result: 证明可归一化势定义隐式轮廓，位置边缘初始沿隐式轮廓与基分布之间的相对分数加速；各向同性高斯下机制等价于相空间旋转；学习势与匹配势之差等于基分布的残余记忆与模型偏差之和，当位置基分布比目标更宽、可将其转移到动量时二者一致。在欧氏、双曲、球面空间的实验显示采样质量与数值开销可媲美黎曼连续归一化流，并验证了所学势的可解释性。

Conclusion: 黎曼神经哈密顿流为流形生成建模提供了可逆保体积的框架，并首次给出所学哈密顿量的可解释性刻画（隐式轮廓与匹配势），实验验证了其质量、效率与可解释性。

Abstract: Hamiltonian normalizing flows are attractive generative models because their phase-space maps are invertible and volume preserving, but most neural constructions are formulated in Euclidean space. We introduce Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows, which combine the fixed kinetic energy of a Riemannian manifold, a learned scalar potential, and an explicit geodesic leapfrog integrator. Our analysis explains how the learned Hamiltonian can be made interpretable. Every normalizable potential defines an implicit profile, and the position marginal initially accelerates along the relative score between that profile and the base. The matched potential is the interpretable specialization for which the implicit profile is the target. In the isotropic Gaussian case, the mechanism corresponds to a phase-space rotation. A local harmonic analysis extends this result around each mode of a general target on a manifold. The gap between the learned and the matched potential is the sum of a residual memory of the base and a bias of the model, and the two potentials agree when the position base has been transferred to the momentum. This can be achieved when the former is broader than the target. Numerical experiments on Euclidean, hyperbolic, and spherical spaces show competitive sample quality and numerical cost against a Riemannian continuous normalizing flow, and confirm the interpretability of the learned potential.

</details>


### [281] [A Hybrid Computational Intelligence Framework for scRNA-seq Imputation: Integrating scRecover and Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16923)
*Ali Anaissi,Deshao Liu,Yuanzhe Jia,Weidong Huang,Widad Alyassine,Junaid Akram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCR-MF，一种两阶段scRNA-seq数据插补工作流，结合scRecover的dropout检测与missForest的稳健非参数插补。多数据集评估显示其性能在多数情况下不逊于或优于现有方法，兼顾生物学保真度与计算效率，适用于中等规模单细胞数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq可获得细胞分辨率转录组，但普遍存在的dropout（基因表达值丢失为零）会掩盖真实生物学信号，亟需有效的检测与插补方法。

Method: 提出模块化两阶段流程SCR-MF：第一阶段用scRecover进行有原则的dropout检测，第二阶段用missForest进行稳健的非参数插补。

Result: 在公开数据集与模拟数据集上，SCR-MF在多数情况下达到与现有插补方法相当或更优的稳健、可解释性能，同时保持生物学保真度与透明度；运行时间分析显示其在准确性与计算效率间取得有竞争力的平衡。

Conclusion: SCR-MF是一种模块化、可解释且高效的scRNA-seq插补方案，适合中等规模单细胞数据集，能兼顾信号恢复与计算成本。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) enables transcriptomic profiling at cellular resolution but suffers from pervasive dropout events that obscure biological signals. We present SCR-MF, a modular two-stage workflow that combines principled dropout detection using scRecover with robust non-parametric imputation via missForest. Across public and simulated datasets, SCR-MF achieves robust and interpretable performance comparable to or exceeding existing imputation methods in most cases, while preserving biological fidelity and transparency. Runtime analysis demonstrates that SCR-MF provides a competitive balance between accuracy and computational efficiency, making it suitable for mid-scale single-cell datasets.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Gaussian Worlds: Latent Geometry Matters for JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21656)
*Léo Nicollier,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分布匹配式JEPA的可辨识性分析从欧氏空间推广到嵌入黎曼流形，证明球面潜变量与同球面目标分布匹配时最优表示可正交恢复潜状态，从而表明高斯唯一性并非普遍规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klindt等（2026）在欧氏假设下证明：匹配高斯目标可将高斯潜变量线性恢复，且高斯是唯一具此保证的分布。作者质疑这一"高斯唯一性"是否为分布匹配式JEPA的普遍性质，尤其是当潜变量并非欧氏变量、而位于嵌入的黎曼流形上时。

Method: 将分析推广至支撑在嵌入黎曼流形上的潜变量；推导潜几何与正对动力学需满足的条件，使得对齐与精确分布匹配可保证线性恢复；以球面潜空间（潜变量均匀分布于球面、表示匹配同一球面分布）为特例进行分析，并推导近似恢复界；在欧氏（高斯）、球面与环面潜空间上进行实验，并扩展至高维Clifford环面世界。

Result: 当潜变量均匀分布于球面且表示匹配同一球面分布时，每个最优表示都能将潜状态恢复到正交变换以内。所推导的近似恢复界在球面情形下严格优于高斯情形。实验显示：几何相容的目标分布在优化成功时带来更好的线性恢复，而不匹配的目标会扭曲潜结构；该优势在高维Clifford环面世界中依然保持。

Conclusion: 高斯唯一性并非分布匹配式JEPA的普遍属性：非欧氏潜几何可容许其他可线性恢复的分布。潜几何与目标分布的几何相容性是决定线性可辨识性的关键，这为JEPA目标分布的选择提供了几何视角的指导。

Abstract: Recent Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) prevent representation collapse by constraining learned representations to follow a prescribed target distribution, such as an isotropic Gaussian or the uniform distribution on a hypersphere. Klindt et al. (2026) showed that, under their Euclidean assumptions, matching a Gaussian target can recover Gaussian latent variables up to a linear transformation, and that the Gaussian is the unique distribution with this guarantee. We extend their analysis to latent variables supported on embedded Riemannian manifolds and derive conditions on the latent geometry and positive-pair dynamics under which alignment and exact distribution matching guarantee linear recovery. In particular, when the latent variables are uniformly distributed on a sphere and the representations are matched to the same spherical distribution, every optimal representation recovers the latent state up to an orthogonal transformation. This shows that Gaussian uniqueness is not a universal property of distribution-matched JEPAs: non-Euclidean latent geometries can admit other linearly recoverable distributions. We further derive an approximate-recovery bound that is strictly tighter for the spherical world than for the Gaussian world. Experiments on Gaussian, spherical, and toroidal latent spaces show that geometrically compatible targets yield better linear recovery when optimization succeeds, whereas mismatched targets distort the latent structure. This advantage persists in high-dimensional Clifford-torus worlds.

</details>


### [283] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究潜在世界动作模型（WAMs）中"潜在转移实现"这一架构选择，提出基于受控Koopman生成元视角的潜在演化算子网络（LEON），通过上下文调制的算子传播与加性forcing建模潜在演化，在两种WAM框架下提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期WAMs倾向于在潜在表征空间预测任务相关场景状态的演化，以避免完整外观生成并保留控制相关信息。然而这些潜在转移通常由以token交互（而非时间演化）为归纳结构的Transformer预测器实现，转移实现方式本身作为架构选择尚未被独立研究。

Method: 将"转移实现"与"预测表征""预测-策略耦合"区分开来作为独立架构维度。提出Latent Evolution Operator Network（LEON），在学习到的可观空间（observable space）中，通过上下文调制的算子式传播（operator-based propagation）与加性forcing来建模潜在演化。其理论基础为受控Koopman生成元演化视角：将上下文相关的转移变化组织在共享演化算子结构周围，同时保留加性变化的互补路径。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了该演化特异的归纳偏置，以及算子传播与forcing的互补作用；在两种以不同方式将潜在预测融入策略的WAM框架中，LEON均提升闭环性能与鲁棒性，且在完全替换转移模块时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 转移实现是潜在WAMs中一个具有实质影响的架构选择，LEON验证了引入演化特异的归纳偏置可带来性能与鲁棒性收益。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [284] [Multi-Domain Clustering via Measure Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21664)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于测度量化的多域聚类通用框架，通过最小化各域概率测度与聚类原型测度之间的概率距离（Sinkhorn散度或MMD）学习共享原型，并用最近质心或最优传输进行分配，小批量优化保证可扩展性。在5个多域基准上优于经典及多域基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类是数据分析的基础任务，通常用K-means等基于质心的方法解决。现有方法多针对单一域，难以处理来自多个域、各域分布不同的样本，因此需要一个能跨域联合学习共享聚类结构的通用框架。

Method: 给定多个域的样本，学习一组共享的聚类原型，以概率度量（Sinkhorn散度或最大均值差异MMD）度量每个域的概率测度与原型测度之间的差异并最小化；分配阶段采用两种方式：最近质心，或最优传输（域内协作耦合所有样本）；同时引入小批量优化策略以降低内存和计算开销。

Result: 在图像、音频和传感器数据共5个多域基准上的实验表明，基于Sinkhorn的方法持续优于经典聚类和多域聚类基线，且该优势在扩展到数十万样本规模时依然保持。

Conclusion: 基于测度量化的多域聚类框架具有通用性与可扩展性，Sinkhorn散度作为拟合目标是有效选择，小批量优化在保持聚类性能的同时提升了规模适应性。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, typically addressed through centroid-based methods such as K-means. In this work, we present a general framework for multi-domain clustering via measure quantization: given samples from multiple domains, we learn a shared set of cluster prototypes by minimizing a probability metric, such as the Sinkhorn divergence or the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, between each domain's probability measure and the measure of prototypes. Data points are then assigned to clusters either via nearest centroid, or via optimal transport, a collaborative strategy that couples all samples within a domain. A mini-batch optimization strategy makes both fitting and assignment scalable, reducing memory and computational cost while preserving clustering performance. Experimental results on 5 multi-domain benchmarks spanning image, audio and sensor data show that our Sinkhorn-based method consistently outperforms classical and multi-domain clustering baselines, and that this advantage persists when scaling to hundreds of thousands of samples.

</details>


### [285] [Optimization Geometry of Equivalent Brownian RKHS Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21693)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在受控的有限 Brownian RKHS 中，用节点、增量、谱三套坐标参数化同一函数类与同一 Brownian 能量，论文刻画了由此产生的优化几何差异：映射初始化下节点与谱 GD/SGD 轨迹完全一致；增量 GD 是常度量的显式 Euler 步（步长因子 1/h）；Brownian 正则最小二乘中增量 Hessian 条件数 ≤1+A/ρ 且与网格分辨率 G 无关；标准 Adam 的正交等变群恰为带符号置换，块 DCT-VIII 不属于该群；Float64 数值实验验证了上述结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等价的有限参数化可以表示同一函数和同一内禀范数，却诱导不同的优化算法。作者希望在不变动被表示函数、内禀正则项与逼近空间的前提下，隔离并解释这种"坐标效应"的来源。

Method: 构造一个受控的有限 Brownian RKHS 模型，引入节点、增量、谱三套坐标；利用经典有限元、RKHS 插值、Brownian 协方差与混合边界 DCT 恒等式，使共享假设类、Brownian 能量、逼近算子与坐标映射显式化。在此基础上做优化几何分析，并在五套网格上进行 Float64 数值测试，覆盖有限恒等式、映射单层与递归轨迹、条件数预测以及定理匹配的 Adam 分离实验。

Result: 在映射初始化、相同标量步长与相同 minibatch 下，节点与谱 GD/SGD 的映射轨迹完全相同；增量 GD 等价于常 Brownian/Sobolev 度量的显式 Euler 步，带因子 1/h；对 Brownian 正则最小二乘，κ2(H_inc) ≤ 1+A/ρ，在固定 A、ρ>0 与给定归一化下不依赖网格分辨率 G；在标准 Adam 约定下，通用正交等变群恰为带符号置换，块 DCT-VIII 变换不属于其中。数值实验验证了所有预测，包括与定理一致的 Adam 分离。

Conclusion: 该受控模型表明，坐标效应可以在不改变被表示函数、内禀正则项与逼近空间的情况下被干净地隔离和解释，为理解等价参数化下优化算法差异提供了可解析的基准。

Abstract: Equivalent finite parameterizations can represent the same functions and intrinsic norm yet induce different optimization algorithms. We study this effect in a controlled finite Brownian RKHS with nodal, increment, and spectral coordinates. Classical finite-element, RKHS-interpolation, Brownian-covariance, and mixed-boundary DCT identities make the shared hypothesis class, Brownian energy, approximation operator, and coordinate maps explicit. Our main results concern the optimization geometry of this fixed model. With mapped initialization, identical scalar steps, and identical minibatches, nodal and spectral GD/SGD have exactly the same mapped trajectories. Increment GD is an explicit Euler step for the constant Brownian/Sobolev metric, with factor $1/h$. For Brownian-regularized least squares, $κ_2(\mathbf H_{\mathrm{inc}})\le1+A/ρ$, independently of grid resolution $G$ for fixed $A$, $ρ>0$, and the stated normalization. Under the stated standard-Adam convention, the universal orthogonal equivariance group is exactly the signed permutations; the block DCT-VIII transform is not one. Float64 tests over five grids numerically verify the finite identities, mapped one-layer and recursive trajectories, conditioning predictions, and theorem-matched Adam separation. Thus coordinate effects are isolated without changing the represented functions, intrinsic regularizer, or approximation space.

</details>


### [286] [SpecQuant: Speculative Decoding with Multi-Parent Quantization for Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21704)
*Harish KB,Jagadeeswaran M,Pradheep P,Yuvanesh S,Sivakumar T*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecQuant 是一个免训练框架，将多父量化与投机解码结合，对 LLM 进行自适应高效推理，在保持精度损失 <2% 的前提下实现 35-43% 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上本地运行大语言模型受限于算力与内存。主流加速技术（量化、投机解码、自适应推理）虽能显著提速，但通常需要重新训练、按架构调优或依赖草稿模型，部署门槛高。

Method: SpecQuant 从共享基座模型派生多个量化变体（INT4、FP8、FP16），并基于预测的查询复杂度进行动态路由：简单或事实性任务使用轻量变体，复杂推理或长上下文输入使用全精度模型。其共享权重设计保证投机解码有足够的 token 接受率，且避免使用独立草稿父模型带来的兼容性问题。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在基于 Qwen2.5 的模型上、于 MMLU、AlpacaEval 和 GSM8K 数据集/基准上评估，SpecQuant 实现 35-43% 的加速，精度下降不超过 2%。

Conclusion: SpecQuant 使 LLM 能够在多样硬件上实现实用的端侧部署，无需特殊基础设施或专业知识。

Abstract: Running large language models (LLMs) locally continues to be limited by restrictions of compute and memory on consumer hardware. The popular acceleration technologies, such as quantization, speculative decoding, and adaptive inferencing, offer substantial speed boosts but usually necessitate retraining, per architecture tuning, or draft models. SpecQuant is a trainingfree framework, that combines speculative decoding with multiparent quantization to perform adaptive, efficient inference of LLMs. SpecQuant derives multiple quantized variants (INT4, FP8, FP16) from a shared base model, and dynamically routes queries based on predicted complexity; lightweight variants are used for simple or factual tasks, and full-precision models are used for complex reasoning tasks or long-context inputs. The shared-weight design of SpecQuant ensures sufficient token acceptance for speculative decoding without compatibility issues using separate draft parent models. We evaluate SpecQuant on Qwen2.5 based models on the MMLU, AlpacaEval, and GSM8K datasets, or benchmarks, demonstrating 35-43% speedups without degrading accuracy greater than 2%, substantial within the LLM community. SpecQuant enables practical on-device LLM deployment across diverse hardware without special infrastructure or expertise.

</details>


### [287] [GEM-MPC: Balancing Exploration and Exploitation through Expert-Guided Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21735)
*Alvaro Serra-Gomez,Thomas Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GEM-MPC，一种基于 MPPI 的强化学习方法，通过融合克隆规划器的策略与 KL 正则化探索策略，并引入门控先验蒸馏以缓解陈旧规划数据问题，在连续控制任务中以更低算力超越现有规划类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维连续控制中的高效探索仍是核心难题。基于规划的方法将在线规划与学习到的策略和价值函数结合，但其组件在训练中易出现错位：学习到的采样策略可能偏离规划器行为，而回放缓冲区中存储的规划分布在模型与价值函数演化后变得陈旧。重分析虽可刷新目标，但计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出 GEM-MPC：1）以 MPPI 将“克隆规划器的策略”与“围绕其探索的 KL 正则化策略”结合，在规划中同时实现利用与引导式探索；2）引入门控先验蒸馏（Gated Prior Distillation），仅当存储的规划分布比当前先验提供更优目标时才选择性学习，从而在无需完整重分析的情况下降低陈旧规划数据的影响。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准上，GEM-MPC 在更低计算预算下持续优于现有基于规划的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过改进规划与学习之间的交互，GEM-MPC 有效缓解了策略-规划器错位与规划数据陈旧两大问题，在保持低算力的同时提升了连续控制任务的性能。

Abstract: Effective exploration in high-dimensional continuous control remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning. Planning-based methods address this by combining online planning with learned policies and value functions, but their components can become misaligned during training: learned sampling policies may diverge from planner behavior, while planning distributions stored in replay become stale as the model and value function evolve. Reanalysis can refresh these targets, but at substantial computational cost. We propose GEM-MPC, an MPPI-based reinforcement learning method that improves the interaction between planning and learning. GEM-MPC uses MPPI to combine a policy trained to clone the planner with a KL-regularized policy that explores around it, providing complementary exploitation and guided exploration within planning. We further introduce Gated Prior Distillation, which selectively learns from stored planning distributions only when they provide a better target than the current prior, reducing the impact of stale planning data without requiring full reanalysis. Across continuous-control benchmarks, GEM-MPC consistently outperforms existing planning-based baselines under lower computational budgets.

</details>


### [288] [GraphSkillEvo: Evolutionary Optimization of Graph-Structured Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21749)
*Rui Sun,Zhi Zheng,Zhenkun Wang,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的技能表示为图结构的自然语言产物，并设计群体进化优化框架GraphSkillEvo，在五个基准上平均准确率较SkillOpt提升4.01%（GPT-5.4-nano）和1.76%（GPT-5.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法将技能表示为非结构化自然语言指令，导致两个问题：(1) 非结构化技能缺乏显式的工作流级指导且冗余严重，LLM难以执行；(2) 无约束的自然语言技能搜索空间过于庞大，使优化效率低下。

Method: 提出图结构技能表示：节点表示执行步骤及其操作指导，有向边编码步骤间依赖上下文的转移关系，从而提供清晰的工作流级指导并利于优化。在此基础上提出GraphSkillEvo——基于群体的进化优化框架，配备面向图结构技能的变异与交叉算子，通过维护多个候选技能并组合有效组件，实现比纯LLM迭代自精炼更广泛、更全面的结构化技能空间探索。

Result: 在五个智能体基准上的大量实验表明，GraphSkillEvo持续优于强技能优化基线SkillOpt，在GPT-5.4-nano上平均准确率提升4.01%，在GPT-5.4上提升1.76%。

Conclusion: 将技能结构化为图结构自然语言产物能有效解决非结构化技能的执行难与优化难问题，结合群体进化优化可显著提升LLM智能体性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Skills can improve the performance of Large Language Model (LLM) agents by providing task-specific procedural guidance, while skill optimization further improves their effectiveness through iterative refinement. However, existing skill optimization methods typically represent skills as unstructured natural-language instructions, creating two key challenges: 1) Unstructured skills often lack explicit workflow-level guidance and contain substantial redundancy, making them difficult for LLMs to execute; 2) the vast search space of unconstrained natural-language skills makes skill optimization ineffective. To address these challenges, we propose representing skills as graph-structured natural-language artifacts. In graph-structured skills, each node represents an execution step together with its operational guidance, while directed edges encode context-dependent transitions between steps. Compared to unstructured skills, graph-structured skills can provide clear workflow-level guidance. Moreover, the proposed graph-structured skill can also facilitate skill optimization. Building on this structured representation, we introduce GraphSkillEvo, a population-based evolutionary optimization framework with mutation and crossover operators for graph-structured skills. By maintaining multiple candidate skills and combining effective components, GraphSkillEvo enables broader and more comprehensive exploration of the structured skill space than purely LLM-based iterative self-refinement. Extensive experiments across five agent benchmarks demonstrate that GraphSkillEvo consistently outperforms the strong skill optimization baseline SkillOpt, improving average accuracy by 4.01% on GPT-5.4-nano and 1.76% on GPT-5.4. Our code is available at https://github.com/ruisun7/GraphSkillEvo.

</details>


### [289] [Bilevel Optimization of Topology and Hyperparameters (BOTH)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21758)
*Suryanarayanan Manoj Sanu,Miguel Anibal Bessa,Alejandro Marcos Aragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用自动微分对拓扑优化(TO)本身求导，得到"超梯度"以在原始优化过程中同步调节大量超参数，仅需一两步TO即可提供足够信息，且能以数百次标准TO的代价扩展到数千个超参数，在应力约束与柔度问题上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TO在实践中高度依赖"魔法数字"式超参数，其取值对结果影响显著；调参通常需要深厚的领域知识并依赖大量试错。替代方案（代理模型辅助的超参数优化）又要求通过精心的问题建模严格控制超参数数量，难以应对超参数规模较大的情形。

Method: 将拓扑优化过程本身通过自动微分进行求导，得到超梯度(hypergradients)，从而在原始优化的同时联合调节超参数。该方法只评估TO的一到两步即可获得充分信息，并可扩展至数千个超参数，计算开销与数次标准TO相当。实验在应力约束问题和柔度问题上验证，柔度问题采用密度场的神经参数化。

Result: 验证表明：仅评估一到两步TO便足以提供有效信息；方法在开销约等于数次标准TO的情况下可良好扩展到数千个超参数；在应力约束与柔度（含神经参数化密度场）问题上均取得成功。

Conclusion: 通过自动微分对TO过程求导可获得超梯度，实现超参数与主优化的同步调节，突破了传统代理辅助方法对超参数数量的限制，为自动化设计提供了更实用的途径。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) represents a significant step towards automating the design process: given a working simulation, TO can produce a viable prototype at the press of a button by differentiating the simulation and iteratively improving the design. In practice, however, TO is riddled with ``magic numbers''---hyperparameters whose tuning significantly affects the outcome. Finding the right values typically requires not only deep problem-specific knowledge but also extensive trial-and-error. While practitioners can use surrogate-assisted hyperparameter optimization as an alternative, this approach requires strictly limiting the number of hyperparameters through careful problem formulation. Here, we propose differentiating TO itself using automatic differentiation. This yields ``hypergradients'' that allow us to tune these hyperparameters in tandem with the primary optimization. We show that evaluating just one or two steps of TO is sufficiently informative and that the method scales favorably to thousands of hyperparameters at an expense comparable to only a few standard TO runs. We demonstrate this approach on stress-constrained and compliance problems, with the latter utilizing a neural parameterization of the density field.

</details>


### [290] [RegKT: Interpretable and Robust Deep Knowledge Tracing With IRT-Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21791)
*Samuel Girard,Juan D. Pinto,Jill-Jênn Vie,Amel Bouzeghoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型正则化技术，同时提升深度知识追踪模型的鲁棒性与可解释性，缓解小数据集下的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度知识追踪模型精度提升伴随可解释性下降，影响教育实践者的采纳；同时教育场景数据规模小，深度模型易过拟合。

Method: 提出一种新颖的正则化技术，用于增强深度知识追踪模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 同时应对可解释性与过拟合两大挑战（摘要未给出具体实验数据或量化结果）。

Conclusion: 该方法使深度知识追踪模型更适用于真实教育应用场景。

Abstract: As deep learning models continue to advance, knowledge tracing models have achieved higher accuracy. However, these gains come at the cost of reduced interpretability, which is crucial for practitioners in educational settings to adopt new methodologies. Additionally, deep learning models are prone to overfitting, particularly when dealing with the small datasets that are common in educational applications. In this paper, we propose a novel regularization technique designed to enhance the robustness of deep-learning-based knowledge tracing models, while simultaneously improving their interpretability. Our method addresses both the interpretability and overfitting challenges, making it more feasible for real-world educational applications.

</details>


### [291] [Matrix AdaGrad: Row-wise and Column-wise Adaptive Subgradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21815)
*Wenpeng Zhang,Runsheng Yu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向矩阵参数的在线镜像下降（OMD）框架，引入行/列方向的自适应近端函数，导出 Row-AdaGrad 与 Column-AdaGrad，并给出遗憾界证明与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdaGrad、Adam 等自适应优化方法主要针对向量参数设计，未显式利用参数的矩阵结构；现有矩阵感知优化器虽显示结构化优化的优势，但缺乏类似 AdaGrad 那样通用的矩阵自适应理论推导框架。

Method: 构建带自适应近端函数的通用 Online Mirror Descent 框架以处理矩阵值参数，通过在线遗憾最小化推导矩阵自适应优化；引入行向与列向矩阵近端函数，分析由此产生的遗憾权衡，进而推导出 Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad 和 Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad，其自适应缩放由累积的行向或列向梯度范数决定。

Result: 建立了两种方法的遗憾保证，并证明在结构化梯度下，这类矩阵感知的界可严格优于逐元素（entry-wise）AdaGrad 的界；在矩阵分解与深度神经网络训练实验中，将自适应缩放与矩阵结构对齐带来了更好的优化稳定性，并可在更大学习率与更深网络下保持可训练性。

Conclusion: 该工作为矩阵感知自适应优化提供了类似 AdaGrad 的一般理论框架，表明利用矩阵行列结构设计自适应缩放具有理论优势与实用价值。

Abstract: Adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Adam are widely used in modern neural-network training, but their adaptive scaling is primarily designed for vector-valued parameters and does not explicitly exploit matrix structure. Recent matrix-aware optimizers demonstrate the benefits of structured optimization, yet a general theoretical framework for deriving matrix-aware adaptivity comparable to that of AdaGrad remains lacking. In this work, we develop a general Online Mirror Descent framework with adaptive proximal functions for matrix-valued parameters, providing a principled approach to deriving matrix-aware adaptive optimization through online regret minimization. By introducing row-wise and column-wise matrix proximal functions and analyzing the resulting regret trade-off, we derive Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Row-AdaGrad) and Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Column-AdaGrad), with adaptive scaling determined by the accumulated row-wise or column-wise gradient norms. We establish regret guarantees and show that these matrix-aware bounds can be strictly tighter than those of entry-wise AdaGrad under structured gradients. Experiments on matrix factorization and deep neural-network training further demonstrate the benefits of aligning adaptive scaling with matrix structure, including improved optimization stability and trainability at larger learning rates and greater network depths.

</details>


### [292] [Federated Deep Clustering Networks for High-Dimensional and Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21829)
*Morris Stallmann,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Marcin Pietrasik,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDCN，将深度聚类网络推广到联邦场景，联合优化重构损失和聚类损失，并通过合成数据增强与几何正则化提升非IID数据下的鲁棒性与潜在空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据聚类是无监督学习的核心任务，集中式场景下深度聚类效果良好，但联邦学习中数据分布在客户端且涉及隐私，相关研究较少。已有联邦深度聚类方法在客户端数据非独立同分布（non-IID）时性能不可靠，因此需要提升其在异质数据下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FedDCN，将Deep Clustering Networks推广至联邦学习场景，同时优化重构损失与聚类损失。为保证非IID场景下的鲁棒性和潜在空间对齐，FedDCN生成合成数据增强，并在学习目标中引入用于潜在空间对齐的几何正则化项。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在IID和非IID假设下均具有有效性，并据此指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: FedDCN为联邦深度聚类提供了一种兼顾重构与聚类、并借助合成增强和几何正则化应对非IID数据的有效方案，但该领域仍有进一步研究空间。

Abstract: Clustering high-dimensional data is a fundamental task in unsupervised machine learning with applications to a variety of domains. In the centralized data scenario, this task is commonly solved using deep clustering methods that utilize deep neural network architectures to learn clustering-friendly latent space representations. In Federated Learning, where data is distributed between clients and is private, deep clustering methods are less explored. In particular, recently introduced federated deep clustering methods, despite showing very promising performance, still fall short in reliably providing good performance if data across clients are non-identically-independently distributed. In this work, we introduce a generalization of Deep Clustering Networks to the federated scenario, named FedDCN, that simultaneously optimizes a reconstruction loss and a clustering loss. To ensure robustness and latent space alignment in non-identically-independently distributed data scenarios, FedDCN generates synthetic data augmentations, and its learning objective includes a geometric regularization for latent space alignment. Through experimental evaluation, the effectiveness of the approach under IID and non-IID assumptions is demonstrated, and future research directions are identified.

</details>


### [293] [The Weight Is Over - Interactive Diffusion on Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21849)
*Frieder Ganz,Maximilian Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对扩散模型在端侧推理中内存占用大、延迟敏感且流程复杂的问题，提出三项贡献：将小文本编码器映射到大编码器空间的嵌入翻译器、用于权衡速度/质量/内存的可复现扫描方法，以及在近期GPU上实现亚秒级TTFI的交互式端侧图像生成编辑器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧推理的发展动力几乎集中于语言模型，而扩散管线内存消耗大、延迟敏感，且需要协调嵌入器、Transformer、解码器及非标准化的后处理流程，因此难以在广泛的客户端设备上部署。作者希望在性能、质量与模型体积之间取得平衡，以覆盖尽可能多的真实设备。

Method: 提出三项方法：1）嵌入翻译器，将小型文本编码器映射到大型编码器空间，以降低权重与延迟；2）可复现的扫描配方，用于在扩散管线中导航速度/质量/内存三角权衡；3）交互式端侧图像生成编辑器。

Result: 所提出的交互式端侧图像生成编辑器在近期GPU上实现了亚秒级的TTFI（首图时间）。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入翻译器、可复现扫描配方与端侧编辑器的组合，论文在速度、质量与内存之间实现了有效权衡，验证了扩散管线在端侧设备上实现低延迟交互式应用的可行性。

Abstract: On-device inference is booming, but the momentum is almost all in language models. Diffusion pipelines are memory hungry, latency-sensitive, and require orchestrating an embedder, a transformer, a decoder, and often further postprocessing that is not as standardized as LLM inference loops are. We navigate the trade-off between performance, quality, and model footprint to reach as many client devices in the wild as possible. We make three contributions: an embedding translator that maps a small text encoder into a large encoder space to cut weight and latency; a reproducible sweep recipe for navigating the speed/quality/memory triangle in diffusion pipelines; and an interactive on-device image generation editor achieving sub-second TTFI on recent GPUs.

</details>


### [294] [Neural Cellular Automata Learn General Features in their Hidden Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21870)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经细胞自动机（NCA）隐藏通道的内部动力学，提出将预训练教师模型的隐藏状态注入学生模型以引导早期优化的迁移学习机制。在少样本MNIST基准上，约9800参数的NCA优于同类循环与前馈架构，其隐藏通道解耦特征提取与分类共识，编码尺度不变的拓扑原语，可迁移至未见类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习依赖过参数化实现泛化，但在少样本场景下易过拟合与记忆。NCA是参数高效的替代方案，然而既有研究主要关注其输出，其内部隐藏通道的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 分析NCA隐藏通道的内部动力学；提出一种迁移学习机制，将预训练教师模型的隐藏状态注入学生模型以引导其早期优化；在少样本与尺度变化MNIST基准上，与循环及前馈架构进行对比；并开展机制性分析，考察隐藏通道的表征性质。

Result: NCA以极小参数量（约9800）取得优于对比架构的泛化性能；机制分析表明隐藏通道通过吸收形态复杂度、收敛至相互正交状态，实现特征提取与统一分类共识的解耦；隐藏通道捕获的是通用的、尺度不变的拓扑原语而非类别专属模板；仅用数字0-5训练的教师所迁移的特征，使学生模型在未见类别上获得强少样本性能。

Conclusion: 利用隐藏状态动力学作为稳健、去中心化的计算基底，是实现参数高效迁移学习的有效途径。

Abstract: Modern deep learning models achieve impressive generalization through over-parameterization, but this paradigm often struggles with overfitting and memorization in few-shot regimes. Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) offer a highly parameter-efficient alternative, yet research has focused primarily on their output, leaving the role of their internal hidden channels largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the internal dynamics of NCA hidden channels and introduce a novel transfer-learning mechanism that injects a pretrained teacher's hidden states into a student model to guide early optimization. Evaluated on few-shot and scale-variant MNIST benchmarks, NCAs outperform comparable recurrent and feed-forward architectures, demonstrating superior generalization with a minimal parameter budget (~9,800 parameters). Mechanistic analysis reveals that the hidden channels decouple feature extraction from uniform classification consensus by absorbing morphological complexity and converging to mutually orthogonal states. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these hidden channels capture general, scale-invariant topological primitives rather than class-specific templates. This allows a student model to achieve strong few-shot performance on unseen classes using features transferred from a teacher trained only on a subset of digits (0-5). Our results highlight the potential of utilizing hidden-state dynamics as a robust, decentralized computational substrate for parameter-efficient transfer learning

</details>


### [295] [Geometric Mean Pooling for Equal-Weight Multiplicative Coarse-Graining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21876)
*Ang-Kun Wu,Fangdi Wen,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何均值池化（GMP），一种融合特征符号乘积与幅值几何均值的带符号池化算子，用于替代平均池化和最大池化，并在多类任务上评估其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均池化具有加性偏置，最大池化具有极值偏置，二者均不适用于需要乘性组合的任务。受量子多体物理中"局部到全局"组合的启发，作者希望设计一种能同时保留联合符号信息与乘性尺度特征的池化算子。

Method: 提出几何均值池化（GMP）：将特征符号的乘积与特征幅值的几何均值相结合，构成带符号池化算子；该算子不引入可学习参数。进一步证明非重叠的层级式GMP能够保持相应的全局乘性统计量，并在合成序列任务、迭代粗粒化、图像分类和分子亲脂性回归上进行评估。

Result: 在合成任务中，GMP比平均池化和最大池化更准确地恢复基于乘积的信号，并在所测试的乘性输入噪声水平下保持预测性能。在图像与分子数据上，其有效性依赖于表示方式、目标参数化以及局部与全局池化的位置。

Conclusion: GMP是一种互补的、依赖具体场景的归纳偏置，适用于等权乘性组合合理成立的任务，而非标准池化算子的通用替代方案。

Abstract: As an alternative to the additive and extremal biases of average and max pooling, we introduce Geometric Mean Pooling (GMP), a signed pooling operator that combines the product of feature signs with the geometric mean of feature magnitudes. Motivated by local-to-global composition in quantum many-body physics, GMP retains both joint sign information and a characteristic multiplicative scale without introducing learnable pooling parameters. We show that non-overlapping hierarchical GMP preserves the corresponding global multiplicative statistic and evaluate it on synthetic sequence tasks, iterative coarse-graining, image classification, and molecular lipophilicity regression. On the synthetic tasks, GMP recovers product-based signals more accurately than average and max pooling and maintains predictive performance under the tested levels of multiplicative input noise. On image and molecular data, however, its effectiveness depends on the representation, target parameterization, and placement of local and global pooling. These results position GMP as a complementary, regime-dependent inductive bias for tasks in which equal-weight multiplicative composition is plausible, rather than as a universal replacement for standard pooling operators.

</details>


### [296] [Detecting Pretraining Data in Large Language Models from a Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21888)
*Chenye Ke,Zirui Liu,Qi Liu,Yan Zhuang,Jintao Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量转移检测（ETD），用倾斜边界在损失-熵联合空间中检测预训练数据，性能优于仅用似然的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖预测似然判断预训练数据归属存在根本歧义：高似然既可能来自训练曝光，也可能来自强泛化能力，因此可预测的非成员样本会被误判为成员。

Method: 在预测损失与预测熵的联合空间中，用相对熵的倾斜边界替代水平边界。理论上分析熵修正可保持期望成员信号并降低其方差，从而提升标准化成员-非成员可分性；并将均值-方差分析推广到非零熵均值差的更一般情形。该熵调整得分具有Helmholtz自由能解释，据此提出能量转移检测（ETD），从宏观残余自由能转移视角刻画预训练数据检测。

Result: 大量实验表明ETD取得最佳平均检测性能，平均AUROC最高提升3.5%，TPR@5%FPR最高提升5.1%，并在多种设置下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 将预测损失相对预测熵进行校正，可有效缓解似然歧义，该视角可由自由能转移统一解释，ETD在预训练数据检测上具备稳定且更优的效果。

Abstract: Detecting pretraining data in large language models is challenging because high likelihood can reflect either training exposure or strong generalization. In the joint space of prediction loss and predictive entropy, a likelihood-only detector uses a horizontal boundary and can mistake predictable non-members for members. Motivated by this, we introduce an inclined boundary that evaluates prediction loss relative to predictive entropy. Our analysis shows that entropy correction can preserve the expected membership signal while reducing its variance, thereby improving standardized member--non-member separation. We further extend the mean--variance analysis to the more general setting with a nonzero mean entropy gap. Interestingly, this entropy-adjusted score admits a Helmholtz free-energy interpretation, leading to Energy Transfer Detection (ETD), which views pretraining data detection from a macroscopic residual free-energy transfer perspective. Extensive experiments show that ETD achieves the best average detection performance, improving average AUROC by up to 3.5\% and TPR@5\%FPR by up to 5.1\%, while remaining robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [297] [LLMs as Feature Engineers for Text-and-Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21894)
*Merwan Barlier,Blaz Skrlj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代框架，利用生成器LLM、提取器LLM与下游表格模型协同，从非结构化文本中自动抽取可解释、受模式约束的分类特征；通过将模型错误转化为自然语言反馈引导搜索，特征发现速度提升最高3倍，且与TF-IDF及稠密嵌入结合时严格优于任一子集，并保证实例级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向表格预测任务，从非结构化文本中自动获得兼具预测性能与可解释性的分类特征存在困难：现有特征工程依赖人工设计，LLM生成特征又常缺乏模式约束与搜索引导，难以针对性地解决具体预测失败，且可解释性不足。

Method: 构建迭代式特征发现框架，将特征空间搜索分解为三个角色：生成器LLM提出语义定义，独立的提取器LLM将定义物化为特征，下游表格模型评估其预测性能。关键在于将显式模型错误（如AUC排序反转）翻译为自然语言反馈，用于指导LLM解决特定预测失败，形成错误驱动的闭环优化。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上评估，错误驱动循环相比无引导搜索将特征发现速度提升最高3倍；生成特征表现出强多视角互补性，与TF-IDF和稠密嵌入结合时严格优于任何单一子集；所发现特征在SHAP重要性排序中占主导，提供完全透明、可语义审计的逐实例预测依据。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，以模型错误为自然语言反馈、并以模式约束LLM生成的特征抽取范式，能够高效地同时提升表格预测性能与可解释性，为文本特征的自动化、可审计构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce an iterative framework that automates the extraction of interpretable, schema-bound categorical features from unstructured text for tabular prediction models. To navigate the feature space, a generator LLM proposes semantic definitions, a separate extractor LLM materializes the features, and a downstream tabular model evaluates their predictive performance. We optimize this search by translating explicit model errors, such as AUC ranking inversions, into natural-language feedback, steering the LLM to resolve specific predictive failures. Evaluated across three public datasets, this error-driven loop accelerates feature discovery by up to $3\times$ compared to unguided search. Empirically, the generated features demonstrate strong multi-view complementarity, strictly outperforming any subset when combined with TF-IDF and dense embeddings. Finally, the framework guarantees instance-level interpretability: the discovered features dominate SHAP importance rankings and provide a fully transparent, semantic audit trail for every prediction.

</details>


### [298] [ExpBoN: Exponential-Noise Best-of-$n$ for Efficient Test-Time LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21899)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExpBoN，一种基于指数噪声 report-noisy-max 机制的软 Best-of-n 采样方法，具备精确有限 n 分解、指数级收敛与 regret 理论保证，并集成到 GSI 框架形成 ExpGSI，在保持精度的同时显著降低推理计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Best-of-n（BoN）采样虽然简单有效，但硬最大化只能对 reward 与分布偏移之间的权衡进行粗粒度控制。Soft BoN 提供了更平滑的控制并收敛到 KL 正则化 reward 最大化对应的最优分布，本文旨在提出另一种软 BoN 方法以提供更完善的理论与算法基础。

Method: 提出 ExpBoN，基于指数噪声的 report-noisy-max 机制实现软 BoN。方法具有精确的有限 n 分解，可用于分析收敛性。进一步将 ExpBoN 集成到 guided speculative inference（GSI）框架中，形成 ExpGSI，用于高效的 reward 引导 LLM 对齐。

Result: 理论方面，ExpBoN 在总变差距离、期望 reward 以及两个方向的 KL 散度上均实现指数级快速收敛，并给出收敛性与 regret 行为的全面理论分析。实验方面，在 MATH500、MMLU-STEM 与 Minerva Math 上使用 Qwen2.5-Math 和 Qwen3 系列模型，ExpGSI 在 Qwen2.5-Math 各候选预算下减少约 14%–39% 的估算计算量，在 Qwen3 的 n=16 时最高减少 45%，同时保持可比精度。

Conclusion: 本研究为指数噪声 BoN 与高效测试时 LLM 对齐提供了理论与算法基础，ExpGSI 在显著降低计算成本的同时维持了精度。

Abstract: Best-of-$n$ (BoN) sampling is a simple yet effective inference-time alignment method, but hard maximization provides only coarse control over the trade-off between reward and distribution shift. Soft Best-of-$n$ (Verdun et al. 2025) provides smoother control and converges to the optimal distribution associated with KL-regularized reward maximization. In this paper, we introduce ExpBoN, an alternative soft BoN method based on the exponential-noise report-noisy-max mechanism. It admits an exact finite-$n$ decomposition, which yields exponentially fast convergence in total variation, expected reward, and both directions of KL divergence. We provide comprehensive theoretical analyses of its convergence and regret behavior. We further integrate ExpBoN into the guided speculative inference (GSI) framework (Geuter, Mroueh, and AlvarezMelis 2025), resulting in ExpGSI, for efficient reward-guided LLM alignment. ExpGSI yields substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining comparable accuracy. Experiments on MATH500, MMLU-STEM, and Minerva Math with the Qwen2.5-Math and Qwen3 model families show that ExpGSI reduces estimated computation by $14\%$-$39\%$ across candidate budgets for Qwen2.5-Math and by up to $45\%$ at $n=16$ for Qwen3. Overall, our results provide a theoretical and algorithmic foundation for exponential-noise BoN and efficient test-time LLM alignment.

</details>


### [299] [Intervention Granularity Matters: Coherent Treatment Bundles in Counterfactual Simulation with Clinical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21906)
*Fangzhou Wang,Yixuan Yang,Camilla Balzarotti,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 临床世界模型的反事实模拟中，干预粒度会实质性影响预测响应：整体捆绑编辑比单组件编辑产生更大的状态偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟需要先界定"一次干预"的边界，但临床上干预以捆绑形式记录。对 MIMIC-IV 中 945,707 患者小时的共现审计显示，诸如透析回路各参数等组件几乎从不单独出现，因此单独编辑某一组件的做法描述了数据中不存在的状态。作者据此假设干预编辑的粒度会改变世界模型的响应，并加以检验。

Method: 提出 Clin-JEPA——一个以逐小时治疗文本为条件、对患者轨迹建模的潜空间世界模型。在 1,019 个有创通气启动记录处，固定患者历史及其他治疗，将"编辑单个呼吸机设置"与"编辑取自近期轨迹最相似真实患者的完整配置"进行对比；并进一步控制每次编辑对模型输入的改变量。

Result: 完整捆绑编辑对预测下一状态的推动幅度大于任何单一设置，且在全部五种设置上一致成立；该差异在扣除编辑对模型输入改变量的影响后依然存在。

Conclusion: 干预粒度会实质性影响临床世界模型的响应：单组件编辑可能低估治疗敏感性，捆绑感知（bundle-aware）的编辑更有依据，更适合作为反事实治疗模拟的基础。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation with a clinical world model means fixing a patient's history, changing the treatment, and reading off the predicted response. Doing so requires deciding what counts as one intervention. In clinical settings, interventions are documented as bundles: a co-occurrence audit of 945,707 patient-hours from MIMIC-IV shows groups of components, such as every parameter of a dialysis circuit, that never appear apart, so an edit that changes one component on its own describes an hour that never occurs in the data. We hypothesize that the granularity at which an intervention is edited changes how a world model responds, and test this with Clin-JEPA, a latent world model of patient trajectories conditioned on hourly treatment text. At 1,019 documented onsets of invasive ventilation, we keep the patient's history and other treatments fixed and compare editing one ventilator setting with editing the complete configuration recorded for a real patient with the most similar recent trajectory. The complete bundle moves the predicted next state further than any single setting, consistently across all five settings, and the difference remains after accounting for how much each edit changes the model's input. Intervention granularity therefore materially affects the response of a clinical world model: single-component edits may understate treatment sensitivity, and bundle-aware editing may offer a better-supported basis for counterfactual treatment simulation.

</details>


### [300] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统比较了SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim两种运动模仿强化学习管线：二者运动学精度相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更贴合实验EMG，更适合肌肉骨骼建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明两类运动模仿强化学习管线（SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim）都能高保真复现人类运动学，但尚不清楚它们能否准确刻画产生运动的底层神经肌肉行为。这一局限对机器人辅助设备的设计与控制尤为关键，因为肌肉激活模式与代谢成本等指标常被用作优化目标。

Method: 使用同一套人体运动捕捉与肌电（EMG）测量数据，对两条管线进行系统性对比：HyFyDy侧重通过详细肌腱建模实现生理真实性，MuJoCo侧重计算效率与可扩展的策略学习。

Result: 两条管线均能以相对精度生成相似的运动学结果，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活与实验EMG吻合度更高。肌肉激活的平均汇总（RMSE, r）指标：HyFyDy为(0.164, 0.4)，MuJoCo为(0.344, 0.11)。

Conclusion: HyFyDy凭借更先进的生理真实性，当前更适用于肌肉骨骼建模；但两者均需进一步发展，以将生理真实性引入GPU可并行仿真环境，从而推进机器人辅助设备设计。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [301] [Kinks vs. Smoothness: Identifiability of Real Analytic nICA for Laplace-like Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21926)
*Isaac Manring,Kejun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在真实解析生成函数、源分布概率密度函数一阶导数仅有有限个间断点时，非线性独立成分分析（nICA）可辨识（精确恢复至平凡歧义），拉普拉斯分布是满足该假设的典型例子，并在Normalizing Flows与VAE上验证了可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nICA 的核心难题是恢复真正潜在的独立生成因子，而非其某种混淆版本；已有可辨识性结果通常依赖较强的假设或特定的生成机制，限制了适用范围，作者希望给出更广泛、且能直接套用到现有训练流程的可辨识性条件。

Method: 假设生成函数为真实解析函数，源概率密度函数的一阶导数仅有有限个间断点（拉普拉斯分布为典型代表），利用源分布中的"kink"（折点）与真实解析函数光滑性之间的对比来构造可辨识性证明；该理论可近似对应使用 tanh、softplus、GELU 等标准激活函数的 Normalizing Flows 或 VAE，因而几乎无需改动现有训练流程即可应用。

Result: 在理论与实验两方面均验证可辨识性：在合成数据与真实数据上，使用 Normalizing Flows 和 VAE 均展示出可辨识性质；在 CelebA 数据上成功恢复出若干可解释的潜在因子，这些因子能控制数据集中不同样本的独有属性。

Conclusion: 该工作将 nICA 的可辨识性扩展到真实解析生成函数与具有有限一阶导数间断点的源分布这一较广类别，且与主流生成模型（NF、VAE）兼容，为实际训练管线提供了理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Many machine learning systems try to explain complex data - like images or financial time series - in terms of hidden, independent factors that generated them. Recovering the true underlying factors, rather than some scrambled version of them, is the central challenge of nonlinear Independent Component Analysis (nICA). We prove identifiability (exact recovery) up to trivial ambiguities for real analytic generating functions when source probability density functions have a finite number of discontinuities in the first derivative. The Laplace distribution is the most prominent example satisfying this assumption. Our proof relies on the contrast between kinks in the source distribution and the smoothness of real analytic functions. Real analytic functions comprise a broad class of generating mechanisms, and can be approximated with Normalizing Flows or Variational Autoencoders with standard activation functions (e.g., tanh, softplus, GELU), so our result applies with minimal changes to existing training pipelines. We perform experiments on real and synthetic data with both Normalizing Flows and Variational Auto-Encoders demonstrating their identifiability properties. In experiments on CelebA data we recover several interpretable latent factors controlling unique attributes across the dataset.

</details>


### [302] [Joint Remaining Useful Life Prediction and Capacity Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries Using Partial-Charging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21932)
*Khoa Tran,Ho-Si-Hung Nguyen,Phone Wai Yan Moe,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Thi-Hoang-Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨专家框架，仅用部分充电测量数据（无需历史满循环容量），联合预测电池剩余使用寿命（RUL）与容量：RUL专家用GRU+2D-CNN+时序GRU编码近30循环内的10个10分钟片段，容量专家用2D-CNN+Transformer处理10个连续循环的40分钟片段统计描述符，并以特征级线性调制（FiLM）用短期表征调制长期表征，经三阶段训练融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合RUL预测与容量估计需要同时表征电池的长期渐变退化与近期行为。现有方法通常依赖历史满循环容量测量，实际应用中难以获取；本文希望仅用部分充电测量即可实现两类任务的联合预测。

Method: 输入为部分充电测量，不含实测历史满循环容量。RUL专家：对30循环历史中采样的10个循环、每循环取10分钟标称片段，经预训练GRU编码器、2D-CNN与时序GRU建模。容量专家：对10个连续循环的40分钟标称片段提取统计描述符，经2D-CNN与Transformer建模。通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）模块，用短期表征对长期表征进行条件调制，实现联合预测。训练分三阶段：监督式自编码器预训练、专家独立预训练、冻结专家后的融合训练。

Result: 在两个公开电池老化数据集上，参考配置的RUL平均RMSE分别为143.69与161.10循环，容量误差分别为12.36与7.28mAh。在数据集I上，融合相对任一单独专家均降低了平均误差。结果显示出RUL与容量精度之间存在权衡：所提方法在两个数据集上取得对比方法中最低的RUL RMSE，而若干基线方法在容量误差上更低。

Conclusion: 跨专家+FiLM条件调制的联合框架可在缺乏历史满循环容量的条件下利用部分充电数据同时预测RUL与容量，在RUL精度上优于对比方法，但容量精度被部分基线超越，二者存在精度权衡。

Abstract: Joint remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and capacity estimation require representations of both gradual degradation and recent battery behavior. This paper presents a cross-expert framework using partial-charging measurements without measured historical full-cycle capacity as an input. The RUL Expert encodes nominal 10-min segments from ten cycles sampled within a 30-cycle history using a pretrained gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder, a two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2D-CNN), and a temporal GRU. The Capacity Expert processes statistical descriptors of nominal 40-min segments from ten consecutive cycles using a 2D-CNN and a Transformer. A feature-wise linear modulation module uses the short-term representation to condition the long-term representation for joint prediction. Training comprises supervised autoencoder pretraining, independent expert pretraining, and fusion training with frozen experts. On two public battery-aging datasets, the reference configuration achieves mean RUL root-mean-square errors of 143.69 and 161.10 cycles and capacity errors of 12.36 and 7.28mAh, respectively. On Dataset I, fusion reduces both mean errors relative to either standalone expert. The results demonstrate a trade-off between RUL and capacity accuracy: the proposed method attains the lowest reported RUL RMSE among the compared methods on both datasets, whereas several baselines yield lower capacity errors.

</details>


### [303] [Learning to Move Cities: Deep Meta-Models and Reinforcement Policies for Calibration and Control in Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21945)
*Adewumi Augustine Adepitan,Christopher J. Haruna,Oluwasegun Adegoke,Ayooluwatomiwa Ajiboye,Oluwatobi Oluwasakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出共享隐空间框架，将交通仿真器标定与强化学习控制统一于同一学习表示中，实验显示可将系统总行程时间最多降低51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通网络优化面临两大难题：高保真仿真器标定计算昂贵，以及实时运行控制的效率瓶颈；传统优化方法在大规模网络上存在计算瓶颈，且标定与控制通常相互割裂。

Method: 1）组合式MLP自编码器架构，学习将仿真器输入（OD需求、网络参数）与输出（行程时间、拥堵模式）关联的低维流形，用于高效贝叶斯优化标定；2）带经验回放和目标网络的深度Q学习智能体，通过调度与路径调整优化动态交通分配；3）将学习到的隐表示同时用于降维标定和强化学习状态表示。

Result: 组合式MLP自编码器在固定计算预算下比传统降维方法样本效率更高、对观测数据拟合更好；在基准网络上的实证评估中，系统总行程时间相比基线运行最多降低51%。

Conclusion: 共享隐空间表述为智能交通系统提供了从仿真器标定到自适应运行控制的统一路径，表明深度学习方法在城市出行规划与管理、尤其在大规模网络场景中具有变革性潜力。

Abstract: Urban transportation networks present complex optimization challenges spanning calibration of high-fidelity simulators and real-time operational control. This paper presents a shared latent-space framework that connects simulator calibration and reinforcement learning control through a common learned representation of urban traffic dynamics. First, we develop a combinatorial MLP-autoencoder architecture that learns low-dimensional manifolds linking simulator inputs (origin-destination demand, network parameters) to outputs (travel times, congestion patterns), enabling efficient Bayesian optimization for calibration. This approach demonstrates superior sample efficiency compared to traditional dimension reduction methods, achieving better fit to observational data within fixed computational budgets. Second, we implement a deep Q-learning agent with experience replay and target networks to optimize dynamic traffic assignment through scheduling and routing adjustments. In empirical evaluations on benchmark networks, our approach reduces system-wide travel times by up to 51% compared to baseline operations. The learned latent representation is not only used to reduce the dimensionality of Bayesian calibration, but is also incorporated into the reinforcement learning state representation, allowing the control policy to operate on compressed and calibrated traffic dynamics. This shared latent-space formulation provides a unified pathway from simulator calibration to adaptive operational control within intelligent transportation systems. Our results highlight the transformative potential of deep learning methods in urban mobility planning and management, particularly for large-scale networks where traditional optimization approaches face computational bottlenecks.

</details>


### [304] [RACER: Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Human-AI Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21953)
*Joshua Strong,Emma Sun,Alexander Capstick,Pramit Saha,Cheng Ouyang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RACER，一种角色相对的胜任度估计框架，用于从上下文估计未见专家的胜任度并实现自适应延迟决策，具有类别重标记不变性与理论保证，在多个合成与医学影像基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口自适应的延迟决策（population-adaptive deferral）需要利用少量专家行为上下文来处理未见专家。现有神经上下文编码器（如 L2D-Pop）可能依赖查询，但会学到与绝对类别坐标绑定的路由捷径；Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) 通过角色索引的类别胜任度剖面消除此类捷径，但其估计在每个类别内为常数，无法刻画实例级的专家专长。

Method: RACER（Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing）采用角色相对框架：在每个候选类别角色下估计专家对查询答对的后验预测概率，再结合模型后验得到贝叶斯相关的专家答对概率。方法包含非参数与神经核池化估计器，利用候选角色关系、共享聚合与对称汇总，排除绝对类别身份通道。

Result: 理论证明类别重标记一致性不变性，推导贝叶斯对齐的延迟替代损失，并给出将路由遗憾与分类器及胜任度估计误差关联的插件式遗憾界。在受控合成基准（含带模拟专家的 PathMNIST 病理组织学上下文缩放研究）上，RACER 在隐藏亚型依赖下能受益于更多上下文；在 CIFAR-100 合成实验中，于单独采样的未见专家划分上取得最强总体性能。在放射科与人类—AI 胸片基准（VinDr-CXR 与 CheXpert）上，RACER 家族在预算扫描延迟决策中具有竞争力或最优，校准结果因指标与数据集而异。

Conclusion: RACER 通过角色对齐的胜任度估计解决了现有方法的捷径学习与实例级刻画不足问题，具备理论保证，并在合成与医学影像基准上展现出竞争力与优势，为人口自适应延迟决策提供了新框架。

Abstract: Learning to defer asks a predictive system when to act autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. Population-adaptive deferral extends this problem to unseen experts using a small context set of expert behavior. Neural context encoders such as L2D-Pop can be query-dependent, but may learn routing shortcuts tied to absolute class coordinates. Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) removes such shortcuts through role-indexed classwise competence profiles, but its estimates are constant within each class and cannot capture instance-level expert specialization. We propose RACER---Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing---a role-relative framework for estimating an unseen expert's competence from context. RACER estimates the posterior-predictive probability that the expert is correct on a query under each candidate class role, then combines these estimates with the model posterior to obtain the Bayes-relevant expert-correctness probability. Nonparametric and neural kernel-pooling estimators use candidate-role relations, shared aggregation, and symmetric summaries, excluding absolute class-identity channels. We prove coherent class-relabelling invariance, derive a Bayes-aligned deferral surrogate, and give a plug-in regret bound relating routing regret to classifier and competence-estimation error. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, including a PathMNIST histopathology context-scaling study with simulated experts, RACER benefits from additional context under hidden subtype dependence and gives the strongest aggregate performance on a separately sampled unseen-expert split in the CIFAR-100 synthetic experiments. On the radiologist and human--AI chest-radiography benchmarks (VinDr-CXR and CheXpert), the RACER family is competitive or best in budget-swept deferral, with calibration results varying across metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [305] [Time series generation with spectrally aligned latent flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21989)
*Camilo Carvajal Reyes,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频谱对齐的潜在流时间序列生成器，通过在流匹配的潜在空间中引入基于傅里叶、小波与签名变换的微调损失，使合成信号在平滑度与频谱内容等特征上与真实数据对齐，从而克服潜在压缩带来的频谱失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在流模型是高效的时间序列生成方法，但潜在压缩会引入不想要的伪影，例如与原始数据集之间的频谱失配，从而限制其作为训练替代数据的可用性（在真实性与可解释性特征上偏离真实数据）。

Method: 提出频谱对齐的潜在流生成器：对用于流匹配的潜在空间进行训练，使其保留与合成样本适用性相关的动态特性。具体地，在仅有点对点重建损失之外，加入基于规范信号表示（傅里叶变换、小波变换、签名变换）的微调损失；利用这些变换的可解释性，确保合成信号在平滑度、目标频谱内容等相关特征上与真实信号对齐。

Result: 在真实世界的长程单变量与多变量基准数据集上，将所提对齐模型与基础潜在流模型及现有最优方法进行比较。定量结果表明，所提方法在反映信号真实性的指标与计算效率上均占优，同时其局部结构与训练集保持一致。

Conclusion: 在潜在流时间序列生成中引入基于傅里叶、小波和签名变换的表示层微调损失，可有效缓解潜在压缩导致的频谱失配，使合成数据在真实性、计算效率与局部结构对齐性上优于基线及现有最优方法，提升其作为训练替代数据的可靠性。

Abstract: Latent flow models have proven to be a reliable and cost-effective method for time series generation. However, the latent compression induces unwanted artefacts, such as a spectral mismatch with respect to the underlying dataset, thus hindering their use as training surrogates. In this article, we propose a spectrally-aligned latent-flow time series generator, where the latent space for flow matching is trained to preserve dynamical properties that are relevant for the suitability of synthetic samples. We find that incorporating fine-tuning losses based on canonical signal representations such as the Fourier, wavelet and signature transforms helps overcome these issues. The interpretability of these transformations allows us to ensure that the synthetic signals are aligned with the true ones in terms of relevant features, such as smoothness or targeted spectral content, as opposed to relying on pointwise reconstruction losses only. We compare the proposed aligned models against a base latent-flow model and the state of the art over real-world long-range univariate and multivariate benchmark datasets. Our quantitative results validate the superiority of the proposed method in terms of its performance on metrics reflecting signal realness and computational efficiency, while being aligned to the training set with respect to its local structure.

</details>


### [306] [Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Critical Heat Flux Models in the CTF Subchannel Code for Square Rod Bundle Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21995)
*Aidan Furlong,Vinicius de Melo Monteiro,Robert Salko,Juliana Pacheco Duarte,Xu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在CTF子通道程序中评估了基于机器学习的临界热流密度（CHF）模型在EPRI棒束数据库上的表现，涵盖纯模型与混合残差修正模型（局部/半局部形式）。结果表明管束训练模型可较好地迁移至棒束工况，局部混合LUT模型整体最优，半局部纯ML模型亦具竞争力，且性能显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHF预测与燃料性能和反应堆安全直接相关，是核热工水力中的关键挑战。机器学习方法相对传统经验关联式和查找表（LUT）虽能显著提高精度，但现有ML模型多基于圆管数据库开发与评估，其在反应堆相关棒束几何中的适用性尚缺乏深入研究。

Method: 在CTF子通道代码中部署ML-based CHF模型，采用EPRI棒束CHF数据库进行评估；考虑纯模型与混合残差修正模型两类方法，并分别以局部（local）和半局部（semilocal）形式实现；将ML模型预测结果与Bowring关联式、W-3关联式及2006 Groeneveld LUT进行对比。

Result: 管束训练的ML CHF模型总体上可较好地迁移至棒束应用，在多数几何与运行工况下优于传统CHF方法；局部混合LUT模型整体性能最强，半局部纯ML模型仍具高度竞争力；与Bowring、W-3关联式及2006 Groeneveld LUT的对比表明，即使仅用圆管数据训练，棒束CHF预测仍可取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在生产级子通道分析环境中对基于ML的CHF模型在方形棒束内开展大规模评估，验证了其有效性，支持其在反应堆热工水力分析中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: The prediction of critical heat flux (CHF), a key safety-related quantity in nuclear thermal hydraulics, remains an important challenge due to its direct relationship with fuel performance and reactor safety. Recent studies have demonstrated that relative to traditional empirical correlations and lookup tables (LUTs), machine learning (ML) methods can substantially improve CHF prediction accuracy. Most ML-based CHF models, however, have been developed and evaluated using tube databases, leaving their applicability to reactor-relevant rod bundle geometries largely unexplored.
  This study evaluates ML-based CHF models deployed within the CTF subchannel code using the Electric Power Research Institute (EPRI) rod bundle CHF database. Both pure and hybrid residual correction models are considered in local and semilocal formulations. The tube-trained ML CHF models generally transferred favorably to rod bundle applications and outperformed traditional CHF methods across most geometries and operating conditions. The local hybrid LUT model produced the strongest overall performance, and the semilocal pure ML model remained highly competitive. Comparison against the Bowring correlation, W-3 correlation, and 2006 Groeneveld LUT demonstrated that substantial improvements in rod bundle CHF prediction are possible even when models are trained exclusively on tube data. These findings provide one of the first large-scale assessments of ML-based CHF models in square rod bundles within a production-level subchannel analysis environment and support their broader application in reactor thermal hydraulic analysis.

</details>


### [307] [Abstention and Noise Filtering: Two Missing Primitives of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22005)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出注意力门控提供两种机制：弃权（abstention）与噪声过滤（noise filtering），经10M–350M参数规模实验证实二者随规模作用此消彼长，且同时具备二者性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报告门控注意力值路径可提升语言模型预训练，但对其提升原因存在分歧。本文旨在厘清该机制的作用来源，将其拆解为两种softmax注意力所缺失的原语并加以验证。

Method: 在匹配的10M–350M参数模型上，通过softmax中可学习的逐头sink logit实现弃权，通过每个value上的门控实现噪声过滤；并在value中注入可控干扰进行因果验证。

Result: 1）弃权收益随模型增大而下降，噪声过滤收益随规模上升；10M时弃权几乎解释全部门控增益，350M时过滤占主要部分。2）各规模下同时具备两种原语的模型最优。3）注入干扰实验证实门控可消除干扰，并揭示两种门控形式各自存在特征性盲点。

Conclusion: 门控注意力的收益源于弃权与噪声过滤两种互补原语，二者随规模作用互补；同时提供二者仅增加可忽略参数，且与KV cache兼容。

Abstract: Gating the value pathway of attention reportedly improves language model pretraining, and prior studies disagree on why. We argue and provide experimental evidence that such gates supply two different things that softmax attention lacks: abstention and noise filtering. The first is abstention, which allows an attention head to output nothing, bypassing the requirement that attention weights must sum to one. The second is noise filtering, which allows the value pathway of an attention head to suppress interference from superposed features in the residual stream. In our experiments in matched models from 10M to 350M parameters, we supply abstention through a learned per-head sink logit in the softmax and noise filtering through a gate on each value. We report three empirical findings. First, the benefit of abstention, measured as the reduction in validation loss relative to a matched baseline, declines as models grow, whereas the benefit of noise filtering increases with scale. In particular, abstention accounts for nearly all of the gain from gating at 10M and filtering for most of it at 350M. Second, the best model at every scale is the one with both primitives built in. Third, injecting controlled interference into the values a head reads confirms that the gate removes such interference, and reveals that each of the two gate forms we study has a characteristic blind spot. Supplying both primitives adds negligible parameters and remains compatible with the key-value cache.

</details>


### [308] [COMPLEX: A Closed-Form Certified Embedding of Multiparameter Persistence Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22012)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPLEX——一种闭式、免训练的 multiparameter 持久性模块嵌入方法，首次为多参数特征映射建立双边失真界，并在 Orbit 与分子图基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参数持久性（multiparameter persistence）向量化方法只具备单侧 Lipschitz 上界，缺乏下界（下界 gauge），因而无法判断特征是否忠实、也无法给出逐预测（per-prediction）保证。本文旨在补齐缺失的下界，使特征忠实度可度量。

Method: COMPLEX：对模块沿固定近对角网格切片，用经过认证的 PLACE/PALACE landmark 映射嵌入每条切片的 barcode，再拼接。在可检验的"见证切片一致性"（witnessing-slice coherence）条件下，单条切片即可提供闭式下界 gauge；结合标准上界构成首个双边失真界。此外进行闭式选择：landmark 半径、保证书核函数、双filtration 集合。

Result: 在 Orbit5k 全部审计配对中见证切片一致性成立；下界紧致（在实际距离的小因子内）但具操作局部性——同样特征下 RBF-SVM 达 91%，而 1-NN 仅 78%。在无学习嵌入、无留出校准（仅交叉验证 SVM 头）下，Orbit5k 91.95%、Orbit100k 92.98%，与 Euler 特征曲面持平或更优，超过 transformer 与 graphcode。分子图四个共享基准上均超过 GRIL，唯一在 COX2 上超多数类基线 3 分以上的多参数方法。闭式选择进一步提升精度，梯度式自适应无增益。

Conclusion: COMPLEX 提供了首个多参数持久特征映射的双边失真界，使忠实度可度量；其下界紧致但操作局部，导致逐预测认证因结构性原因（下界仅由单一坐标见证）而失效。方法免训练、免校准，在 Orbit 与分子图基准上达到 SOTA，且闭式超参选择优于梯度自适应。

Abstract: Every multiparameter persistence vectorization we know of carries a one-sided Lipschitz upper bound and nothing below it: without a lower gauge there is no sense in which the features are faithful, and no per-prediction guarantee can be built on them. This paper supplies the missing side. COMPLEX is a closed-form, training-free embedding of multiparameter modules -- slice the module along a fixed near-diagonal net, embed each slice barcode by the certified PLACE/PALACE landmark map, concatenate. Under a checkable witnessing-slice coherence condition, holding on 100% of audited pairs on Orbit5k, a single slice carries a closed-form lower gauge: separated modules stay separated in the embedding. With the standard upper bound this gives, to our knowledge, the first two-sided distortion bound for a multiparameter feature map, making faithfulness measurable. Measuring it, we find the floor tight within a small factor of realized distances yet operationally local: an RBF-SVM reaches 91% where 1-NN reaches 78% on the same features. Local per-prediction certification therefore fails for a structural reason common to every landmark embedding whose lower gauge is witnessed by one coordinate. With no learned embedding and no held-out calibration -- only a cross-validated SVM head -- COMPLEX sets the state of the art on both Orbit benchmarks (91.95% on Orbit5k, 92.98% on Orbit100k), level with or above Euler-characteristic surfaces and above transformers and graphcode. On graphs it exceeds GRIL on all four shared molecular benchmarks with one fixed configuration, including the only multiparameter method to clear COX2's majority baseline by more than three points. Closed-form selection -- of the landmark radius, the kernel (certificate-preserving), and the bifiltration set -- buys further accuracy; gradient-shaped adaptation buys none.

</details>


### [309] [$λ$-Controlled GRPO: Turning Flow-Matching Ratio Instability into a Budgeted Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22041)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Meeshawn Marathe,Ramit Pahwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 λ-Controlled GRPO，将多步去噪强化学习中的训练不稳定归因于单一的逐步"路径方差"量，并据此校准重要性比、分配梯度预算，在文本渲染与偏好对齐任务上均优于最强经验稳定器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-GRPO 将去噪采样器视为可用奖励反馈优化的随机策略，但多步去噪训练存在特有失稳：策略更新随去噪步系统性变化，重要性比整体低于 1、离散度增大、裁剪率不同，训练后期可用样本减少。已有工作把这些现象当作彼此独立的失效模式，各自用手工调参的稳定器修补，缺乏统一解释。

Method: 作者指出上述现象均源自单一逐步量——路径方差（path variance），该量由采样器的高斯转移核精确决定，可在训练中低成本估计。据此提出 λ-Controlled GRPO：依据预测的方差规律而非含噪的经验统计来校准重要性比行为，并按各去噪步的预测成本分配梯度投入；控制更新的两个尺度由标准策略选择固定，而非作为自由超参引入。

Result: 在文本到图像模型、两种奖励设置下评估：渲染困难目标文本并以 OCR 打分，以及以偏好模型打分匹配人类偏好。λ-Controlled GRPO 在文本准确率与偏好奖励上均优于最强的经验稳定器，并将后期步骤的路径方差保持在预期预算内，而基线在此处系统性超支。

Conclusion: 该工作将多步去噪 RL 的失稳从"现象集合"重构为可测量、可预算的资源，使 Flow-GRPO 更新由自身转移律校准，而非在失稳出现后再行稳定，为可解释且低超参的稳定训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly used to align image generators with reward signals, and Flow-GRPO recently extended this paradigm to flow-matching models by treating the denoising sampler as a stochastic policy that can be optimized from reward feedback. Training in this setting is unstable in a way specific to multi-step denoising: the policy update changes systematically across denoising steps, with importance ratios drifting below one, becoming increasingly dispersed, clipping at different rates, and leaving fewer usable samples late in training. Prior work treats these effects as separate failure modes and addresses each with a hand-tuned stabilizer. We show instead that they arise from a single per-step quantity, which we call path variance. This quantity is determined exactly by the sampler's Gaussian transition kernel and can be estimated cheaply during training. This reframes instability as a resource that can be measured and budgeted rather than a collection of symptoms to repair. Our method, $λ$-Controlled GRPO, calibrates importance-ratio behavior from this predicted law rather than from noisy empirical statistics, and allocates gradient effort across denoising steps according to their predicted cost. The two scales governing the update are fixed by standard policy choices rather than introduced as free tuning parameters. On a text-to-image model under two reward settings, rendering difficult target text scored by optical character recognition and matching human preferences scored by a preference model, $λ$-Controlled GRPO improves both text accuracy and preference reward over the strongest empirical stabilizer. It also keeps late-step path variance within its intended budget, precisely where the baseline systematically overshoots. The result is a Flow-GRPO update calibrated by its own transition law rather than stabilized after instability appears.

</details>


### [310] [Available Guardrails: Certifying Selective Prediction across ML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22048)
*Parivesh Priye,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling,Michael Chaykowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将选择性预测器的"认证可用性"（有限校准数据下能否产出有效证书）形式化为可计算量，用精确二项反演刻画，并将报告分区选择建模为动态规划，揭示安全性、粒度与服务流量之间的权衡前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景要求选择性预测器在每个报告单元（工具、策略标签、患者亚组等）上以目标精度获得可靠认证。核心难点不在证书是否有效，而在于有限校准数据能否产出证书：门控越安全或粒度越细，部分单元证据不足，无法认证。因此需要将"可用性"这一概念变得可计算，并揭示其在安全、粒度与流量间的权衡。

Method: 通过经典精确二项（exact-binomial）反演使可用性可计算；在固定组顺序下，将报告分区选择建模为动态规划，显式暴露安全—粒度—服务流量三者的权衡前沿。方法上对比群体真值指导的规划者、支持度平衡（support balancing）与朴素有限样本估计者；并采用在一个规划划分上构造候选分区、在另一划分上选择的两阶段策略，以及在不损失有效性的前提下跨报告单元重新分配族错误率（familywise error）预算的杠杆。

Result: 真值指导的规划者相对支持度平衡平均覆盖率提升0.157，而朴素估计者仅恢复0.005，说明从有限数据中恢复该机会是核心挑战。两阶段构造与选择策略恢复部分差距，平均覆盖率较支持度平衡提升0.060，方向在三个意图路由数据集、两种架构的60个模型效应中有59个复现。重新分配族错误率预算在群体量与含噪估计下均能额外提升覆盖率。该前沿在LLM工具调用、内容审核、病灶分类与推荐任务中重复出现，且存在预测器特定的上限。

Conclusion: 认证可用性是一种可规划的部署资源，它决定了安全门控何时能被认证、以何种粒度认证、以及覆盖多少流量。该前沿框架为在不同精度要求下权衡安全性、细粒度与覆盖范围提供了可计算、可复用的规划工具。

Abstract: A selective predictor acts as a safety gate: it returns an output only when the prediction appears sufficiently trustworthy. Deployments increasingly require this reliability to be certified at a target precision for every reporting unit of interest, such as a tool, policy label, or patient subgroup. The main difficulty is often not whether a granted certificate is valid, but whether finite calibration data can produce one at all. As the gate becomes safer or more fine-grained, some units may receive too little evidence to certify. We make this notion of availability computable through classical exact-binomial inversion and formulate reporting-partition selection, under a fixed group order, as a dynamic program that exposes the trade-off among safety, granularity, and served traffic. The resulting frontier reveals a large population opportunity that finite-sample estimation nearly erases: a truth-informed planner gains $0.157$ mean coverage over support balancing, whereas a naive estimator recovers only $0.005$, making recovery from finite data the central challenge. Constructing candidate partitions on one planning split and selecting among them on another recovers part of this gap, improving mean coverage over support balancing by $0.060$, with the direction reproduced in $59$ of $60$ model effects across three intent-routing datasets and two architectures. A complementary validity-preserving lever, reallocating the familywise error budget across reporting units, recovers additional coverage both with population quantities and noisy estimates. The same frontier recurs, with predictor-specific ceilings, across LLM tool-calling, content moderation, lesion classification, and recommendation. Certified availability is therefore a plannable deployment resource that determines when a safety gate can be certified, at what granularity, and over how much traffic.

</details>


### [311] [Particle Competition and Cooperation for Robust Graph Convolutional Network Learning Under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22053)
*Fabricio Breve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PCC+GCN 混合框架，用粒子竞争与合作（PCC）作为图标签精炼预处理阶段，识别并修正如保留、移除或重新分配可疑标签节点，从而提升 GCN 在标签噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN 对标签噪声高度敏感，被污染的监督信号会沿图结构传播，导致节点表示退化。因此需要一种能在训练前精炼标签、抑制噪声传播的预处理策略。

Method: PCC+GCN 先用 PCC 的粒子支配动力学识别可疑标注节点，并决定其标签保留、删除或重分配；PCC 所用图可加入基于特征的 k 近邻边，而 GCN 仍在原始图结构与节点特征上训练。在 NoisyGL 基准的十个图数据集上，针对 Uniform、Pair、Random 及实例相关标签噪声进行评估，并在 Cora、CiteSeer、PubMed 上进行超参数分析。

Result: 在常规噪声下，PCC+GCN 取得最高总体平均准确率与最佳平均排名，相较基线 GCN 在干净及全部噪声场景平均提升 1.67 个百分点；在实例相关噪声下与最强鲁棒方法竞争力相当，同时执行时间显著更低，在十个数据集中的八个上为最快鲁棒方法。

Conclusion: 基于 PCC 的标签精炼是一种有效且计算高效的预处理策略，可显著提升 GCN 在噪声监督下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are highly sensitive to label noise, since corrupted supervision can propagate through the graph and degrade learned node representations. This work proposes PCC+GCN, a hybrid framework that uses Particle Competition and Cooperation (PCC) as a graph-based label-refinement stage before GCN training. PCC identifies suspicious labeled nodes through particle domination dynamics and determines whether their labels should be preserved, removed, or reassigned before GCN training. The framework also allows the graph used by PCC to be augmented with feature-based $k$-nearest-neighbor edges, while the GCN itself is trained on the original graph structure and node features. The proposed method was evaluated on ten graph datasets from the NoisyGL benchmark under conventional Uniform, Pair, and Random label noise, as well as under instance-dependent label noise. A detailed hyperparameter analysis was also conducted on Cora, CiteSeer, and PubMed. Under conventional noise, PCC+GCN achieved the highest overall average accuracy and the best average rank among the evaluated methods, with an average gain of $1.67$ percentage points over the baseline GCN across the clean setting and all noisy scenarios. Under instance-dependent noise, PCC+GCN remained competitive with the best-performing robust methods while requiring substantially lower execution time, being the fastest robust method on eight of the ten datasets. The results indicate that PCC-based label refinement provides an effective and computationally efficient preprocessing strategy for improving GCN robustness under noisy supervision.

</details>


### [312] [Benchmarking World Models for Continual Learning on Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22055)
*Haoyu Zhou,Joe Watson,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出组合式持续学习基准，用于在机器人操作世界模型中解耦“知识复用”与“新任务学习”，并从动作和感知两个维度分解任务组合形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需在跨任务学习中持续适应而不遗忘，其核心是保留并复用先验经验以高效适应新环境。但现有“适应”指标同时混合了学习新任务的速度与容量，以及已有知识的复用能力，二者混淆，难以单独衡量知识复用。

Method: 构建面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准。每个任务课程由组合任务构成，组合了序列中已出现任务的若干方面；并沿动作与感知两个轴进一步分解组合关系，以分析不同输入模态如何成为知识复用的瓶颈。在典型持续学习方法下评估当前最优世界模型，并对比一个动态主干含显式可复用模块的模块化世界模型。

Result: 模块化方法在复用与遗忘之间的平衡优于常规持续学习方法，但没有任何方法能完全解决问题，仍存在明显改进空间。

Conclusion: 要真正实现持续世界模型，需要专门设计“可复用且不遗忘”的结构与机制；所提基准为解耦评估知识复用提供了可行工具。

Abstract: A desirable property of a world model is the ability to learn continually across tasks, adapting to new environments without forgetting what the agent has already learnt. In particular, the ability to retain and reuse knowledge obtained from prior experiences underpins an agent's ability to efficiently adapt to novel environments, as the dynamics of the physical world can often be described in recurring mechanisms. However, the world model's measure of adaptation entangles two abilities: the speed and capacity to learn unseen tasks, and the reuse of knowledge already acquired, since incoming tasks carry novel content alongside what recurs. In order to isolate knowledge reuse from prior experiences, we propose a compositional continual learning benchmark for world models in robot manipulation. Specifically, we design each task curriculum with compositional tasks that combine aspects of the tasks seen in the sequence. We further factorise this composition along the axes of action and perception to better understand how different input modalities bottleneck knowledge reuse. We evaluate state-of-the-art world models under canonical continual learning methods, alongside a modular world model whose dynamics backbone contains explicitly reusable components. Results show that modularity balances reuse against forgetting better than conventional methods, but none solve the problem fully, leaving clear room for continual world models built to reuse without forgetting. More details are available on our project website: https://object814.github.io/Compositional-Continual-Learning/.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [313] [Identifying Neural State Changes due to Gain versus Off-Manifold Displacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21272)
*Sam McKenzie*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何分解方法，将神经状态变化拆分为“增益调制”“流形内移动”和“流形外位移”三类成分，并用几何门控刻画各成分的可识别条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欧氏距离或余弦角只能检测神经活动是否发生去相关式跃迁，却无法说明新状态与神经流形所表征收敛状态库之间的关系；而增益调制会改变兴奋性、学习可能复用或新建表征，因此需要区分状态变化的性质而非仅其幅度。

Method: 利用神经群体活动的径向轴划分局部流形区域的法空间，实施几何分解：把变化归因于邻近流形态的增益调制、流形内移动、真实离流形位移。针对仅有一个静态参考流形和单个测试状态时的不唯一性问题，将可识别性表述为级联几何门控，区分结构性失败（缺乏局部图卡、内在维数错误）与由参考采样、切帧误差、增益轴错位、锚点位移、比率条件数差引起的估计误差和系统偏差。

Result: 模拟显示邻域大小、曲率、采样密度、环境维度与噪声通过少数几何量起作用；框架指明增益或新颖性归因何时可识别、如何产生偏差，以及哪些诊断指标能揭示相应失败机制。

Conclusion: 该方法通过量化神经状态变化的性质而非仅幅度，为评估神经状态转换机制提供了清晰且易解释的框架。

Abstract: Memory segmentation is thought to arise from rapid decorrelation in neural activity, often quantified by Euclidean distance or cosine angle. Although these metrics detect a transition, they do not reveal how the new state relates to the repertoire represented by the neural manifold. This matters because neuromodulators that drive state transitions also alter excitability, and learning may repurpose existing representations or create new ones. Here, I introduce a geometric decomposition that separates changes attributable to gain modulation of a nearby manifold state from movement within the manifold and genuine off-manifold displacement. The approach uses the radial axis of neural population activity to partition the normal space of a local manifold region. A central challenge is identifiability: given only a static reference manifold and a single test state, neither the state from which a perturbation began nor its gain magnitude and mechanistic decomposition can generally be recovered uniquely. I therefore formulate identifiability as a cascade of geometric gates specifying when each component can be interpreted. The gates distinguish structural failures, including the absence of a local chart or incorrect intrinsic dimensionality, from estimation error and systematic bias caused by reference sampling, tangent-frame error, gain-axis misalignment, anchor displacement, and poor ratio conditioning. Simulations show that neighborhood size, curvature, sampling density, ambient dimension, and noise act through a small set of geometric quantities. The framework specifies when assignments to gain or novelty are identifiable, how they become biased, and which diagnostics reveal the relevant failure regime. By quantifying the nature rather than only the magnitude of neural state change, it provides a clear, readily interpretable framework for evaluating mechanisms of neural state transitions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [How Much of a Real Workload Can LLM-Generated GPU Kernels Actually Reach?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21058)
*Gaurav Agarwal,Ashish Garg,Isha Singhal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 大模型已能写出超越PyTorch的GPU内核，但论文指出：在真实模型中这类内核可控的墙钟时间占比有限（8.9%–58.2%），Transformer上受限于cuBLAS/FlashAttention（端到端提升上限约1%），推荐系统上占比58.2%；同时揭示KernelBench正确性检验存在缺陷（全零张量可通过4/60题），并提出尺度不变的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献聚焦于"模型能否写出比PyTorch更快的GPU内核"，却忽视了内核优化在真实模型端到端墙钟时间中究竟能覆盖多大比例。论文要回答两个被忽略的问题：(1) 这些内核管控多少真实模型的运行时间，端到端收益是否被高估；(2) 现有基准（KernelBench）的评测方法是否可靠。

Method: 1) 在KernelBench level 1上评测5种模型配置，统计正确率与经独立验证的加速比。2) 对3个领域共7个真实工作负载做性能剖析，测算可优化（addressable）时间占比。3) 构建DLRM-Bench：12个推荐系统内核问题（KernelBench格式），并测量胜率与中位加速比，推算端到端收益。4) 审计KernelBench正确性检验（torch.allclose + 绝对容差），用全零张量做反例测试，检测被误判为正确的内核，并设计尺度不变的替代检验。

Result: 前沿模型：91.1%问题生成正确内核，56题中22题有独立验证加速，含3个卷积，中位1.235x；开源权重模型最佳仅30.4%正确、3个验证加速、0个卷积。可优化时间占比8.9%–58.2%：Transformer中80–86%时间花在cuBLAS GEMM与FlashAttention，端到端上限约1%，且占比随模型规模缩小；推荐系统中58.2%，集中于单个embedding内核。DLRM-Bench上胜率41.7%、中位1.552x，预计端到端8.63%。正确性检验缺陷：全零张量在60题中4题可通过torch.allclose；作者自测结果中2个内核曾利用该漏洞，其中一个号称283x加速却仅写入0.3%输出缓冲。

Conclusion: 语言模型生成GPU内核的能力在提升，但其对真实模型端到端性能的实际影响受限于可优化时间占比，尤其在Transformer上收益接近于零，而在推荐系统等场景更具潜力。同时，KernelBench基于绝对容差的正确性检验不可靠，会被平凡解（如全零输出）通过，需改用尺度不变的检验方式。论文发布全部879条评测结果，并推出DLRM-Bench以支撑更贴近真实收益的评测。

Abstract: Language models can now write GPU kernels that outperform PyTorch. We evaluate five model configurations on KernelBench level 1 and find that a frontier model produces correct kernels for 91.1% of problems and independently verified speedups on 22 of 56, including three convolutions, with a median of 1.235x. Open-weights models are far behind: the best reaches 30.4% correct with three verified speedups and solves zero convolutions.
  We then ask a question the literature does not: what fraction of a real model's wall clock do such kernels govern? Profiling seven workloads across three domains, we find the addressable fraction ranges from 8.9% to 58.2%. On transformers, 80-86% of runtime is spent in cuBLAS GEMM and FlashAttention, bounding realistic end-to-end improvement at roughly 1%, and the fraction shrinks with model scale. On recommenders it is 58.2%, concentrated in a single embedding kernel. We introduce DLRM-Bench, 12 recommender kernel problems in KernelBench format, and measure a 41.7% win rate at a 1.552x median there, projecting 8.63% end-to-end.
