  Separately, we show that KernelBench's correctness check (torch.allclose with an absolute tolerance) is satisfied by a tensor of zeros on 4 of 60 level-1 problems. Two kernels in our own results exploited this before we detected them, including one scored at 283x that wrote 0.3% of its output buffer. We propose scale-invariant replacements and release all 879 evaluations.

</details>


### [315] [DLB: Distributed Load Balancing at Scale for Generative AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21079)
*Santiago R. Balseiro,Bartek Wydrowski,Sameer Agarwal,David Applegate,Aaron Archer,Soheil Hassas Yeganeh,Alex Iriza,Bobby Kleinberg,Balasubramanian Sivan,Pranav Vaish,Oscar Zegarra,Wenxin Zhang,Vahab Mirrokni,Amin Vahdat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 DLB（分布式负载均衡器），通过点对点探测与在线延迟模型学习，在异构硬件与多阶段生成式 AI 负载下最小化端到端延迟，并给出稳定性与全局性能的理论保证；仿真与 Google 22 个月生产部署表明其显著优于现有方法，中位延迟降低 17%、p95 降低 13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心依赖稀缺昂贵的 GPU/TPU，后端基础设施压力巨大。对于生成式 AI 等具有服务时间异构、多阶段处理特征的负载，传统负载均衡方法效果不佳，往往只能依靠成本高昂的过度配置来满足服务等级目标（SLO），因此需要面向大规模异构负载的新型负载均衡方案。

Method: DLB 采用可扩展的分布式架构，通过点对点探测（peer-to-peer probing）在广域分布式基础设施中实时感知服务器容量；系统持续学习延迟模型，用于估计路由决策的延迟影响，从而适配异构硬件与多样模型架构。作者还对路由算法进行了理论分析，证明其随时间推移的稳定性与全局性能保证，并通过大规模仿真及生产部署进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果显示 DLB 相较最先进的负载均衡算法有显著增益；在 Google 为期 22 个月的生产部署中，DLB 支撑了数千个机器学习模型、每秒数百万请求的大规模生成式 AI 推理。生产迁移分析表明延迟下降具有统计显著性：中位延迟降低 17%，p95 尾部延迟降低 13%。

Conclusion: DLB 是一种面向大规模异构生成式 AI 负载的分布式负载均衡系统，兼具理论保证与工程可行性。生产经验证明其可在不依赖过度配置的情况下显著降低端到端延迟，并给出了实际部署中的设计取舍与实践经验。

Abstract: The reliance on scarce and expensive accelerators such as GPUs and TPUs in modern datacenters places unprecedented demands on backend infrastructure. For workloads characterized by heterogeneous service times and complex multi-stage processing, such as Generative AI, conventional load balancing techniques are often inadequate, relying heavily on costly overprovisioning to maintain service level objectives. This paper introduces DLB, the Distributed Load Balancer, a novel system designed to minimize end-to-end user latency for large-scale, heterogeneous workloads.
  DLB employs a scalable, distributed design with peer-to-peer probing to maintain real-time visibility into server capacity across large-scale, geographically distributed infrastructure. The system continuously learns latency models to estimate the latency impact of routing decisions, allowing it to effectively manage heterogeneous hardware and diverse model architectures. We provide a novel theoretical analysis of our routing algorithms that establishes their stability and global performance guarantees over time. We also evaluate DLB through extensive simulations, which show substantial gains compared to state-of-the-art load balancing algorithms. Finally, following a 22-month deployment of DLB at Google, where it facilitates large-scale Generative AI inference for thousands of different machine learning models and millions of requests per second, we detail the design choices and practical experiences gained from the system in production. Analysis of production migrations demonstrates that DLB yields statistically significant latency reductions compared to the legacy baseline, including a 17\% decrease in median latency and a 13\% decrease at the p95 tail.

</details>


### [316] [SensorWF: A FAIR Generalizable Workflow Framework for Scientific Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21110)
*Logan Luna,Joseph Rigo,Kellan Shew,Raul Alejandro Vargas-Acosta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SensorWF，一个面向通用科学时间序列分析的 FAIR 标注工作流框架。其五模块可复用核心（M1-M5）通过领域适配器模式隔离领域逻辑，仅需约 170-500 行的 M1 适配器即可在航天器遥测、动态心电和大气气候三个领域复用同一代码库，并生成 PROV-O/ProvONE 溯源轨迹与 SSN/SOSA 对齐的 OWL 本体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传感器数据广泛存在于航天器工程、临床医学和大气科学等领域。各领域通常需构建流水线以完成原始档案摄取、数据质量评估、特征工程、语义标注与溯源记录，但现有实现多为单体、领域特定的脚本，隐含假设多、跨领域复用性差，缺乏可推广的通用分析框架。

Method: 提出 SensorWF 框架：核心为五模块（M1-M5）可复用架构，模块间采用带类型约束的输入/输出契约；通过领域适配器模式将所有领域特定逻辑隔离在 M1 中，使 M2-M5 可跨学科以相同方式运行；领域假设以机器可读的组件注册表编码，实现跨传感器领域复用而无需修改分析核心。框架运行时生成带 SHA-256 校验和（针对所有文件路径实体）的 PROV-O/ProvONE 溯源轨迹，并输出 SSN/SOSA 对齐的 OWL 本体。在航天器遥测、动态心电和大气气候三个领域实例化验证，并加入合成故障注入与多检测器异常检测作为用例扩展。

Result: 单一代码库仅通过各约 170-500 行的 M1 适配器参数化，即可支持不同采样率、通道数和故障分类体系的跨领域分析流水线；框架成功生成溯源轨迹与本体工件。所有代码、组件注册表、本体工件及数据集均作为开放科学对象发布，代码库地址为 https://purl.archive.org/sensor-wf。

Conclusion: SensorWF 通过适配器模式与机器可读注册表实现了科学时间序列分析的跨领域通用性，在保持 FAIR 原则与溯源可追溯性的同时显著提升代码复用率，为多领域传感器数据分析提供了可推广、可复现的框架基础。

Abstract: Scientific sensor data is foundational across disciplines including spacecraft engineering, clinical medicine, and atmospheric science. In each context, pipelines are constructed to ingest raw archives, assess data quality, perform feature engineering and semantic annotation, and record provenance. However, these pipelines are often implemented as monolithic, domain-specific scripts with implicit assumptions and limited reusability across fields. This work introduces SensorWF, a FAIR-annotated workflow framework for generalizable scientific time-series analysis. The framework features a five-module reusable core (M1-M5) with typed input/output contracts. A domain adapter pattern isolates all domain-specific logic within M1, enabling modules M2-M5 to operate identically across disciplines. Domain assumptions are encoded in a machine-readable component registry, enabling reuse across sensor domains without modifying the analytical core. SensorWF also generates runtime PROV-O/ProvONE provenance traces with SHA-256 checksums for all file-path entities and emits SSN/SOSA-aligned OWL ontologies as primary outputs. To assess generalizability, SensorWF is instantiated in three distinct scientific domains: spacecraft telemetry, ambulatory ECG, and atmospheric climate, with synthetic fault injection and multi-detector anomaly detection demonstrated as use-case extensions. Results show that a single codebase, parameterized solely through M1 adapters of approximately 170-500 lines each, supports analytical pipelines across domains with varying sampling rates, channel counts, and fault taxonomies. All code, the component registry, ontology artifacts, and datasets are made available as an open scientific object. Our codebase is publicly available at https://purl.archive.org/sensor-wf.

</details>


### [317] [Cloud-Side Transactional Orchestration Framework for Resource-Constrained Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21143)
*Pravin Nagare,Aditya Sabbineni,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出面向资源受限嵌入式终端的T-BFF事务编排框架，将状态机与微服务编排迁移至云端，并以"双握手"协议结合非易失闪存实现重启后状态恢复，在ARM Cortex-A53上把最大堆占用降低35%、端到端事务时延改善40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低端消费电子在数字商务生态中受限于有限CPU占空比与堆碎片，传统端侧中间件为维护多步事务状态机需要高"网络通信频度"，在不稳定家庭网络下造成时延升高与事务失败风险。

Method: 构建云端侧的事务型BFF编排框架：把事务状态机与微服务编排上移至云侧层以降低客户端开销；设计"双握手"协议，利用非易失闪存实现硬件重启后的状态恢复。

Result: 在ARM Cortex-A53平台上，最大堆使用量减少35%，端到端事务时延改善40%。

Conclusion: 该框架为在旧有硬件上维持事务完整性提供了可扩展、可持续的方案蓝本，适用于2026年IoT环境。

Abstract: As digital commerce ecosystems expand into low-end consumer electronics (CE), hardware constraints-specifically limited CPU duty cycles and volatile heap fragmentation-become significant bottlenecks for complex transactional flows. Traditional on-device middleware requires high "network chattiness" to manage multi-step state machines, leading to increased latency and potential transaction failure on unstable residential networks. This paper proposes a novel Transactional Backend-for-Front-End (T-BFF) Orchestration Framework designed for resource-constrained embedded platforms. By migrating the transactional state machine and microservice orchestration to a cloud-side layer, we achieve a significant reduction in client-side overhead. Our framework introduces a "Double-Handshake" protocol utilizing non-volatile flash memory for state recovery after hardware reboots. Experimental results on an ARM Cortex-A53 platform demonstrate a 35% reduction in maximum heap usage and a 40% improvement in end-to-end transaction latency. This framework provides a scalable, sustainable blueprint for maintaining transactional integrity on legacy hardware in the 2026 IoT landscape.

</details>


### [318] [AI-Driven Scientific Computing Workflows: A Systems Review of Orchestration, Execution, Reproducibility and Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21162)
*Jamie J. Alnasir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从系统视角综述了AI与科学工作流融合的研究，提出从计算阶段到协同自适应控制的AI参与深度连续谱，并围绕五大系统关注点组织需求，梳理代表性系统与应用，并指出开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI日益嵌入包含模拟、数据处理、优化、可视化和实验的科学计算工作流，学习模型可作为显式组件、保持持久状态并在自适应环境下影响后续计算。已有工作分别刻画了科学工作流管理系统、动态与引导式工作流、AI-HPC耦合模式及机器学习生命周期，但这些领域常被割裂讨论，缺乏统一的系统视角。

Method: 采用系统视角的综述方法，将上述分散领域整合。区分四类工作流：传统科学工作流、机器学习流水线、AI耦合HPC工作流和更广泛的自动化研究工作流；提出描述AI参与深度的连续谱（从计算阶段到协同自适应工作流控制）；围绕五个关注点组织系统需求：控制与编排、计算与执行、数据与模型状态、可复现性与溯源、治理与保障。

Result: 梳理了代表性系统与应用，包括AI引导的分子模拟、药物与材料发现、模拟-代理耦合以及分布式自驱动实验室；并从科学进展、执行成本、数据移动、资源使用、韧性和决策可追溯性等维度讨论工作流层面的评估。

Conclusion: 总结并识别出若干开放问题：动态工作流表示、状态感知恢复、异构调度、可互操作数据平面、模型介导的决策溯源以及可复现的自适应执行。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded within scientific computing workflows that combine simulation, data processing, optimisation, visualisation and experimental or observational components. Learned models may serve as explicit workflow components, retain persistent state and, in adaptive settings, influence subsequent computation. Existing work has characterised scientific workflow management systems, dynamic and steered workflows, AI--HPC coupling motifs and the machine-learning lifecycle, although these areas are often discussed separately. This review brings them together from a systems perspective. We distinguish conventional scientific workflows, machine-learning pipelines, AI-coupled high-performance computing (HPC) workflows and broader automated research workflows, and propose a continuum describing the depth of AI participation from a computational stage to co-adaptive workflow control. The associated systems requirements are organised around five concerns: control and orchestration; compute and execution; data and model state; reproducibility and provenance; and governance and assurance. Representative systems and applications include AI-steered molecular simulation, drug and materials discovery, simulation--surrogate coupling and distributed self-driving laboratories. Workflow-level evaluation is considered in terms of scientific progress, execution cost, data movement, resource use, resilience and decision traceability. We conclude by identifying open problems in dynamic workflow representation, state-aware recovery, heterogeneous scheduling, interoperable data planes, model-mediated decision provenance and reproducible adaptive execution.

</details>


### [319] [Brain API: An Intent-Aware Control Plane for Policy-Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21299)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Brain API——一种面向意图感知、策略治理的智能体系统控制平面，核心贡献是"决策工件"：对"意图如何转化为可执行计划"进行持久化、版本化、可审计的记录。原型在外部策略语料上评估，OPA Gatekeeper 42/42 一致，Cedar 策略测试暴露 3 项模型缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云与分布式系统采用以资源为中心的控制抽象（服务、部署、网络流、执行图），而智能体与工具增强系统已转向意图驱动、自适应执行。现有控制平面、工作流引擎与服务网格缺乏意图级决策治理抽象：无法将高层目标表示为一等控制对象，无法对"意图→执行计划"的映射施加策略，也无法生成"为何选择某执行路径而非其他备选"的可审计记录。控制逻辑因此散落在应用代码中，导致系统脆弱、不透明且难以治理。

Method: 提出 Brain API 意图感知控制平面，核心为"决策工件"（decision artifact）：持久、版本化、可审计地记录意图转化为可执行计划的过程，包括适用的策略、被评估的能力、被拒绝的备选方案及原因。动机用例为"智能体数据集"——数据集作为受驻留、合规与成本约束的策略治理能力参与。原型评估采用两个作者未编写的第三方策略语料：OPA Gatekeeper 约束库与 Cedar 示例策略（通过与其参考实现的差分测试进行标注）。

Result: 在 OPA Gatekeeper 约束库上，对 42 个可编码用例全部与库自身已发布裁决一致（19 允许、23 拒绝）。在 Cedar 示例策略上，来自刻意相异领域的测试暴露了模型的 3 项缺陷，其中包括一项"默认允许"假设，该假设会反转所有授权策略。评估覆盖策略过滤与选择；上下文信号与排序仍属设计主张，负载下的决策延迟未量化。

Conclusion: Brain API 通过决策工件为智能体系统提供意图级决策治理与可审计性，在跨领域策略语料上初步验证了策略过滤与选择的正确性，并借由异构领域测试发现并规避了严重建模缺陷。上下文信号、排序机制及负载延迟仍需后续量化研究。

Abstract: Contemporary cloud and distributed systems expose control through resource-centric abstractions: services, deployments, network flows, execution graphs. Agentic and tool-augmented systems have meanwhile shifted application logic toward intent-driven, adaptive execution. Existing control planes, workflow engines and service meshes lack abstractions for intent-level decision governance: they cannot represent high-level goals as first-class control objects, cannot enforce policy over the mapping from intent to execution plan, and cannot produce auditable records of why one execution path was chosen over its alternatives. Control logic is therefore embedded in application code, leaving systems brittle, opaque and hard to govern.
  We propose Brain API, an intent-aware control plane for policy-governed agentic systems. Its central contribution is the decision artifact: a durable, versioned, auditable record of how an intent became an executable plan, capturing which policies applied, which capabilities were evaluated, which alternatives were rejected, and why. A motivating use case is agentic datasets: datasets participating as policy-governed capabilities under residency, compliance and cost constraints.
  We evaluate a prototype of the decision layer against two external policy corpora we did not author. On the OPA Gatekeeper constraint library it agrees with the library's own published verdicts on 42 of 42 encodable cases, 19 admit and 23 deny. On Cedar example policies, labeled by differential testing against its reference implementation, a deliberately dissimilar domain exposed three defects in our model, including a default-allow assumption that would have inverted every authorization policy. The evaluation covers policy filtering and selection; context-signal and ranking remain design claims, and decision latency under load is unquantified.

</details>


### [320] [TokaGLINT: A Scalable GPU-Tailored Implicit Solver for Full 3D Tokamak Electromagnetic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21366)
*Zifan Yang,Haoyuan Zhang,Jialin Li,Wu Yuan,Xiazhen Liu,Jian Zhang,Jianyuan Xiao,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TokaGLINT：面向全3D托卡马克电磁场计算的GPU加速隐式求解器，通过层次化区域分解与张量结构快速局部求解器协同设计，实现万卡级并行与高稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全3D托卡马克等离子体模拟中电磁场求解规模大、时长长，需要高效的大规模GPU并行计算能力，同时保持长时间稳定性。

Method: 协同设计层次化区域分解与快速精确局部求解器：层次化划分匹配卡内细粒度子域；针对曲线坐标辛CN-FDTD离散的3D Maxwell方程设计张量结构求解器；结合面向卡内多子域结构的自动算子融合与批处理，通过离散变换解耦未知量并利用张量结构计算提升硬件利用率。

Result: 扩展至10,000+ GPU，弱扩展效率90.1%、强扩展效率53.9%；单节点相较无预条件BiCGStab基线（HIP版HYPRE）加速2.67倍；在SymPIC中的EAST托卡马克模拟得到验证。

Conclusion: TokaGLINT为全3D托卡马克电磁场计算提供可扩展且保持辛离散长时间稳定性的GPU隐式求解方案，支持高保真长时程等离子体建模。

Abstract: We introduce TokaGLINT, a GPU-accelerated implicit solver for electromagnetic field computations in full 3D tokamak simulations, aimed at efficient large-scale parallel GPU computing. Its central innovation lies in the co-design of hierarchical domain decomposition and a fast exact local solver, where hierarchical partitioning is tailored to match fine-grained intra-card subdomains and exploit the tensor-based solver dedicated to curvilinear-coordinate symplectic CN-FDTD-discretized 3D Maxwell equations. Backed by automated operator fusion and batching customized for the intra-card multi-subdomain structure, the solver decouples unknowns through discrete transformations and leverages tensor-structured computations to achieve high hardware utilization, while preserving the long-time stability characteristic of symplectic discretizations. TokaGLINT scales the electromagnetic field solve beyond 10,000 GPUs, achieving 90.1% weak and 53.9% strong scaling efficiency, while delivering a 2.67X single-node speedup over an unpreconditioned BiCGStab baseline (HIP-enabled HYPRE). It is validated in EAST tokamak simulations within the SymPIC plasma simulation code, enabling high-fidelity long-duration modeling.

</details>


### [321] [Weave: Fine-Grained Dynamic SM Scheduling in an MoE Megakernel for Compute-Communication Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21483)
*Ziyu Huang,Yangjie Zhou,Chenhao Zhu,Zihan Liu,Jinyu Liu,Shulai Zhang,Xingxun Tang,Hongzhe Yan,Xinhao Luo,Minyi Guo,Xiu Lin,Yinghao Yu,Guodong Yang,Liping Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Jingwen Leng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Weave 是首个在 MoE 专家并行推理中实现细粒度动态 SM 调度的重叠系统，依据运行时路由结果逐层、逐 GPU 决定通信与计算资源分配，在 4×H100 上取得平均 2.89× 的 MoE 层加速与 1.33× 的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 专家并行推理中，dispatch 与 combine 通信开销大。现有系统依靠通信-计算重叠、将 SM 静态切分给通信和计算来降低开销，但仍存在两类资源浪费：空间上最优 SM 切分比例取决于每层路由结果且随层、随 GPU 变化，固定策略会浪费 NVLink 带宽或计算吞吐；时间上复杂的 MoE 数据依赖产生气泡，使 SM 空闲。

Method: 提出 Weave，基于持久化 megakernel 内运行的轻量级代价模型，利用路由完成后通信量与计算量可预知的特性进行动态调度：空间调度器按通信/计算吞吐比将 SM 划分为通信 worker 与计算 worker；时间调度器协调两组 worker 以最小化 SM 空闲。

Result: 在 4×H100 SXM GPU 上、六个主流 MoE 模型、对比五个 SOTA 基线：MoE 层几何平均加速 2.89×，端到端几何平均加速 1.33×。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度、运行时的逐层逐 GPU 动态 SM 调度，Weave 有效缓解了固定切分策略下的空间资源错配与时间气泡问题，显著提升了 MoE 专家并行推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference under expert parallelism (EP) turns each MoE layer into a distributed computation with costly dispatch and combine communication. State-of-the-art systems reduce this cost through communication-computation overlap, splitting the GPU's SMs for communication and computation respectively. However, this approach still leaves GPU resources wasted along two dimensions. Spatially, the best SM split is determined by each layer's routing result and varies across layers and GPUs, so fixed policies mismatch the workload and waste either NVLink bandwidth or compute throughput. Temporally, complex MoE data dependencies introduce bubbles that leave SMs idle.
  We present Weave, to our knowledge the first MoE overlap system that performs fine-grained dynamic SM scheduling - deciding per layer and per GPU by routing results at runtime. Once routing completes, each layer's communication and computation volumes become known; Weave exploits this predictability through a lightweight cost model running inside the persistent megakernel: a spatial scheduler partitions SMs into communication workers and computation workers to match the communication/computation throughput ratio, and a temporal scheduler coordinates the two worker groups to minimize SM idleness. On 4x H100 SXM GPUs across six mainstream MoE models, Weave achieves a 2.89x geometric-mean MoE-layer speedup and a 1.33x geometric-mean end-to-end speedup over five state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [322] [HyperParallel-FSDP: Topology-Aware Fully Sharded Training with Layout-Driven Muon on Ascend SuperPods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21594)
*Mo Sun,Yifan Yao,Yanwei Liu,Luobin Liu,Zhenzhang Yang,Kaisheng Wang,Xiangyu Meng,Chen Li,Xizheng Pang,Huilan Li,Xinglei Xu,Yushi Cui,Xinyao Lin,Kaiqi Chen,Jie Zhang,Zeke Wang,Teng Su*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperParallel-FSDP 提出将分片语义仅表达在 autograd 之上的张量 API 边界，用一份分片计划支持生产与校验双模式，并结合拓扑感知 FSDP 与布局驱动分布式 Muon，在 Atlas 900 A3 上从 16 die 扩展到 384 卡（768 rank），相比 PyTorch FSDP2 与 Megatron DDP 平均步时分别降低 29.7% 与 25.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声明式 SPMD 方案（PyTorch DTensor）在每个算子下进行派遣，带来重复的派遣与元数据开销，且缺少低成本端到端校验路径；同时 FSDP 依赖显式参数打包/解包、Muon 的整矩阵正交化与参数分片冲突，二者均与两层超节点拓扑不匹配。

Method: 核心洞见是分布式张量只需在 autograd 之上的张量 API 边界表达分片语义，使微分与内核作用于普通张量。系统包含三部分：(1) 双模式 DTensor 执行，同一分片计划同时服务生产模式（一次性布局解析、稳态无派遣开销）与校验模式（端到端元数据传播、fail-fast 检查、梯度等价测试）；(2) 拓扑感知 FSDP，含超节点内零拷贝集合通信、超节点间融合归约、跨层反向流水以避免等待慢链路；(3) 布局驱动的分布式 Muon，采用分片推导的通信组、去重正交化与形状融合 Newton-Schulz 迭代。

Result: 在 Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD 上，系统从 16 die 扩展至 384 卡（768 rank），505B 参数 MoE 维持 421k tokens/s，FSDP 通信仅占步时 2.9%；平均步时较 PyTorch FSDP2 降低 29.7%，较 Megatron DDP 降低 25.5%，1000 步内 Pearson 相关系数高于 0.999997；分布式 Muon 相比对比系统 profiler 步时提升 5.4–16.0%。

Conclusion: 将分片语义上移至 autograd 之上的 API 边界可同时消除稳态派遣开销并提供统一校验能力；配合拓扑感知 FSDP 与布局驱动 Muon，可在超大规模超节点拓扑上实现高吞吐、低通信占比与高数值一致性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Declarative SPMD programming uses tensor sharding descriptions to drive distributed execution, separating parallelization from model code. However, the evaluated PyTorch DTensor stack dispatches every operator below autograd, incurring repeated dispatch and metadata costs, while lacking an inexpensive end-to-end validation path. Existing FSDP and distributed Muon implementations also mismatch two-tier supernode topologies: FSDP relies on explicit parameter packing and unpacking, and Muon's whole-matrix orthogonalization conflicts with parameter sharding.
  We observe that distributed tensors need only express sharding semantics at the tensor API boundary above autograd, allowing differentiation and kernels to operate on plain tensors. Based on this insight, we present HyperParallel-FSDP, featuring: (1) dual-mode DTensor execution, using one sharding plan for both a production mode with one-time layout resolution and no steady-state dispatch overhead, and a validation mode with end-to-end metadata propagation, fail-fast checks, and gradient-equivalence testing; (2) topology-aware FSDP, with zero-copy intra-supernode collectives, fused inter-supernode reduction, and a cross-layer backward pipeline that avoids waits on slow links; and (3) layout-driven distributed Muon, with sharding-derived communication groups, deduplicated orthogonalization, and shape-fused Newton-Schulz iterations.
  On Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD, HyperParallel-FSDP scales from 16 dies to 384 cards (768 ranks), sustaining 421k tokens/s for a 505B-parameter MoE while FSDP communication uses 2.9% of step time. It reduces mean step time by 29.7% versus PyTorch FSDP2 and 25.5% versus Megatron DDP, with Pearson correlation above 0.999997 over 1,000 steps. Distributed Muon improves profiler step time by 5.4-16.0% over competing systems. Source code is available at https://atomgit.com/mindspore/hyper-parallel.

</details>


### [323] [PoVD: Efficient Consensus Protocol based on Verifiable Delay Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21627)
*Rui Jiang,Xintong Ling,Bin Cao,Jiaheng Wang,Xiqi Gao,Zhi Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可验证延迟函数（VDF）的轻量级共识协议PoVD，在较弱网络假设下以低于PoW的分叉率维持相同吞吐量，且计算与通信开销低，适合资源受限节点与带宽受限网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识机制普遍存在计算成本高或通信开销大的问题，制约了区块链及去中心化应用的鲁棒性与可扩展性，尤其在资源受限和带宽受限场景下难以适用。

Method: 基于可验证延迟函数（VDF）设计轻量级共识协议PoVD，给出包含区块挖掘与验证规则的完整协议流程，并支持根据网络状况灵活调整出块时间分布；通过数学证明分析其在较弱网络假设下的分叉率与吞吐量表现，并通过实验评估计算与通信复杂度。

Result: 数学证明表明，在相对较弱的网络假设下，PoVD能以低于PoW的分叉率保持相同吞吐量；实验验证PoVD具有较低的计算与通信复杂度，并能提升区块链一致性。

Conclusion: PoVD是一种适用于资源受限节点与带宽受限网络的轻量级共识方案，在保证吞吐量的同时降低分叉率与开销。

Abstract: Consensus protocols ensure the robustness and scalability of blockchains and decentralized applications built on them. However, existing consensus mechanisms often impose high computational cost or require heavy communication overhead. To address these challenges, we propose proof of verifiable delay (PoVD), a lightweight consensus protocol based on the verifiable delay function (VDF). We present the detailed protocol of PoVD, including the block mining and verification rules, and illustrate how PoVD can flexibly adjust the block time distribution according to the network condition. Through mathematical proof, we point out that, under a relatively weak network assumption, PoVD can achieve a lower fork rate than PoW while maintaining the same throughput. Our experiments verify that PoVD exhibits low computation and communication complexity and can also improve the blockchain consistency, making it particularly suitable for resource-constrained nodes and bandwidth-limited networks.

</details>


### [324] [Distributed Balanced Butterfly Counting in Signed Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21848)
*Kiran Mekala,Apurba Das,Suman Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式平衡蝴蝶计数算法D-BBC，基于MPI+TBB混合框架，在15个真实数据集上较串行和现有多核方法取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡蝴蝶（balanced butterfly）是分析符号二分图和更高阶结构（如聚类系数、社区结构）的基础原语，但现有算法主要为串行实现，在大规模图上效率低。

Method: 提出分布式算法D-BBC，采用MPI+TBB混合框架：MPI负责进程间通信，Intel TBB负责节点内并行。

Result: 在15个真实数据集上评估。单节点分布式系统下，D-BBC相较串行BB2K平均加速1321倍，相较多核M-BBC加速16.2倍；端到端执行时间相较分布式基线S-Monarch最高加速23.58倍。

Conclusion: D-BBC验证了分布式方案的高效性，具备支撑大规模二分图上高性能符号模体分析的潜力。

Abstract: The balanced butterfly is a fundamental primitive for analyzing signed bipartite graphs and provides a basis for studying higher-order structural properties, such as clustering coefficients and community structure. Despite its importance, existing approaches primarily rely on serial algorithms for balanced butterfly counting, which become inefficient on large-scale graphs. To address this limitation, we propose a distributed algorithm, D-BBC, based on a hybrid MPI+TBB framework that exploits MPI for inter-process communication and Intel TBB for intra-node parallelism. We conduct an experimental assessment of the proposed approach across 15 real-world datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that, on a single-node distributed system, D-BBC achieves average speedups of 1321x and 16.2x over the serial BB2K and multi-core M-BBC implementations, respectively. Furthermore, D-BBC achieves a maximum speedup of 23.58x over the distributed baseline S-Monarch in terms of end-to-end execution time. These results demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed distributed approach and its potential to enable high- performance signed motif analysis on large-scale bipartite graphs.

</details>


### [325] [dSTAR: Straggler Tolerant and Byzantine Resilient Distributed SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07151)
*Jiahe Yan,Pratik Chaudhari,Leonard Kleinrock*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出轻量级分布式SGD方法dSTAR,通过选取前k个响应的worker并用集成中位数过滤偏差梯度,同时应对掉队者效应与拜占庭攻击,理论证明具备(α,f)-拜占庭弹性和线性收敛率,实验中保持高精度并优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式模型训练面临掉队者效应和拜占庭攻击两大挑战,在多节点协同训练时,需要在网络和系统故障下仍能及时、可靠地完成梯度聚合。已有拜占庭鲁棒方法在攻击下精度可能下降40-50%,因此需要兼顾鲁棒性与收敛效率的轻量方案。

Method: 提出dSTAR方法,用于分布式SGD。其核心是选择性梯度聚合:收集最先响应的前k个worker的更新,利用集成中位数计算偏差并据此过滤梯度,从而抑制掉队者影响并抵御拜占庭敌手。

Result: 理论上证明dSTAR具有(α,f)-拜占庭弹性并达到线性收敛率。多种场景下的实验表明dSTAR持续保持高精度,而其他拜占庭鲁棒方法在攻击下精度下降高达40-50%。

Conclusion: dSTAR是面向同时存在掉队者延迟与拜占庭故障的分布式训练环境的鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed model training needs to be adapted to challenges such as the straggler effect and Byzantine attacks. When coordinating the training process with multiple computing nodes, ensuring timely and reliable gradient aggregation amidst network and system malfunctions is essential. To tackle these issues, we propose \textit{dSTAR}, a lightweight and efficient approach for distributed stochastic gradient descent (SGD) that enhances robustness and convergence. \textit{dSTAR} selectively aggregates gradients by collecting updates from the first \(k\) workers to respond, filtering them based on deviations calculated using an ensemble median. This method not only mitigates the impact of stragglers but also fortifies the model against Byzantine adversaries. We theoretically establish that \textit{dSTAR} is (\(α, f\))-Byzantine resilient and achieves a linear convergence rate. Empirical evaluations across various scenarios demonstrate that \textit{dSTAR} consistently maintains high accuracy, outperforming other Byzantine-resilient methods that often suffer up to a 40-50\% accuracy drop under attack. Our results highlight \textit{dSTAR} as a robust solution for training models in distributed environments prone to both straggler delays and Byzantine faults.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [326] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WM-VS 世界模型框架，通过未来误差对齐的动作后果预测，实现闭环视觉伺服中的预测-控制匹配，在真实 7-DoF 系统上取得高成功率与跨目标零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服需要预测判断动作是否能减小任务误差，而不仅是动作是否合理；现有方法存在"预测-控制失配"（prediction-control mismatch）问题，即预测的合理性与控制目标不一致。

Method: 以目标为中心定义离线目标区域 DINOv2 对应关系，得到平移、尺度、面内旋转的四维带符号伺服坐标。Stage 1 将动作条件潜状态转移与该坐标对齐；Stage 2 冻结世界模型，训练反应式关节速度策略，结合动作模仿、后果监督和偏好误差收缩的短程想象 rollout。部署时仅用 RGB、反应式执行，无需在线轨迹优化。

Result: 真实 7-DoF eye-to-hand 系统上 30/30 次试验使角点 RMSE 降至初值 10% 以内，最终有效帧保持该标准 25/30（83.33%）；去除未来误差对齐后保持率降至 26.67%。学习到的进度信号与未参与训练/控制的 AprilTag 外部角点误差一致（Spearman rho=0.8778）。无需重训练，两个未见 3D 目标平移误差降低 86.48% 和 90.27%，旋转误差降低 70.01% 和 65.70%。

Conclusion: 结果将进度对齐的动作后果与重复闭环修正、跨目标迁移联系起来，验证了以误差收缩为目标的后果预测对闭环视觉伺服的有效性；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [327] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出紧凑型双模态空中-地面机器人 AeRove，利用桨叶护罩作为滚轮在地面滚动行进，并通过弹簧加载双稳态机构实现飞行/地面模式 200 ms 内切换，从而大幅降低功耗并提升管道检测能力与气体传感性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无人机进行近距离管道检测面临悬停功耗高、气流敏感性强、螺旋桨尾流干扰气体传感等问题，需要一种兼具地面高效率移动与空中越障能力，并能改善气体检测性能的平台。

Method: AeRove 使用固定式桨叶护罩作为滚轮沿管道滚动，采用弹簧加载双稳态机构在 200 ms 内完成地面与飞行模式重构，且维持任一状态无需持续驱动功率；其收敛式护罩几何提升推进效率。飞行仅用于越障，地面滚动用于连续行进。在 51 cm 直径钢管上进行自主导航试验（直线/弯曲段、越障、模拟泄漏标记检测），并单独开展 CO₂ 传感实验，对比 perched（停驻）与悬停状态，以及机身底部进气式与加长探针式两种飞行检测构型。

Result: 相较持续飞行，地面滚动使电流从 10 A 降至 0.7 A（降低 14 倍），估计行进距离由 144 m 提升至 2057 m。自主试验验证了直线与弯曲段导航、越障及对模拟检测标记的泄漏检测。停驻检测的浓度响应显著大于悬停状态；飞行时底部螺旋桨进气构型借助桨致气流，比加长探针获得更快、更强的气体检测响应。

Conclusion: AeRove 通过双模态设计与护罩滚轮方案，有效降低管道巡检功耗并延长行程，同时利用停驻检测与桨致气流提升气体检测灵敏度，代码与设计以 CC-BY 许可开源，为近距离管道巡检提供可行方案。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


### [328] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出"shake-to-learn"机械询问范式：用折纸启发式软体机械臂作为物理储备池计算机，通过对抓取物体施加固定抖动激励并读取振铃响应，结合轻量线性读出解码隐藏物体物理属性（质心方向与距离），并用于引导再抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多与机器人操作相关的物理属性（如封闭物体的质心位置、内部结构）无法通过视觉或常规传感获知，只有在被抬起、摇晃或动态扰动时才间接显现。作者希望将这种交互诱导的动态响应转化为一种新的机器人感知与学习模态，以推断视觉不可达的物体物理信息。

Method: 采用受折纸启发的软体机械臂，将其用作物理储备池计算机（physical reservoir computer）。抓取物体后，在臂基座施加固定抖动激励，通过相机跟踪或嵌入式传感器记录耦合机器人-物体系统的振铃响应。由于输入在各试验中恒定，隐藏属性（如质心位置）通过其对系统动力学的影响被编码。随后使用轻量线性读出解码这些动力学。作者还提出一种振铃响应的动态汇总表示以提高预测精度。

Result: 该软体臂储备池完成了三项难度递增的任务：推断物体隐藏质心的方向、推断质心与抓取点之间的距离，以及利用推断出的质心信息引导后续再抓取。提出的动态汇总表示进一步提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: 结果确立了"shake-to-learn"机械询问作为一种有前景的策略，使机器人系统能够将短暂物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，服务于下游操作任务。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [329] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeTac-VLA，一种基于预测的触觉-视觉-语言融合模型，通过预测未来触觉状态来引导动作生成，在四类真实接触密集型操作任务中平均成功率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型依赖视觉感知，在接触密集环境中鲁棒性受限，因为关键物理交互状态可能无法被视觉观测。现有触觉增强VLA方法虽利用触觉反馈提升物理感知，但大多是反应式的，未显式建模接触的未来演化过程。

Method: ForeTac-VLA将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，并通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；采用基于Transformer的预测模块预测多步未来触觉状态，使模型能联合推理已观测与预期接触；融合后的多模态表示与预测的未来触觉状态输入VLA主干以条件化动作生成。为稳定训练，早期预测不可靠时采用真值到预测的课程学习策略。

Result: 在四个真实世界接触密集型操作任务上，ForeTac-VLA平均成功率达95%，比微调后的VLA模型高36.25个百分点，比最先进的触觉增强VLA基线高出22个百分点以上；在低光照和视觉杂乱条件下也保持强性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测未来触觉状态并联合推理观测与预期接触，ForeTac-VLA有效弥补了视觉感知在接触密集场景中的不足，显著提升操作成功率与环境鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention. Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on https://foretac-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [330] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT系统，将视觉语言模型的语义推理与几何规划结合用于越野机器人地形评估，通过物理接地加权多模态可通行性得分，并采用两级导航架构，在6.4 km混合地形试验中将自主性从59.6%提升至97.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于几何的地形评估计算快但在非结构化环境中过于保守，限制了越野移动机器人的自主导航能力，需要引入语义层面的可通行性推理来弥补几何方法的不足。

Method: PIVOT通过VLM预测通行能耗、机器人振动与车轮滑移，量化这些预测与真实测量的相关性，并据此加权重构统一可通行性得分；同时设计两级导航架构，以几何规划为默认模式，仅在其无法找到路径时触发语义重规划。

Result: 在约6.4 km混合地形路线的五次闭环重复试验中，系统将整体自主性从59.6%提升至97.0%，人工干预从11次降至3次，平均干预间隔（MDBI）从69.2 m提升至412.9 m。

Conclusion: 物理接地的VLM地形评估能显著突破纯几何方法的自主导航局限，同时保留高效几何规划作为常规模式。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [331] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转式FMCW雷达的逐方位多普勒速度测量能否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能，并构建自动标注流程在Boreas数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转FMCW雷达因全天候鲁棒性和360°视场在自动驾驶感知中广受欢迎，且新近可生成逐方位多普勒速度；但多普勒数据缺乏带真值动态目标标签的数据集，其能否改善检测与跟踪尚不明确。

Method: 检测方面估计自车运动以消除雷达图像的多普勒距离畸变后再输入网络；跟踪方面提出逐车辆速度估计方法，用作跟踪器运动模型先验。同时构建自动标注流程，用集成微调的现成激光雷达检测器标注Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km，将检测迁移到雷达，取250 km以上车辆密集序列作训练真值。

Result: 训练并评估两个SOTA检测器，多普勒去畸变使mAP最高提升2.37点；多普勒速度先验相较零速初始化基线使MOTA提升13.68点，并达到使用真值速度先验所得MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度测量可有效提升旋转雷达的车辆检测与跟踪精度，且所提速度先验接近真值速度先验的上限，自动标注流程为缺乏真值标签的多普勒雷达数据提供了可行的监督方案。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [332] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT，一个ROS集成的机载感知与控制框架，用于防御型无人机对入侵无人机的实时视觉跟踪与闭环拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未授权无人机激增，亟需抗干扰的周界防御反无人机系统，且需摆脱对外部传感设施与地面站算力的依赖。

Method: 机载视觉检测、目标关联、航迹滤波与控制指令生成一体化；感知流程采用TensorRT加速的无人机检测、ByteTrack关联及轻量级航迹保持状态机，抑制目标跳变并维持短时连续性；以平面"守门"策略执行闭环拦截控制。

Result: 实飞实验中，目标有效检测帧占比达92.2%，并保持实时机载检测速率，验证了视觉跟踪与闭环控制用于无人机周界防御的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOUT表明无需外部基础设施即可在防御无人机上实现实时感知-控制闭环的周界防御，为机载反无人机跟踪提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. https://figshare.com/s/497befc033091fc0b84f

</details>


### [333] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将临时障碍下的图导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程，提出基于POMCP的信念空间规划器SPARROW，在线学习障碍清除生存模型并权衡学习价值，仿真与实机均优于OSCAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划路线上可能出现临时障碍，此时需在等待清除、重新规划路线或先获取更多障碍信息之间做出序贯决策；仅依赖静态或先验的障碍清除模型难以应对潜在障碍类别与清除时间的不确定性，且替代路径上也可能出现新的阻塞。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POSMDP）；提出SPARROW——基于部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）的信念空间规划器，在遍历、观测与有限时长等待动作上搜索，并维护关于潜在障碍类别与清除时间的粒子信念。采用类别条件生存模型，从清除观测及机器人提前改道导致的右删失遭遇中在线学习；用生成模型随动作展开模拟障碍到达与清除，以考虑替代路线上的阻塞。此外引入学习价值准则，在采集带标签生存数据的即时成本与其带来的未来导航后悔缩减之间权衡。

Result: 在两个仿真图与多种障碍类别设置下，SPARROW相比近期基于生存分析的OSCAR方法将平均到达目标时间降低12–26%；在实体移动机器人上，SPARROW相比OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低20.5%，并能随环境条件变化选择性地观测、等待与改道。

Conclusion: 将临时障碍导航视为信念空间下的序贯决策问题，并结合POMCP规划、在线生存学习与主动学习价值评估，可显著提升导航效率与自适应性，仿真与实机验证均显示一致优势。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [334] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作证明：仅用总价低于1万美元的消费级传感器（立体相机、MEMS IMU、深度传感器）与开源AUV平台，在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中即可实现可用的高质量VI-SLAM导航，为低成本海事机器人提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV依赖DVL、USBL等昂贵传感器套件，成本高昂，阻碍了先进海洋机器人从专业实验室走向更广泛的研究与爱好者应用。因此需要验证低成本平台能否实现稳健、高质量的导航。

Method: 采用价格相近的开源AUV，在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中评估立体相机、MEMS IMU与深度传感器的性能；分析现成SLAM软件包的有效性，并针对水下无缆作业的视觉与物理挑战提出传感器融合优化方案。

Result: 结果表明，在仅使用廉价消费级传感器、总价低于1万美元的平台上，能够获得可用的SLAM解，性能可满足苛刻的实时海事任务需求，而无需工业级硬件的资金门槛。

Conclusion: 低成本、开源的水下VI-SLAM方案可使高质量SLAM导航惠及大众，并为低成本平台在严苛实时海事任务中的性能预期提供了基准。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [335] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-Feedback，一种双时间尺度架构，将低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合，通过保留扩散最后一步去噪作为轻量反馈接口，实现执行前的实时动作校正，在动态仿真与真实机器人任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式动作生成器虽能建模时间连贯的动作块，但这些动作块通常在推理后被开环执行，导致当物体移动、接触变化或场景演化时响应能力受限。

Method: VLA-Feedback采用双时间尺度架构：低频扩散规划器负责生成动作块，同时保留其最后一步去噪过程作为轻量级反馈接口，利用最新观测在执行每个动作前进行校正，无需重新运行完整的视觉-语言-扩散模型。

Result: 在静态LIBERO任务上与GR00T持平；动态仿真任务平均成功率从27.5%提升至85.0%；真实机器人任务平均成功率从51%提升至73%。

Conclusion: 该设计在保留扩散规划器表达能力的同时实现了实时动作校正，有效改善了动态场景下的执行鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: https://vla-feedback.github.io.

</details>


### [336] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将刚体动力学一阶导数的已有算法推广到基于约束嵌入建模的闭链运动系统，去除了"关节速度效应局部构型不变"假设，并评估了新引入项的计算代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效动力学导数算法普遍假设关节速度效应具有局部构型不变性，这一假设无法覆盖约束嵌入闭链模型中的一般关节类型，导致此类系统的动力学导数计算不准确或不可用。

Method: 在原有刚体动力学一阶导数算法基础上，移除局部构型不变的关节速度效应假设，推导适配更一般关节类型（含约束嵌入闭链模型所产生关节）的导数算法；并将传统销关节机器人模型与更完整的、能刻画局部闭链的驱动模型进行对比。

Result: 广义化引入的附加项在仅建模驱动运动学（actuation kinematics）时计算影响很小；但当驱动链中包含额外刚体（如电机转子）或考虑非局部回路时，计算代价明显升高。

Conclusion: 该工作使约束嵌入驱动子机构的动力学计算更精确，可应用于模型预测控制与可微仿真。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [337] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究部分探索地图下的单快照射频发射源定位，提出并比较两种输出位置后验的方法：扩展数字孪生射线追踪法与免推理传播模拟的 MAPLE-RF。混合地图覆盖率训练对两者至关重要；数字孪生精度更高，MAPLE-RF 精度接近且查询成本低两个数量级以上，均优于高斯与高斯混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人应用中常仅有部分地图（如 SLAM 探索阶段），而传统 RF 发射源定位依赖完整环境模型来预测障碍物的遮挡与反射，因此需研究部分探索地图下的定位问题。

Method: 比较两种输出发射源位置后验的方法：其一将数字孪生方法（对每个候选位置做射线追踪）扩展到部分地图，把未探索空间视为自由空间并在混合地图覆盖上训练；其二为 MAPLE-RF，将估计的到达路径角与信噪比编码为与地图已知度、占据度及视距可见性对齐的网格通道，用 U-Net 一次前向为所有候选位置打分，推理时不模拟传播。

Result: 室内环境射线追踪仿真表明：混合地图覆盖训练对两种方法均必不可少；数字孪生在多数单快照指标上更准确，MAPLE-RF 精度接近，且其查询成本不依赖传播模型，比使用通用射线追踪的全网格查询低两个数量级以上；两者均优于高斯与高斯混合基线；在由自身估计引导的探索路径上，融合的 MAPLE-RF 后验在真实源附近赋予更高概率。代码与数据将公开。

Conclusion: 在部分探索地图的单快照发射源定位中，混合地图覆盖训练是关键；数字孪生方法精度最优，而 MAPLE-RF 以极低且与传播模型无关的查询成本实现接近精度，并在探索任务中提供更优的后验定位，是兼顾精度与效率的方案。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [338] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA是一个自动化的real-to-sim-to-real框架，仅需单张RGB图像即可生成可部署的灵巧操作策略，实现零样本真机部署与协同训练性能大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人数据采集成本高、耗时长；高保真仿真虽可扩展数据合成与策略学习，但手工构建可部署数字孪生劳动密集，且视觉、几何与动力学残差阻碍可靠的sim-to-real迁移。

Method: 提出四阶段统一框架：(1) 单图场景分解为静态高斯背景与可交互刚体/铰接资产，并由VLM推断物理参数；(2) 度量级场景全局对齐、物体规范化与形态均衡的机器人标定；(3) 可扩展仿真任务基元构建、VR遥操作与以物体为中心轨迹合成；(4) 共享多模态策略接口，同时支持模仿学习与强化学习。

Result: 在13个任务-本体对、2个灵巧机器人平台、6种策略架构上评估：视觉保真度与3D几何重建优于生成式基线，跨域轨迹回放验证强物理交互一致性；纯仿真训练策略可实现可行零样本真机部署，仿真-真实协同训练将平均物理策略成功率从29.2%提升至61.9%。

Conclusion: DEXTERA实现了从单张图像到可部署策略的自动化real-to-sim-to-real流程，兼顾重建质量与物理一致性，并有效缓解sim-to-real差距，支持零样本部署与协同训练提升。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [339] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的二次规划（QP）控制器，将轨迹跟踪、推力分配与每推进器力/速率约束一并求解，并用外部故障检测器给出的二值标志实现故障重构，已在苏黎世湖与威尼斯展会实船编队演出中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编排水上表演要求小型自主水面艇在单推进器推力与速率受限（含推进器故障后）条件下精确跟踪路径；传统级联架构（跟踪与分配分离）在故障后易偏离参考路径，需要一种能统一处理约束与故障重构的控制方法。

Method: 在每推进器指令层面构建单一二次规划，同时求解轨迹跟踪、推力分配及单推进器推力与速率限制；故障重构通过外部检测器提供的每推进器一个二值标志进入优化。与自研级联基线作了对比实验。

Result: 现场实验：10分钟定点保持RMS位置误差1.6 cm，以0.6 m/s跟踪10 m方形轨迹误差4.3 cm；0.5 m/s下失去前推进器误差增至11.6 cm，失去右舷推进器增至11.5 cm；同时失去两个推进器时仍能以0.4 m/s跟踪方形，RMS误差1.25 m。相比级联基线，统一式在名义方形跟踪上精度相当（数厘米），定点保持更紧且推进器力约减半；故障后两者分化，统一控制器保持在参考路径24 cm以内，级联则偏离路径。

Conclusion: 统一QP式轨迹跟踪+推力分配+力/速率约束可满足演出级精度，并借助二值故障标志实现优雅降级；故障情形下显著优于级联架构，已在真实湖泊与展会演出中长期部署。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [340] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文聚焦植物遮阴模拟，构建了涵盖大豆、番茄、糖甜菜和草莓四种作物的植物生长与阴影数据集，并提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，支持对未见植物的逼真阴影生成及下游机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长与农业生产是国家可持续发展与民生的重要基础，前沿AI推动科学农业发展。植物遮阴是影响植物生长的关键因素，但植物阴影模拟具有内在复杂性，相关研究不足，亟需推动该方向以带来更广泛社会效益。

Method: 一，构建覆盖四种植物（大豆、番茄、糖甜菜、草莓）的综合植物生长与阴影数据集，包含俯视视角、沿圆形轨迹的辅助光源、跨多个生长阶段的动态阴影以及多样观测复杂度。二，提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，引入时间条件控制以支持不同时间阶段的灵活阴影模拟。

Result: 对模型性能进行了定量与定性评估；方法能够为未见植物生成逼真阴影，并支持感知、光照控制、视角规划等下游机器人任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为植物感知的阴影建模提供了基础性研究，对更广泛的农业与机器人应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [341] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将A3C算法与PID控制器结合，用神经网络动态优化PID参数，并配合系统辨识模块预测状态，实现四旋翼姿态与轨迹跟踪，性能优于A2C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼具有非线性特性和对扰动敏感的问题，基础PID控制器难以应对此类复杂性，需要兼具传统控制稳定性与学习适应性的控制方案。

Method: 构建A3C与PID集成的控制系统：A3C通过并行智能体，利用神经网络动态整定PID参数；引入系统辨识模块，为互补系统预测系统状态以获取最优控制策略。并与标准A2C模型进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，A3C与A2C均能实现精确跟踪；但基于A3C的控制器在损失函数上收敛更好，奖励与损失曲线证实其参数优化更优。

Conclusion: A3C与PID的融合可提升四旋翼控制性能，有效结合强化学习与传统控制以获得更强的适应性。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [342] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种二自由度摆锤机构驱动的推力矢量共轴无人机，建立完整拉格朗日动力学模型并设计LQR控制器与EKF状态估计，仿真验证其能效与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轴旋翼无人机具有能效高、体积小的优势，但存在横滚与俯仰的固有欠驱动问题；传统斜盘方案虽能解决该问题，却带来较高的机械复杂度，因此需要一种机械结构更简单的替代方案。

Method: 设计二自由度摆锤机构实现主动推力矢量；建立包含机体、舵机臂和电机组件的完整拉格朗日动力学模型（保留所有部件惯性贡献）；在悬停平衡点线性化后设计LQR控制器；采用EKF融合GPS、气压计与IMU进行状态估计；仿真中考虑执行器动态与传感器噪声。

Result: 高保真仿真验证了所提架构的有效性；EKF融合GPS、气压计与IMU实现了高精度状态估计。

Conclusion: 该方法对于节能、快速共轴无人机具有潜力与可靠性，且机械复杂度低于斜盘方案。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium. High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [343] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在分层人形-四足点球系统中研究策略信息与身体执行能力对学习决策的耦合约束，提出动力学诱发承诺映射（DIC-Map），刻画继续执行能力、识别终端替代方案的首次持续丧失，并检验剩余交互是否可化为零和博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人博弈中的学习不仅受策略信息限制，还受身体能否继续执行的约束；作者希望显式建模并分析这种"策略—身体"耦合对承诺时机和均衡策略的影响。

Method: 构建人形（射门，基于自采集动捕数据初始化）与四足（扑救，强化学习）的点球分层系统，游戏层自博弈策略指挥固定全身足球控制器（S-WBC）。提出DIC-Map进行身体落地的承诺分析：估计继续能力、定位终端替代方案首次持续丧失点、检验约化零和博弈可行性。对对称终端替代给出最优策略集中度的闭式界，并分析估计器介入下的一阶学习通道。

Result: 实验定位承诺发生于接触前约0.29秒；仅改变球速即可改变延迟覆盖范围。在四种响应策略下，替换估计器将扑救率从0.240提升至0.472，而通过等待获得同等读判准确率提升仅使扑救率升至0.246。因现有覆盖项为观测代理，采用后验分析进行均衡比较。

Conclusion: 身体的执行能力构成博弈学习的实质性约束，承诺可由动力学分析定位；在估计器介导的响应中，响应价值相等会消除终端分配的直接梯度，留下估计器介导的一阶学习通道，估计器质量而非单纯延长等待时间才是提升扑救性能的关键。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: https://chris-ruizegeng.github.io/penaltykick/

</details>


### [344] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MTC框架，利用VR程序化场景采集人体动作并结合场景感知重定向算法，训练Unitree G1人形机器人穿越密集杂乱环境的全身运动策略，在多障碍穿越测试中达到70.2%的无碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人体运动学习的方法虽已实现舞蹈、武术等高动态技能，但多在无障碍空间完成；密集杂乱环境具有三维几何约束，需要全身运动与场景几何紧密耦合的场景感知运动能力，该方向仍研究不足。

Method: MTC采用示范学习框架：用程序化生成的虚拟现实环境替代昂贵的物理场景搭建，以沉浸式方式采集人体运动数据；提出场景感知运动重定向算法，将人体示范转换为符合机器人形态的轨迹，并严格施加机器人与场景的间隙约束以保证无碰撞；用这些参考轨迹训练场景感知运动策略，并部署到Unitree G1人形机器人上。

Result: 在作者提出的多障碍穿越基准MTC-Challenge上，策略在多种场景中实现70.2%的无碰撞率，并能通过爬行穿越低净空通道、挤过狭窄缝隙等多样化全身技能完成复杂环境穿越。

Conclusion: MTC证明了以VR程序化环境与场景感知重定向为核心的示范学习范式，可有效赋予人形机器人在几何约束杂乱空间中的无碰撞全身运动能力，为场景感知人形运动研究提供了可行路径与评测基准。

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [345] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V. R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFactory：以单次视频扫描为唯一人工输入、生成环中不含物理引擎的高保真演示数据引擎，用3DGS重建可编辑场景并纯运动学规划抓取与轨迹，仅在抓取接触处引入学习到的接触模型，合成演示训练的标准扩散策略在仿真与真实UR10e上分别达95.1%和84.2%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略训练依赖大量与目标环境高度匹配的演示，而重新采集成本高昂。现有演示合成方法虽降低成本，但仍受限于人工投入大、视觉保真度低或对物理仿真器依赖过重。

Method: 以单次视频扫描为唯一人工输入，将场景重建为可编辑的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）副本；从场景所能提供的物体组合任务中采样；对每个任务在几何重建上做纯运动学抓取与轨迹规划，渲染与目标环境视觉匹配的逼真演示；仅在接触力决定结果的抓取成形阶段，通过一个在交互数据集上一次性预训练的接触模型引入物理动力学，生成环中不使用物理引擎。

Result: 实现端到端的“扫描到部署”流程并在两种设置中评估：作为真实世界替代以支持可复现性的仿真场景，以及配备物理UR10e机器人的真实工作空间。仅用合成演示训练的标准扩散策略分别取得95.1%与84.2%的成功率。

Conclusion: GaussianFactory表明，无需物理引擎、仅凭单次视频扫描即可大规模合成高保真演示，足以支撑下游视觉运动策略的部署，显著降低演示采集成本并提升数据与目标环境的匹配度。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [346] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SYS：一种面向低光环境的单目视觉定位流水线。它基于前馈点图前端并加入显式匹配门控，能在极端黑暗下稳定输出三条可跟踪轨迹，并在Jetson AGX Orin上实现实时、低功耗的嵌入式执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑暗环境下机器人需在功率受限的机载计算机上、用单个RGB相机实时定位，此时逐像素信号接近传感器自身噪声。低光重建、实时单目SLAM、嵌入式部署三条技术线各自成熟，但其交叉领域尚属空白：离线低光重建精度高但过慢，实时单目系统（DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等）在低信噪比下退化甚至失效。作者量化了这些方法的失败模式，以凸显该交叉问题的重要性与紧迫性。

Method: SYS包含三方面贡献：（1）算法上构建低光前馈点图（pointmap）前端并引入显式匹配门控（match gate），据此判定是否可跟踪，从而避免输出无信息轨迹；（2）嵌入式执行路径上，在Jetson AGX Orin上将建图、关键帧与后端运行于384像素、跟踪运行于256像素，并加入两处逐帧位姿求解修正；（3）评测上构建可校准、位精确可复现的噪声阶梯，对真实暗光曝光重标注，并使用Boston Dynamics Spot采集的新暗室视频阶梯。

Result: 在场景最暗的九个等级中，DROID-SLAM在全部九个上都输出全长但无运动信息的轨迹，VGGT-SLAM与CUT3R为八个，pi^3为七个，DPV-SLAM为四个。SYS则输出三条可跟踪轨迹且无无信息轨迹，在可跟踪处误差最低（为无信息上限的24-47%，最强基线为56-73%），覆盖率最窄。在86.5%帧全黑的真实机器人视频中，SYS各配置均在亮处开始后停止，而DROID-SLAM与DPV-SLAM对全部1178帧均输出位姿。嵌入式方面，该执行路径在场景一实现1.28倍吞吐、0.964倍误差，场景二为1.42倍、0.68倍，峰值GPU内存减少47%，每位姿能耗降低29%，构成可复现的帕累托改进。

Conclusion: 低光实时单目定位的关键并非追求在无信息条件下继续输出轨迹，而是显式识别可跟踪性并拒绝无信息输出。SYS通过匹配门控前馈点图前端与嵌入式执行路径优化，在精度、覆盖、吞吐、显存与能耗上同时改善，验证了该交叉方向在真实机器人平台上的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [347] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaPusher 提出元学习动力学模型与自适应运动动力学规划器耦合的框架，无需对象专属先验交互即可快速适应并规划未见物体的非抓取式操作，成功率达提升最多20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未见物体的动力学依赖摩擦、质量分布等无法仅从感知推断的潜在物理属性；跨物体先验经验虽可提供初始估计，但不确定性高且 sim-to-real 迁移时易退化。交互式适应可逐步修正估计，但模型更新会使已规划轨迹失效，因此需同时具备快速动力学适应与能纳入模型演化的规划策略。

Method: 提出 MetaPusher 框架：元学习动力学模型在执行任务中通过交互快速适应；自适应运动动力学规划器（adaptive kinodynamic planner）通过复用并精炼已有搜索树来更新长时域规划；二者耦合使操作与适应无需单独的数据采集阶段。

Result: 在仿真与 sim-to-real 场景的未见物体上进行评测，与微调、主动学习方法、基于 MPPI 的控制及强化学习策略对比，MetaPusher 取得更低的预测误差，并将任务成功率提升最多 20%。

Conclusion: 元学习与自适应规划的有效耦合可在无对象专属先验交互条件下实现未见物体的高效非抓取式操作，验证了动力学快速适应与规划复用结合的可行性。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [348] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，用SAIL将CBF安全约束内化到可微策略中，用TALO校正多智能体独立更新，实现多机器人-人协作中可解释决策与安全执行的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人-人协作要求决策可解释可审计，同时动作必须满足物理交互安全约束。可解释决策树策略与CBF过滤结合的架构在MARL中存在两处学习错配：安全投影会改变实际施加到环境的动作；耦合的提议图会使独立优化的actor更新与团队级更新不一致。

Method: SAGE包含两个组件：SAIL使用可微的有限惩罚提议映射，同时保留精确CBF二次规划用于执行，保持约束-范数敏感性以将反复激活的安全约束内化；TALO构建团队感知的更新参考，并施加Lyapunov半空间校正来约束独立actor更新。

Result: 在两个人形机器人和一名人类伙伴的物理实验中验证了部署可行性；九个仿真场景中成功率达71.0%，每千环境步碰撞0.5步。消融实验：直接CBF过滤使碰撞频率降低98.5%但成功率从67.3%降至59.3%；SAIL使提议违反降低48.8%、提议-执行校正降低85.2%；TALO使更新一致性差距降低50.8%。

Conclusion: SAGE通过SAIL与TALO分别解决安全投影导致的动作错配和团队更新错配，在保证安全的同时维持较高任务成功率，并在真实人机协作平台上验证了可行性。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates.
